</TD><TD valign=middle bgcolor=#FFFFFF style=width:382.75pt;padding-left:3.5pt;padding-right:3.5pt><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:49.65pt;color:#808080'>YPF S.A.</P>

<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:78pt;margin-right:42.5pt;color:#1F497D'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Rounding</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:7pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:78pt;margin-right:42.5pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:78pt;margin-right:42.5pt'>Certain figures included in this Annual Report have been rounded to facilitate their presentation. Percentage figures have not always been calculated based on such rounded figures but on amounts prior to rounding. For this reason, percentage amounts may vary from those obtained by performing the same calculations using the figures of these consolidated Financial Statements. Certain numerical figures shown as totals in some tables may not be the arithmetic aggregation of the figures that precede them, due to rounding.</P>

<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
</DIV>
<DIV style=margin-left:85.05pt;width:432.35pt><HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:12pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:-78pt;margin-right:-21.25pt;color:#0070C0'><FONT style=font-size:9pt><I> &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</I></FONT><B>Main financial Indicators</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:-78pt;margin-right:-21.25pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:-78pt;margin-right:-21.25pt'><IMG src=tgs_5.jpg width=549 height=233 alt='Picture 6' title='Picture 6'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:-85.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:-56.7pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:-56.7pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:-56.7pt'><IMG src=tgs_6.jpg width=688 height=382 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>1</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
</DIV>
<DIV style=margin-left:85.05pt;width:446.55pt><HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#0070C0'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><B>1.</B></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.35pt></KBD><FONT style=font-size:11pt><B>Letter to our Shareholders, Customers, Employees and Suppliers </B></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Throughout the 2023, we kept pursuing the creation of value for our shareholders and moved forward on our road towards the great dream we conceived four years ago: <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>To become leaders in the rendering of integrated services </B><B>and </B><B>play a major role in </B><B>the</B><B> </B><B>development of </B><B>energy in Argentina </B><B>and the region</B><B>.</B></FONT> We kept evolving towards an open, dynamic and cooperative culture based on these four cornerstones:</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#0070C0'><FONT style=color:#E36C0A><B>ACHIEVEMENT</B></FONT><B> </B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#0070C0'><B>INNOVATION </B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#0070C0'><FONT style=color:#00B0F0><B>FOCUS ON THE CLIENT</B></FONT></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#92D050'><B>ETHICAL AND TRANSPARENT PERFORMANCE</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Our results reflect our long-term business approach of cautious capital allocation and investment in the development of Vaca Muerta and the safe and efficient operation of our businesses. Year after year, <B>tgs</B> shows great strength and resilience in the face of never-ending macroeconomic challenges, including inflationary pressure, the regulatory framework and the high volatility of financial markets.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>During 2023 we recorded net revenues for Ps. 452,809 million, an operating income of Ps. 116,437 million and a net income of Ps. 23,518 million (Ps. 31.24 per share). These results are compared to the ones recorded in 2022: net revenues at Ps. 512,343 million, operating income of Ps. 163,329 million, and a net income of Ps. 100,640 million (Ps. 133.69 per share).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>In</B><B> 2023 </B><B>we doubled our capital expenditures</B></FONT>, mainly investing in the development of Vaca Muerta and the works we are conducting at our Tratay&#233;n Plant. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Within the natural gas transportation segment, tariff increases remained below inflation rates, a disparity that has been carried over since 2019, which has never been enough to offset the overall increase of operational costs. Due to this situation, in 2023 we recorded an operating loss of Ps. 12,233 million, which underlines how imperative it is that government entities finally conclude the Tariff Review Process (&#8220;RTI&#8221;, according to its acronym in Spanish). In this framework, in April 2023 we only obtained a 95% increase. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Furthermore, in September 2023, we submitted to ENARGAS our request for the extension of the natural gas transportation license for ten years, the expiration date of which is in December 2027. Our request shall be addressed by the corresponding government entities. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Notwithstanding the above, we strengthened our commitment and <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>continu</B><B>ed investing to obtain a safe and reliable natural gas transportation system</B></FONT>, turning <B>tgs</B> into the leading company in the natural gas transportation business. Transporting <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>60%</B><B> </B><B>of the gas consumed in </B><B>Argentina</B></FONT>, our main pipeline system satisfactorily met natural gas transportation demands. </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>2</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>Operating in a safe and reliable manner, our transportation system equipment reached operative efficiency and reliability ratios of 99.99% and 99.99%, respectively. </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>In the Liquids Production and commercialization segment, we kept strengthening our performance and consolidating ourselves as the second natural gas processor in Argentina.  Thanks to the higher quality of the gas arriving to the Cerri Complex and our upgrading works, we reached a production level of 1,134,297 tons (11,658 higher than 2022) thus consolidating our leadership in this business. The operative efficiency and reliability indexes we obtained in this segment were of 98.54% and 99.12%, respectively.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The operating profit of this segment was of 85,764 million (Ps. 35,376 million lower than recorded in 2022) mainly attributable to drops in international reference average prices of 36%, 31% and 16% for propane, butane and natural gasoline, respectively and the rise in the prices of the natural gas purchased as PTR, which resulted in lower operative margins.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>The opportunities derived from the development of Vaca Muerta have presented great challenges to us and nurture our dream of being the leading company in the rendering of integrated services, acknowledged by our values, products and services quality, innovative spirit and leadership in the energy market in Argentina and the region. We thus reassert our commitment to the planet and the community where we conduct our businesses.</FONT> </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Midstream has become the most challenging of all our business segments: where we will be able to recover our growth path and play our major role in the energy development of Argentina and the region. In 2023, we completed the expansion works of the <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Tratay&#233;n </B><B>Plant, by means of the installation of two natural gas conditioning module plants</B> </FONT>with Joule Thomson technology, each of them with a natural gas capacity of 3.5 MMm<SUP>3</SUP>/d and a new gasoline stabilization power. This project, entailing an estimated investment of US$ 32 million, was started up in May 2023 and doubled Tratay&#233;n plant&#8217;s treatment capacity.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>We also started works for the installation of two Propak Systems Ltd (&#8220;Propak&#8221;) conditioning plants, which will be in service by mid-2024. The estimated investment is of around US$ 320 million. These projects will expand Tratay&#233;n Plant&#8217;s conditioning capacity to 28 MMm<SUP>3</SUP>/day to render conditioning services to the Vaca Muerta System, ensuring safety in the exhaust gas discharge of producers&#8217; volumes, in addition to increasing capacity. These modules can also be adjusted to extract liquids, with a low additional investment.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>There is no doubt that a major 2023 highlight was to have been chosen by Energ&#237;a Argentina S.A. (&#8220;ENARSA&#8221;) to render operation and maintenance services for the first section of the Presidente N&#233;stor Kirchner Pipeline(&#8220;GPNK&#8221;) for the oncoming 5 years. This project is of the utmost relevance for our country and we are proud of having designed it.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>None of our achievements would have been possible without the dedication and talent of the 1,125 people that make our team, who in the face of complex and challenging times have been able to adapt and rise to meet every difficulty. </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>3</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>We thus, <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>keep transforming ourselves towards </B><B>an</B><B> open, </B><B>dynamic </B><B>and cooperative culture </B></FONT>working in line with the highest health and safety standards, focusing on the wellbeing of all the persons with whom we conduct our activities. We promote diversity and equity and create long-lasting bonds that encourage the professional growth of our employees. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>To such purpose, in 2023 we launched our <B>tgs</B> Campus, our own learning ecosystem, acknowledged by the IAPG, which allows all our workers to get qualified and trained, fostering cooperation and leadership.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>We keep building up our performance in Environment, Social and Governance (ESG) matters. In 2023, we issued our first sustainability report in line with ESG principles as of December 31, 2022, which has earned us the Eikon prize in the category &#8220;Financial Communication with Investors.&#8221; </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>In 2023 we continued working on the ESG plan we designed in 2022, seeking to integrate our non-financial information to our business strategy, build a sustainable brand to meet energy demand and find sustainable solutions to care for the environment and the communities. With the aim of stepping forward with an innovating approach, we are analyzing the gap between our ESG current performance and international standards requirements, which will allow us to develop guidelines for the sustainable management of our businesses.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>4</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_7.jpg width=549 height=242 alt='Picture 2' title='Picture 2'></FONT>In 2023 we made progress in the actions and initiatives foreseen in the 2022-2026 Strategic Environmental Plan, on each of the cornerstones that uphold it. Not only did we improve the measurement of our emissions with the implementation of monitoring technology but we are also including measurements of indirect Scope 3 emissions. Additionally, we are moving ahead with the preparation of a five-year investment plan devised to mitigate the impact of climate change.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>We launched a broad communication and training program on diversity and inclusion, which we carried out during the year 2023, laying the foundations for where we are heading in 2024.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>To conclude, true to our commitment to the community, our <B>tgs</B> Foundation is striving to transform the reality of the neediest, through implementation of several educative activities.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Outlook to the Future</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>What has set our company apart for many years is our clear vision of the role we intend to play in the development of energy in Argentina. Towards such goal and seeking to stay ahead, we continue assessing two large projects: </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-32.7pt'>&#183;</KBD>The <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>development of a natural gas liquefaction </B><B>Plant </B><B>in </B><B>Bah&#237;a Blanca</B> </FONT>that may lead to the export of natural gas produced in Vaca Muerta, and  &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;text-indent:-32.7pt;margin-left:54pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-32.7pt'>&#183;</KBD>The <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>adaptation of the </B><B>Tratay&#233;n</B><B> Plant</B><B>, </B><B>(</B><B>currently </B><B>operating in a</B><B> </B><B>conditioning</B><B> mode</B><B>)</B><B>, </B><B>into a natural gas processing plant</B></FONT>, allowing the transportation, fractionating and dispatch of liquids from some port in the Bah&#237;a Blanca area.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>This further consolidates our commitment to the energy development of our country, confident that given an appropriate economic and regulatory framework, we can turn Argentina into one of the main energy suppliers of the region and position <B>tgs</B> as a leading and innovating midstream services company. Our 2023 achievements, along with the future projects that lie ahead of us, are evidence of the operative and financial soundness our company has achieved over its track record.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>5</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The future of natural gas as an essential fuel in the country&#8217;s energy matrix gets stronger and stronger every year, which will make Argentina a sustainable country of great growth. <B>tgs</B> is consolidating as <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>a</B><B>n</B><B> integrated services </B><B>provider</B><B> in the hydrocarbon industry</B></FONT>.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>It is of the utmost importance to maintain a constructive dialogue with governmental entities to finalize the tariff review process and thus obtain a fair and reasonable gas transportation service tariff that may enable us to conduct a profitable and efficient natural gas transportation service in line with our country&#8217;s energy development, meeting the demands of our domestic and exports markets and the interest of stakeholders&#8217; groups. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>In 2024 we will keep strengthening our commitment to the sustainable management of our business, seeking to integrate ESG material issues in our strategy and decision making, focusing on the identification of risks and opportunities related to sustainability and climate change.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>A challenging 2024 lies ahead of us, but we face it with the firm belief that we will fulfill all our goals, working with passion and focusing on our clients and the community, committing to contribute to the development of energy in Argentina.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>We thank our shareholders for their trust in our performance, our clients and suppliers for being part of our businesses and our workforce for their tireless cooperation, commitment and dedication. It is to their joint efforts we owe our positioning at the highest performance standards throughout our history of 31 years, which &#8212;empowered by our cultural change process&#8212; will earn us a leading role at the forefront of our industry.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:6pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-2.25pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:35.4pt;margin-left:283.2pt;margin-right:-2.25pt'> &nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp; Luis Fallo</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:35.4pt;margin-left:283.2pt;margin-right:-2.25pt'> &nbsp;&nbsp; &nbsp;&nbsp;Chairman</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>6</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>2. Macroeconomic context</B></P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-left:125.5pt;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:3pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>2.1 Argentine Context</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>2023 was a year marked by high macroeconomic volatility and political uncertainty, mainly as a consequence of an unprecedented drought in the first months of the year and political turmoil after a loaded electoral calendar that concluded in November with a ballotage in which Javier Milei of the political party &#8220;Libertad Avanza&#8221; was elected as president for the period 2023&#8211;2027. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Among the main 2023 macroeconomic variables, we can mention:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:8pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Economic activity: GDP fell by 0.8% in the third quarter of 2023. On the other hand, in November 2023, the Monthly Estimate of Economic Activity (&#8220;EMAE&#8221;, according to its initials in Spanish) recorded a 0.9% year-on-year drop. Among the sectors, the steepest decrease was observed in the manufacturing industry (4.8%). On the other side of the spectrum, the sectors with greatest positive contributions were mining and quarrying (+6.7%) and agriculture and livestock (+3.6%).&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>According to Argentine National Bank issuances, the exchange rate continued its rising trend, closing $ 808.45 per each United States dollar as of December 31, 2023 from $177.16 at the closing of 2022 (representing an increase of around 356%, above the inflation measured by the CPI). Although as of December 31, 2023, the gap between this rate and the parallel United States dollar exchange rate dramatically dropped to 17%, during the rest of the year 2023 the gap had always remained above 100%.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>The year 2023 recorded a CPI of 211.4%, whereas in 2022 it had been of 94.8%. The acceleration dynamics was maintained throughout the whole year in spite of delays in public utilities tariff updates and exchange rates and price policies outlined by the Argentine government to support consumption and mitigate the loss of salaries&#8217; purchasing power.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>For the year 2023, the Internal Index&#160;Wholesale Prices (IPIM, according to its acronym in Spanish) variation was of 276.4%, compared to 94.8% recorded in the year 2022. &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>The Salary Index recorded a year-on-year increase of 144.3% as of November 2023. &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>BCRA reserves remained under pressure despite the growing restrictions in the access to the exchange market. As of December 31, 2023, net reserves reached US$ 23,073 million, down from the US$ 44,498 million recorded in December 2022. &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>The country risk premium paid by the Argentine debt, measured by the Emerging Markets Bonds Index (EMBI+) prepared by JP Morgan, was around 1,906 points as of December 31, 2023.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Trade balance recorded a US$ 6,926 million deficit. Exports amounted to US$ 66,788 million while imports totaled US$ 73,714 million. Exports went down by 24.5% compared to the year 2022 mainly due to the drop in the agricultural sector. On the other hand, imports in 2023 decreased by 9.6% compared to the previous year, mainly as a consequence of the lower imports of fuel and lube oil. &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>The fiscal arena was struck by a primary deficit in 2023 with a figure close to 2.9% of the GDP, rather far from the goal agreed with the IMF, which had foreseen a red ratio of 1.9%. &nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>7</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>Tax revenues closed at the end of the year with an increase of 115.1%. VAT revenues went up by 153.9% (below inflation rates). </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The macroeconomic variables described above drove Alberto Fern&#225;ndez&#8217; administration&#8212; in office until December 10, 2023&#8212; to adopt several measures, including: (i) the decree of new exchange restrictions, (ii) the adoption of the COUNTRY Tax and other taxes aimed at reducing fiscal deficit and (iii) the freezing of certain domestic prices, among them the restriction to public utilities services tariffs increases. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>In spite of the above, in 2023 the IMF Board, under the framework of the program in force with Argentina, approved certain non-compliances exemptions related to the implementation of the measures described above. It also approved amendments to reserve accumulation targets, the primary fiscal deficit goal and its financing along with the commitment to implement a new set of policies to correct the economic politics, safeguard stability and achieve the program goals.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Since its coming to office on December 10, 2023, the new administration has taken a many stringent measures to reverse the difficult country situation and eliminate fiscal deficit. Among them, it is included a fiscal reform program, State reforms and economic deregulations. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>In 2024, the new administration will have to execute its fiscal and monetary adjustment plans unsupported by legislative majorities. With the arrival of Javier Milei to the presidency, a stage of significant changes in economic terms is expected but many questions arise. His reform targets are very ambitious and radical and will require legislative support to be implemented. Also, prices adjustments among them those related to public utilities will derive in further inflation peaks particularly in the first months and further decelerate economic activity.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>As of the date of the issuance of the present Annual Report, it is not possible to foresee either the impact or the duration of this macroeconomic uncertainty, nor the effect that the measures&#160;that have been and will be adopted in the future might have, but they could negatively affect our results, financial condition and cash flows. However, given <B>tgs</B> financial condition, we estimate we will be able to continue honoring our financial commitments in the near future.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:3pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>2.2 International Scenario</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Many challenging factors have unsettled the world economic context in 2023, including the war in Ukraine that caused continuous problems in global supply chains and a persistent inflation, though it has shown a downward trend. These challenges have decelerated world growth: according to the World Economy Outlook Report issued by the IMF, World GDP es expected to have gone up by 3.0% in 2023, having grown by 3.5% in 2022 and being estimated to increase by 2.9% in 2024. &nbsp;These are the lowest growth values recorded over the last decade. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Although in Argentina the impact of the war between Russia and Ukraine has been moderated, the geo-economic uncertainty derived from such conflict had adverse consequences mainly in Europe, given the volatility of commodities prices, oil and byproducts. Additionally, as a consequence of such conflict, several economic sanctions have been imposed on Russia, which has derived in </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>8</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>difficulties in business and financial relations with suppliers from such country, impacting negatively on the supply chain. The war has also generated great economic uncertainty, which has discouraged companies from investing and has decreased consumer trust and worsened inflationary tension. </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>On the other hand, the war conflict in Israel which as of the issuance of this Annual Report has not had a significant economic impact, has increased world geopolitical tension. In combination with the conflict between Russia and Ukraine mentioned above, this might rise uncertainty levels and further decelerate economic world growth expectations, negatively impacting on emerging economies. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Persistent inflation has soared worldwide driven by the increase of prices of energy, food and other goods and services. To fight it, developed countries have toughened their monetary policy with increases in interest rates&#8212; negatively affecting the access of emerging countries to capital markets&#8212; and have redirected their cashflows towards developed countries.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Energy commodities prices have presented strong volatility in 2023, influenced by a combination of factors, including the ones mentioned above and adverse weather conditions. Oil, natural gas and its byproducts prices have gone down from the maximum recorded in 2022. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>International average prices of propane, butane and natural gasoline in 2023 have fallen by 36%, 31% and 16%, respectively as compared to the year 2022.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Despite the downward trend, energy commodities prices remain still high compared to levels recorded before the pandemic. This is due to the fact that the supply is still limited as investments in new energy infrastructure have been scarce over the last years. The outlook for the energy commodities prices in 2024 remains uncertain: their performance will be subject to the evolution of the war in Ukraine, world economic recovery and weather conditions.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:1pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#0070C0'><B>3. Natural Gas Industry in Argentina</B></P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-left:21.3pt;color:#00B0F0'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:10pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><B>a.</B></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:14.1pt></KBD><B>Demand for natural gas</B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Natural gas keeps holding a predominant share in the national energy matrix<FONT style=font-size:8pt><A name=_ftnb2 /><A href=#_ftnf2><SUP>2</SUP></A></FONT>, reaching 53% out of total fuels consumed in accordance with data provided by the National 2022 Energy Balance.</P>
<HR style=text-align:left;width:25%><P align=justify style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style=font-size:8pt><A name=_ftnf2 /><A href=#_ftnb2><SUP>2</SUP></A></FONT> The energy matrix corresponds to the domestic energy supply (production plus imports less exports + losses<FONT style='font:8pt Verdana'>).</FONT></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>9</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><IMG src=tgs_8.jpg width=573 height=311 alt='Picture 2' title='Picture 2'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:36pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>Source: Energy Secretariat &#8211;&nbsp;Energy Balance.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#4F81BD'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Natural gas consumption throughout the year 2023 reached 136 MMm3/d, recording a 1.8% drop in natural gas consumption versus the previous year (2.5 MMm3/d), mainly attributable to lower priority demand due to higher temperatures during winter months. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:35.4pt;margin-left:35.4pt'><FONT style=color:#4F81BD><B>Natural gas consumption per sector (202</B><B>3</B><B> vs. 202</B><B>2</B><B>)</B></FONT></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:21.3pt;color:#4F81BD'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#4F81BD'><B><IMG src=tgs_9.jpg width=558 height=262 alt='Picture 2' title='Picture 2'></B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><FONT style=font-size:8pt>Source: tgs own elaboration based on internal data</FONT> </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>During the first half of the year 2023, recorded average temperatures (21.1&#176;C)<FONT style=font-size:8pt> <A name=_ftnb3 /><A href=#_ftnf3><SUP>3</SUP></A></FONT> were warmer than those recorded in the same period of 2022 (18.5&#176;C), which accounts for the drop in priority demand &nbsp;</P>
<HR style=text-align:left;width:25%><P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style=font-size:6pt><A name=_ftnf3 /><A href=#_ftnb3><SUP>3</SUP></A></FONT><FONT style=font-size:8pt> Buenos Aires Ring&#8217;s average temperature. </FONT></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>10</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>(residential + SMEs) compared to the previous year, recording an average aggregate decrease of 1.5 MMm3/d.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>In spite of a 3.9% increase in the demand for electricity, total electricity generation went up by 2.8% (+3.2 TWh), being the difference covered though imports mainly proceeding from Brazil. The increase in domestic generation is attributable mainly to a recovery in hydroelectric contributions (+7.8 TWh). The higher hydroelectric contribution reduced needs for thermal generation (-4.8 TWh), due to the economic need to minimize fuel imports (liquids and LNG). Nuclear generation also dropped (-0.03 TWh) because of lower availability at Atucha 2, in service as from the month of September, offset by higher contribution from the Embalse power plant. Renewable energies keep growing, although to a lesser extent than in former years (+1.7% vs 2022) contributing to the system a share of 13.8% out of total generation (117.5 TWh).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The graph below presents monthly consumptions throughout the year 2023 for each of the demand sectors, in MMm3/d, compared to total 2022 demand (represented by a red line). </P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><IMG src=tgs_10.jpg width=631 height=235 alt='Picture 3' title='Picture 3'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:36pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>Source: tgs&#8217; own elaboration with internal data.<FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'> </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Demand for natural gas in 2023 remained similar to the levels recorded the previous year. The variations in consumption breakdown are mainly attributable to higher temperatures recorded in winter months (May and June) in which lower priority consumption was offset by higher consumption from electricity power plants, substituting liquid fuel consumption. The months with lowest consumptions compared to the previous year were July and October. In both cases, lower consumption is mainly attributable to lower consumption in thermal power plants due to higher contributions from hydroelectricity and the nuclear sector of the previous year. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The graph below shows 2023 monthly variations compared to the year 2022, in MMm3/d for each of the demand sectors.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>11</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana;color:#4F81BD'><B>Natural gas consumption variations per sector (2023 vs. 2022)</B></FONT> </P>
<P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><IMG src=tgs_11.jpg width=509 height=307 alt='Picture 3' title='Picture 3'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:36pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>Source: tgs&#8217; own elaboration with internal data.<FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'> </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:36pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Going over the total breakdown of natural gas consumption per kind of user in Argentina, it is clear that in 2023 the most demanding sector still is electricity generation, followed secondly by priority demand and the industrial sector ranked third.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#0070C0'><B>NATURAL GAS CONSUMPTION PER KIND OF USER (YEAR 2023)</B></P>
<P align=right style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><IMG src=tgs_12.jpg width=601 height=277 alt='Picture 3' title='Picture 3'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:28.35pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#808080'><FONT style=font-size:8pt>Source: tgs own elaboration with internal data.</FONT></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>12</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Natural gas supply</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Thanks to the Gas.Ar Plan incentives, production levels went up, and this trend continued throughout the year 2023. Higher natural gas availability in the domestic market sustains exports mainly to Chile through the Gas Andes pipeline, even in winter months, given the fact that such delivery would not restrict supply to the domestic market given the current transportation infrastructure. &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#4F81BD'><FONT style=color:#000000>In 2023, total natural gas production went up by 0.6% compared to the previous year, from 132.6 MMm3/d to 131.8 MMm3/d. </FONT></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#4F81BD'><B>Natural gas production per basin (MMm3/d)</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><IMG src=tgs_13.jpg width=558 height=221 alt='Imagen 6' title='Imagen 6'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:36pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>Source: SESCO, Energy Secretariat.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>In a breakdown analysis of natural gas operators, in 2023 YPF continued being the main producer of Argentina, with 26% of the total (unchanged from 2022), followed by Total Austral S.A. with 22%. </P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#4F81BD'><B>Main Argentine natural gas operators&#8217; share over total production (2023) &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><FONT style=color:#4F81BD><B><IMG src=tgs_14.jpg width=416 height=242 alt='Picture 2' title='Picture 2'></B><B> &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:36pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#808080'>Fuente: SESCO, SE</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Given the marked seasonality of the demand for natural gas in Argentina, with peak consumption during winter months, national natural gas production in Argentina&#8212; supplemented with natural </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>13</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>gas imported from Bolivia&#8212; meets local consumption needs in the warmer months (from October to April). However, during the coldest months (from May to September),&#160;such production falls short and is supplemented with the import of additional natural gas volumes proceeding from Bolivia and LNG.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>The Seventh Addendum to the Gas import Agreement between Energ&#237;a Argentina (ENARSA) and YPFB (Bolivian fiscal oil fields) was entered on December 30, 2022 and it includes a reduction in the natural gas volume to be received from Bolivia due to said country&#8217;s gas fields&#8217; production declination. However, in October 2023 said agreement was amended, stipulating that as from July 31, 2024 Bolivia will stop supplying a natural gas firm transportation volume to Argentina. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Supply from Bolivia &#8211;under the agreement entered by both governments &#8211;&nbsp;recorded an average of 5.5 MMm3/d, 4.9 MMm3/d lower than the volume recorded in 2022. On the other hand, LNG imports by sea, re-gasified and injected into the natural gas transportation system at the Escobar and Bah&#237;a Blanca port located in the Province of Buenos Aires, recorded an average contribution of 6.8 MMm3/d in 2023, 0.6 MMm3/d above LNG contribution recorded in 2022.</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#4F81BD'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#4F81BD'><B>Natural Gas Imports (MMm3/d)</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><IMG src=tgs_15.jpg width=595 height=269 alt='Picture 4' title='Picture 4'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Source: tgs own elaboration based on internal data</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#4F81BD'><FONT style=color:#000000>Total natural gas injection in August 2023, highest injection level of the year, reached 133 MMm3/d, 4 MMm3/d above the volume recorded in June 2022 (highest level recorded in said year).</FONT></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#4F81BD'><B> </B>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>14</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:35.4pt;margin-left:141.6pt;color:#4F81BD'><B>Supply (MMm3/d)</B></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#4F81BD'><B><IMG src=tgs_16.jpg width=558 height=294 alt='Picture 2' title='Picture 2'></B>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style=font-size:8pt>Source: Own elaboration based on internal data.</FONT><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'> </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The Neuqu&#233;n Basin produces the highest volume of natural gas for injection, whereas the remaining basins continued their natural decline (including Bolivia), which makes it imperative to build the third main pipeline to transport the incremental gas proceeding from the Neuqu&#233;n basin reserves developments. &nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#4F81BD'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#4F81BD'><B>Local injection (MMm3/d)</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><IMG src=tgs_17.jpg width=558 height=283 alt='Picture 3' title='Picture 3'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Source: tgs&#8217; own elaboration based on internal data</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>15</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Over the last years, there have not been requirements of potential parties interested in hiring additional capacity, mainly due to the shortage of gas and the gradual decline of the conventional fields, mainly the reception areas Tierra del Fuego, Santa Cruz and Chubut. The situation was different in the Neuqu&#233;n basins where due to the Gas.Ar Plan, the gas of Vaca Muerta non-conventional fields boosted production in such region, completing transportation capacity from that basin, making it essential to expand the pipeline system so that the energy potential available in Vaca Muerta can be exploited. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In this sense, towards the end of August 2023 the first section of the GPNK pipeline (section Tratay&#233;n- Salliquel&#243;) came into service, executed by ENARSA. Its complementary works, i.e. Mercedes &#8211;&nbsp;Cardales Pipeline were started in the last week of November 2023 and the 29 km expansion over the final <B>tgs</B> sections also came into service by late August 2023. For further information, see &#8220;Natural gas transportation system operation&#8221; below.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The installation of the compressor plants that will increase the transportation capacity of this first section to 21MMm3/d is currently in process. This work is expected to be finalized in the first half of 2024.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The bidding process for the works related to the construction of the second section is still pending. Such section will range from Salliquel&#243; up to San Jer&#243;nimo &#8212;&nbsp;in the south of the Province of Santa Fe linked to the Transportadora de Gas del Norte S.A. (&#8220;TGN&#8221;) system&#8212; and includes the installation of three additional compressor plants in the section Tratay&#233;n &#8211;&nbsp;Salliquel&#243;, reaching a total natural gas transportation capacity of 39 MMm3/d.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><IMG src=tgs_18.jpg width=554 height=306 alt='Picture 5' title='Picture 5'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:7.05pt;margin-left:28.35pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The swift decline of Bolivia fields calls for a prompt response from Argentina to move forward with the norther pipeline reversal works. These works are of vital importance for the energy development of Argentina and Vaca Muerta and consist in the reversal of the natural gas flow which will take Vaca Muerta gas to the industries of C&#243;rdoba, Tucum&#225;n, La Rioja, Catamarca, Santiago del Estero, Salta and Jujuy. It will also facilitate connection to residential natural gas networks and the major </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>16</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>scale development of industrial activities, particularly lithium mining. Complementarily, reversal will decrease the cost of electricity and natural gas for the regional industries. It will also allow exports towards the north of Chile, the center of Brazil and Bolivia and will increase domestic natural gas supply. </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The startup of these works had been foreseen for the winter of 2024 but as a consequence of the received bids in December 2023 the national authorities decided to re-launch the bidding process, which will imply a delay in its start up.</P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:-11.9pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>4. OUR BUSINESSES</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We are present throughout the whole natural gas production process in Argentina, from the well-head to the transportation and distribution systems. &nbsp;We are an energy company that is part of the whole natural gas supply chain, providing services and solutions.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Since 2018, we have betted on the development of the Argentine market. Our insight, added to our expertise of over thirty years, is what guarantees the excellence of our services.&#160;&#160;&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-21.3pt;color:#00B0F0'><IMG src=tgs_19.jpg width=645 height=296 alt='Picture 4' title='Picture 4'>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>17</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:11pt Verdana;color:#00B1F1'><B>Natural Gas Transportation</B></FONT></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'><IMG src=tgs_20.jpg width=516 height=503 alt='Picture 3' title='Picture 3'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>In 2023, revenues derived from this business segment reached Ps. 99,052 million, thus presenting a negative variation of Ps. 27,515 million compared to the Ps. 126,567 million recorded in 2022. </FONT></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>18</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_21.jpg width=567 height=256 alt='Picture 5' title='Picture 5'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>Source: Our own elaboration based on internal data.</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B>Tariff Overview </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>The closing of the Tariff Review Process under ENARGAS Resolution N&#176; 310/18 was critical in the path towards the development of a sustainable natural gas transportation business that meets the high-quality standards outlined by the mentioned regulatory body, requirements that we have complied with since we were granted the License. This Tariff Review process not only entitled us to a tariff increase but also to the right of obtaining semi-annual tariff increases. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>On March 1&#176;, 2022, we received a 60% tariff increase, which could just partially offset the increase of operative costs. Said transitory tariff adjustment was the first since April 2019, almost three years had elapsed since the previous updating.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt;color:#000000;background-color:#FFFFFF'>On December 6, 2022 through Decree N&#176;815/2022, the deadline for the finalization of the Tariff Review Process was extended for one further year -December 18, 2023- as from its expiration stipulated by Decree N&#186; 1020. It also extends, as from January 1, 2023, ENARGAS intervention, instructing this entity to conduct all the measures required to ensure a transition tariff adjustment while renegotiation continues, in accordance with Decree N&#176;1020.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:11pt Calibri;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>Under this framework, on December 7, 2022, ENARGAS issued Resolution N&#176;523/2022 (&#8220;Resolution 523&#8221;) by means of which a Public Hearing was summoned to be held on January 4, 2023, to address a transitory tariff adjustment for the natural gas transportation service. At said hearing, it was informed that the increase pending to be applied since April 2019 &#8212;by virtue of the lack of semi-annual adjustments based on the Internal Wholesale Prices Index (IPIM, according to its initials in Spanish) - and the transitory adjustment granted as from March 2022&#8212; was estimated to amount to 270% as of December 2022. In spite of such calculation, <B>tgs</B> took into consideration the actual country scenario and requested a transitory tariff increase of 135% for the year 2023, with the aim of ensuring the continuity, accessibility and normal rendering of the natural gas transportation service, in conditions of safety, seeking to mitigate the economic and financial effects of higher costs and expenses related to the service.</FONT></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>19</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>On March 16, 2023, the Board of Directors of <B>tgs</B> approved the addendum proposal to the Renegotiation Transition agreement submitted by ENARGAS (&#8220;the 2023 Transition Agreement&quot;). Said addendum was later ratified by the Executive Branch through Decree N&#176; 250/2023 dated April 29, 2023. Previously, on April 27, 2023, ENARGAS had issued Resolution N&#176; 186/2023 by means of which the new tariff charts were posted.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>The terms of the 2023 Transition Agreement are similar to the ones stipulated by the Transition Agreement entered in March 2022 and they establish:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#000000;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:56.7pt;color:#000000;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Calibri;margin-left:-28.35pt'>&#45;</KBD>A 95% transitory tariff increase over the natural gas transportation tariff and the CAU, effective as from April 29, 2023.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-28.35pt;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#000000;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:56.7pt;color:#000000;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Calibri;margin-left:-28.35pt'>&#45;</KBD>During its term, <B>tgs</B> shall only distribute dividends after having requested authorization from ENARGAS, prior approval by the Ministry of Economy.&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>On December 14, 2023, through Resolution N&#176; 704/2023 ENARGAS called for a public hearing to be held on January 8, 2024. As a result of said hearing, on February 15, 2024, ENARGAS Resolution N&#176; 52/2024 was issued by means of which the validity of the public hearing was ratified and ENARGAS was given a deadline of up to 30 working days as from the issuance of this Resolution to issue the transitory tariff charts that arose from the hearing.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>Res. 52/2024 also stipulated that the determination of a monthly updating rate for natural gas transportation tariffs shall be subject to further analysis, the outcome of which will be made known within 90 working days after the issuance of the mentioned resolution.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>On December 16, 2023, Decree N&#176; 55/2023 was issued, by means of which the state of emergency of the Argentine national energy sector is declared until December 31, 2024. Among other issues, this decree: (i) stipulates the commencement of the tariff review process, (ii) dictates the intervention of the ENARGAS as from January 1&#176;, 2024, and (iii) instructs the Secretariat of Energy to issue the regulations and procedures required to sanction market prices for the natural gas transportation service.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>It must be underlined that the operation of pipelines requires a high level of investments related to the service&#8217;s quality, safety and reliability. There lies the importance of agreeing on a natural gas transportation tariff based on an economic and efficient operation that generates enough revenues to render a sustainable, safe and reliable service.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>It is also noteworthy that on September 8, 2023 <B>tgs</B> submitted to ENARGAS the request to initiate the procedure contemplated under Law N&#176; 24,076 by means of which after complying with all the proceedings and administrative procedures established for such purposes, <B>tgs</B> is granted the extension of its license term for ten further years as from the expiration of its original term (December 28 2027) for the rendering of the gas transportation services contemplating all the scope of the license approved by Decree N&#176; 2458/92. </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>20</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>As of the date of the issuance of the present annual report, we have not received an answer from ENARGAS.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Natural gas transportation system operation</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>A key milestone of the year 2023 was the coming into service of the following works, conducted by ENARSA under the National Production Program Transportar.Ar (&#8220;Transport.Ar&#8221; Program):</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:28.35pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-14.15pt'>&#183;</KBD>The first section of the GPNK, extending from Tratay&#233;n in the Province of Neuqu&#233;n up to Salliquel&#243; in the Province of Buenos Aires, interlinking with the pipelines that constitute the Final Sections under <B>tgs</B> license, contributing an additional 11 MMm3/d of natural gas in this first stage.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:28.35pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-14.15pt'>&#183;</KBD>The Expansion of <B>tgs</B> Final Sections, through the installation of 29 km of loops along the Neuba II Pipeline, adding an additional transportation capacity of 7 MM m3/d from Salliquel&#243; to the Greater Buenos Aires.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:28.35pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-14.15pt'>&#183;</KBD>The Mercedes Cardales Pipeline, which increases gas transfer from <B>tgs</B> to TGN systems, which will have a maximum capacity of 15 MMm3/d once the Compressor Plant has been installed at the wellhead, which is currently ongoing.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><B>tgs</B> is the Technical Operator of the first GPNK section-comprising its two compressor plants, after &nbsp;on June 5, 2023 through a private bidding process ENARSA awarded <B>tgs</B> such operation and maintenance for the term of 5 years, which can be extended to 12 further additional months<B>.</B> <B>tgs</B> is also the operator of the Mercedes-Cardales Pipeline, as from the signing of an agreement that contemplates a five-year term.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2023, the average daily natural gas&#8217; injection to the pipeline system operated by TGS amounted to 73.4 MMm3/d, 2% higher than the value recorded in 2022. Such injection includes contributions from the Austral, San Jorge Gulf and Neuqu&#233;n basins, including gas volumes entered to <B>tgs</B> through the GPNK in Salliquel&#243;.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The average daily injection of re-gasified Liquefied Natural Gas (&#8220;LNG&#8221;) to <B>tgs</B> system proceeding from the terminal located in Bah&#237;a Blanca was 1.7 MMm3/d, lower than the 2.8 MMm3/d recorded in the year 2022, to a certain extent due to the total substitution of such import with Neuqu&#233;n gas provided by the GPNK as from its start-up in August 2023.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>In 2023 the contribution of the Neuqu&#233;n Basin was 5% above the volume supplied the previous year, mainly due to the higher gas transportation capacity from the Vaca Muerta field facilitated by the GPNK pipeline.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>On the contrary, the southern basins of our country, decreased their injection by 6%, as their fields continued their sustained declines.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>21</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Meeting the needs of 70 direct clients and 6.3 million indirect clients, we keep performing a leading role in the natural gas industry in Argentina, rendering services either in a firm transportation (FT) or interruptible modality (IT and ED exchange and displacement)</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The total capacity contracted by clients in a firm or <B><I>&#8220;take or pay&#8221;</I></B> modality (FT) is of 83.1 MMm<SUP>3</SUP>/d, with an average life term of approximately 10 years and a half. Additionally, the system has a further capacity of 7 MMm3/d generated by the recent start-up of the Final Sections&#8217; Expansion, which has not yet been contracted by ENARSA under the <B><I>take or pay</I></B><I> </I>modality.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The breakdown of the total firm capacity per kind of client is as follows: </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:42.55pt'><IMG src=tgs_22.jpg width=402 height=282 alt='Picture 4' title='Picture 4'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;text-indent:35.4pt;margin-left:35.4pt'>Source: Own elaboration based on internal data.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>It should be noted that in this year <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>we entered new IT and ED agreements</B></FONT>, 72 corresponding to interruptible transportation and 132 to exchange and displacement services, which allow <B>tgs</B> to obtain additional revenues and meet the consumption needs or our clients.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>22</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><IMG src=tgs_23.jpg width=490 height=331 alt='Picture 4' title='Picture 4'>&nbsp;</P>

<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
