<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:28.3pt'><SUP>(1)</SUP> <FONT style=font-size:7pt>Information corresponding to consolidated financial statements</FONT>.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:28.3pt'><FONT style=font-size:7pt><SUP>(2)</SUP> Corresponds to the ratio between the annual average deliveries and the average firm contracted capacity.</FONT></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>23</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt;color:#0070C0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman;color:#000000'><IMG src=tgs_26.jpg width=379 height=29></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B><IMG src=tgs_27.jpg width=391 height=424 alt='Picture 6' title='Picture 6'></B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2023, revenues related to this segment accounted for 59% of <B>tgs</B> total revenues. This segment&#8217;s performance went down by Ps. 59,120 million in a year-over-year comparison, from Ps. 324,533 million reported in 2022 to Ps 265,413 million in 2023.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Out of the total tons dispatched in 2023, 32% of them were exported. Breaking down the tons dispatched to the domestic market, 79% were sold at dollar-denominated prices, subject to the evolution of the United States dollar.</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_28.jpg width=443 height=244 alt='Picture 7' title='Picture 7'> </FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>24</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2023,<B> tgs</B> ranked as the second ethane producer in Argentina, below MEGA, our production share went up to over 40% of total ethane produced in the year in our country. The graphs below show our share in aggregate propane and butane production in Argentina in 2023:</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_29.jpg width=567 height=219 alt='Imagen 1539762656' title='Imagen 1539762656'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Source: Energy Secretariat and internal data</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Regarding international reference prices of the products, we export &#8212;propane, butane and natural gasoline&#8212; they recorded some variations in 2023 compared to the previous year, presenting an uneven trend. During the first months of the year, they increased within the context of commodities prices recovery given the world economy recovery and the energy setbacks that Asia and Europe faced. As from the second quarter of the year prices contracted and stabilized similar to the levels of prices at the closing of 2022 and starting the last 2023 quarter they started to rise again. In spite of that, average international prices of propane, butane and natural gasoline tended to normalize, decreasing by 36%, 31%, and 16% in 2023 versus 2022, mainly as a contrast to the high levels of prices recorded in 2022.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><A name=_Hlk121334070 />In the domestic field, we keep providing propane and butane under the programs outlined by the Secretariat of Energy, under which we received partial price increases. There were significant delays in the collection of the subsidies stipulated by the National Government.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>On the other hand, it is worth pointing out that in 2023 we broke the record of ethane deliveries from our facilities in the Cerri Complex for the last 5 years. </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>25</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B>Foreign market</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Volumes sold overseas during 2023 amounted to 360,381 tons, 38,166 tons below the 398,547 tons sold in 2022.</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:-7.1pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_30.jpg width=558 height=216 alt='Imagen 1539762625' title='Imagen 1539762625'> </FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2023, Liquids Production and Commercialization exports revenues were Ps. 95,390 million and accounted for 21% of total revenues and 36% of total Liquids Production and Commercialization revenues.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><A name=_Hlk121334941 />In 2023, <B>tgs </B>continued commercializing LPG by land, dispatching roughly 15,518 trucks (379,544 tons) loaded with our own product, compare to the approximately 16,156 trucks (390,914 tons) of our own product dispatched in 2022. Trucks dispatches are basically carried out to meet our domestic demand but also allow us to export our products to neighboring countries. Although their volumes are substantially lower than the exports conducted by sea, they capitalize a higher operative margin and increase our clients&#8217; portfolio.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Propane and butane deliveries overseas were conducted in a SPOT modality in 2023, seizing opportunities related to different niche markets, which allowed us to increase considerably the fixed premiums of each transaction. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We keep upholding our positioning in the Brazilian market, which constitutes a 2024 goal, maintaining our sea exports in a direct modality (with no go-betweens) to Brazilian LPG distributors. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Regarding natural gasoline exports, throughout the year 2023 and until February 2024 we traded such product by means of an agreement entered with Trafigura Pte Ltd at an international price, less a discount. As of the date of the present Annual Report, a new agreement has been entered with Trafigura Pte Ltd., with expiration date in February 2026 and better terms than the previous agreement in force in 2023.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>26</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>As explained above, on the whole international prices presented a volatile trend throughout the year, rising in the first quarter with some slight month-over-month contractions and then returning to a rising trend in the last quarter. A rising trend is expected in the short term although below the averages of the first half of 2023.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>During 2023, and in order to encourage gas and oil exports, on October 3, 2023, the Energy Secretariat issued Resolution No. 808/2023 by which hydrocarbon exporters are included within the Export Increase Program created by Decree No. 576/22. According to this program, it is allowed to settle a proportion of exports at a different exchange rate than &quot;cash with liquidation&quot; (between 20% and 30% depending on the period in which the settlement was made), and the remainder must be paid at the official exchange rate. As of the date of issuance of this Annual Report, the proportion that may be settled by &quot;cash with liquidation&quot; amounts to 20%.</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B>Domestic Market</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2023, <B>tgs</B> continued participating in the several supply programs dictated by the National Government. Such is the case of the program for LPG Supply at subsidized prices (&#8220;Household Program&#8221;)&#8212; which had been created by National Executive Decree N&#176; 470/2015. The Household Program establishes a maximum reference price to the different links of the Liquefied Petroleum Gas (&#8220;LPG&#8221;) commercialization chain to ensure its supply to low- income residential users, forcing producers to provide LPG to fractionating companies at a determined price and with a defined quota for each of them.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The table below presents an evolution of the prices per ton (at the values of each Resolution) for butane commercialized under this program: </P>

<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>27</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>Participation in this program forces <B>tgs </B>to commercialize LPG volumes required by the controlling entity at prices inferior to market values and under certain circumstances at prices lower than their processing cost. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Regarding the Propane for Networks Agreement, on August 28, 2023, we signed its nineteenth renewal, in effect until December 31, 2023. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>This agreement contemplates compensations to be paid by the Argentine Government to the participants, calculated as the difference between the price at which propane is commercialized under this agreement and the export parity issued monthly by the Secretariat of Energy. However, there have been significant delays in the collection of the mentioned compensation. Its overdue balance as of December 31, 2023, was of Ps. 4,672 million. &nbsp;The nineteenth renewal stipulates those payments shall be conducted through fiscal credit certificates to be used by the producers for the payment of hydrocarbon export rights. As of this date, said certificates have not been issued.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>Beyond the supply programs mentioned above, we sold 158,794 tons of propane and 1,290 tons of butane mainly to the fractionating market and to a lesser extent to the industrial, propellant and automobile segments.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2023, we continued commercializing ethane, under a long-term agreement entered with PBB. This agreement contemplates similar terms to the ones agreed in the previous one, but involves improvements in the <I>take or pay clause </I>of annual compliance<I>,</I> which ensures us an increase in our sales volume to be implemented gradually over the first five years of the agreement. In the year 2023, ethane tons sold to PBB slightly went up to 394,370 tons, from the 329,232 tons recorded in 2022.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Furthermore, we continued rendering logistic services at Puerto Galvan facilities in a successful manner. </P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B><IMG src=tgs_31.jpg width=560 height=231 alt='Picture 5' title='Picture 5'></B>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>28</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B>Natural gas processing</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style=background-color:#FFFF00><IMG src=tgs_32.jpg width=288 height=471 alt='Picture 8' title='Picture 8'>  </FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2023, la <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Liquids Production reached 1,134,297 tons</B></FONT><FONT style=color:#4F81BD> </FONT>(11,658 tons above 2022 levels). Gas arriving to the Cerri Complex for processing slightly increased compared to the volume recorded in 2022, mainly as a consequence of higher volumes arriving from the Neuqu&#233;n basin, partially offset by lower gas volumes arriving from the Austral basin.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2023 <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>we dispatched 31,122 trucks</B></FONT>. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>The price of natural gas in United States dollars, acquired as PTR for processing at the Cerri Complex, was lower than the price recorded in 2022. </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Lower international liquids prices and their effect on annual average prices negatively impacted on the operating margins of this business in 2023. However, the coordinated teamwork of the different areas enhanced equipment performance through the natural gas processing daily programming, improving liquid recovery at the Cerri Complex and conducting works that will further optimize PTR consumption. Besides, thanks to the new natural gas supply agreements and the current natural gas situation, its supply was guaranteed at reasonable prices. </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>29</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-left:-28.35pt'><B><IMG src=tgs_33.jpg width=542 height=16></B>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><IMG src=tgs_34.jpg width=549 height=237 alt='Picture 12' title='Picture 12'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:28.3pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'>(1)</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD><FONT style=font-size:8pt><I>Information corresponding to consolidated financial statements</I>.</FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B>Midstream and telecommunications</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>This segments&#8217; revenues accounted for 20% of <B>tgs</B> total revenues in 2023. It mainly includes the rendering of midstream services and telecommunications. </P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_35.jpg width=168 height=40 alt='Imagen 1539762665' title='Imagen 1539762665'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:49.65pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'> <IMG src=tgs_36.jpg width=404 height=416 alt='Picture 9' title='Picture 9'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>30</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Our leadership in the natural gas industry allows us to integrate all the links of the value chain, from the wellhead to the transportation and distribution systems. We create synergies and offer differential value to our producers and clients, providing integral solutions that comprise, among others:</P>
<P align=center style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><IMG src=tgs_37.jpg width=371 height=154 alt='Picture 10' title='Picture 10'>   &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><B>Our </B>strategy is to become the leader in energy development in Argentina. At this moment, this development relies on Vaca Muerta where <B>tgs</B> has been present since 2018. This year, with our eyes set on the future we anticipated to what the country needed: we undertook the risk and built the Vaca Muerta system, which consists in 183 km of gathering pipelines that go through multiple hydrocarbon areas and a conditioning plant in Tratay&#233;n to allow the entrance of gas to the regulated transportation systems.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2023 ENARSA completed the works related to the first section of the GPNK (for further information see &#8220;Natural gas industry in Argentina&#8221;) which increased the natural gas transportation capacity between the Neuqu&#233;n basin and consumption areas by 11 MMm3/d. Also, it is a source of pride for us to have been awarded by ENARSA the operation and maintenance service of this pipeline that is of such essential importance for the development of energy in Argentina.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Focused on our facilities at the Tratay&#233;n plant and the Vaca Muerta gathering pipeline in the first half of 2023 we completed the installation of two gas conditioning modular plants by means of &nbsp;the Joule Thomson technology, each with a natural gas capacity of 3.5 MMm3/d and a new gasoline stabilization tower at our Tratay&#233;n plant. As a result of these works -which entailed an investment of US$ 22 million &#8211;&nbsp;as of this date we have a natural gas treatment capacity of 14.8MMm<SUP>3</SUP>/d.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In August 2023 we concluded the works to extend the Vaca Muerta system pipeline. With an approximate investment of US$ 60 million, this work of a 32 km length extends from the Los Toldos I Sur area up to El Trapial (Northern Vaca Muerta). The start-up of this section enables the current set up of our gathering system, with a total length of 183 km. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>Finally, we have started works for the installation of two conditioning plants with a capacity of 6,6 MMm<SUP>3</SUP>/d each one, with a total estimated investment of US$ 320 million. Its startup is expected to be gradually staged in the winter of 2024. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>These projects will expand Vaca Muerta System&#8217;s service rendering capacity, improving the profitability of the investment conducted by <B>tgs</B> and ensuring the availability of the infrastructure required to enable the production of growing natural gas volumes in Vaca Muerta, required for the </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>31</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>exploitation of the GPNK in its successive stages. It should be highlighted that these modular plants are convertible to processing with a low additional investment, constituting the first step towards the development of the natural gas processing at the heart of the Neuqu&#233;n basin.</FONT></P>
<P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'><IMG src=tgs_38.jpg width=307 height=306 alt='Imagen 710539970' title='Imagen 710539970'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>These investments also consolidate our commitment to the development of energy in Argentina and materialize our strategy to increase the profitability of our investments in the Vaca Muerta pipeline system. Hence, the building of the Nestor Kirchner natural gas pipeline is of great relevance for this business segment, as it will allow us to enlarge our business portfolio in the region.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>For that reason, we keep in talks with different basins producers, seeking to seize business opportunities that may allow us to increase our service and clients&#8217; portfolio.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The economic impact of this business on the region is remarkable, as it favors the progress of communities and contributes to the development of the Vaca Muerta area. Our accomplishments in this area mentioned above, added to other minor services we render, emphasize and tighten our bonds to our country&#8217;s natural gas producers and give us the chance to keep showing our service rendering capacity.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Lastly, we continued assessing new projects such as the construction of a gas liquefaction plant (LNG) in Bah&#237;a Blanca. This work poses an opportunity to exploit synergies with other natural gas production projects and would seize the domestic market demand drop during summer months.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#00B0F0'><FONT style=font-size:9pt;color:#000000>During the year 2023 we conducted a conceptual engineering study for the development of a project consisting in the construction of a polyduct that would allow the discharge of natural gas liquids (propane, butane and natural gasoline) to be separated at the Tratay&#233;n plant along with a plant for the fractionating, storage and dispatch of these same products. This project would go hand in hand with the conversion to gas processing of the modules that are being installed at the Tratay&#233;n plant and the installation of similar additional units, so as to facilitate the recovery of liquefiable </FONT></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>32</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#00B0F0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>components of natural gas such as propane, butane and gasoline to be then commercialized in the domestic and international market.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>At the R&#237;o Neuqu&#233;n plant, in 2023 we worked on the development of quick implementation solutions that will improve client service quality. We expect to implement such solutions in the year 2024.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Lastly, during 2023 we launched <B>tgs INTEGRA</B>. This brand comprises all the services that <B>tgs</B> offers to the market based on the human resources and tools that support the management of its own assets: from the execution of duct minor and repair works to laboratory tasks such as caliber gauging, ducts and plant maintenance, project management, blanketing facilities services, duct integrity surveys, etc.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><B>tgs</B> have been rendering these services for quite a long time and in the face of the growing demand for them, they have been grouped in a business line with their own identity to enhance their development in the market. Thus, in 2023 <B>tgs INTEGRA</B> was born and launched both in-company and externally.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'> <IMG src=tgs_39.jpg width=224 height=42 alt='Imagen 1539762637' title='Imagen 1539762637'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:12pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:12pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B><IMG src=tgs_40.jpg width=430 height=457 alt='Picture 11' title='Picture 11'></B>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>33</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Regarding the telecommunications services rendered by Telcosur, in 2023 we closed agreements that expanded our sold capacity and consolidated the operations of our telecommunications company.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Aligned to the strategy of business consolidation both in the mid and long term, Telcosur closed agreements with new clients and extended or renewed current agreements. Among the most relevant agreements, we can mention:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Agreement with Claro </B><FONT style=color:#000000><B>(</B></FONT><B>AMX Argentina</B><FONT style=color:#000000><B>)</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2023 we renewed and expanded our agreement with Claro (AMX Argentina) in connection with the use of optical fiber between Bah&#237;a Blanca and Puerto Madryn for ten years. Telcosur provides Claro with services and infrastructure in Chubut and Santa Cruz, which allows critical mission network complementarity for both companies. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Agreement with Transener</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2023 we renewed and expanded our agreement with Transener related to the use of optical fiber between Puerto Madryn and Pico Truncado for the term of fifteen years. Telcosur provides Transener with services that have been technologically upgraded as compared to the ones provided in the initial agreement in Buenos Aires, Neuqu&#233;n, Chubut and Santa Cruz, which allows critical mission network complementarity for both companies. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Installation of an optical fiber network in Vaca Muerta</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2023, we completed the works related to the installation of the high-capacity optical fiber network from Northern Vaca Muerta up to Los Toldos for over 32 km to provide telecommunication services to oil and gas producers in Vaca Muerta, who require large-volume data transmission for their operations and communications. &nbsp;Besides we installed a 72-meter mast at El Trapial node, by means of which we developed connectivity businesses and space location with carriers and producers. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2023 we conducted an optical fiber linking from the Neuqu&#233;n node to the node located at the Tratay&#233;n plant, which allowed optical fiber expansion in Vaca Muerta, providing point-to-point or multipoint solutions at constant speed and high capacity. Its voice, data, internet and video integration features allow to us to enhance costs.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>New technologies</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We moved forward with the implementation of Internet of things (IoT) to expand Telcosur service portfolios, adding augmented reality and drones to Telcosur&#8217;s service portfolio. We made progress with the development of digital twins for the masts that support radio aerials and implemented an innovating system of monitoring of aerial support structures. We implemented a predictive data network monitoring system that facilitated the early detection of events in networks and promotes proactive action towards client service.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>34</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>TELCOSUR Brand</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We worked on the regular issuance of new developments, success cases and relevant Telcosur information in social networks to maximize the brand visibility in sector&#8217;s stakeholders. Also, Telcosur was published in the alliances map of Convergencia (a leader in telecommunications market information). Besides, a new website was developed for Telcosur with updated information and a modern look and feel in line with market standards.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Telcosur actively participated of the AOG 2023 event, generating business prospecting meetings with active and potential clients.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Bidding and Agreement with Total Energies</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We participated in the bidding of links of Total Energies in the Neuqu&#233;n and Austral basins, managing to keep the existing links, expand their capacities and add new sites to the links contracted by the client, building customer loyalty with a strategic producer of the segment.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Relocation of operations to the new Site</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We actively participated in the project for the design and migration of the telecommunications infrastructure that allows the operation of the company at the new site. Likewise, we conducted the tasks required to migrate client services to the new <B>tgs</B> offices, with minimum impact on operations. We also worked along with the <B>tgs </B>IT team in the implementation of the infrastructure required for the operation under new technologies in the cloud and its node in the Skyonline data center.</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B1F1'><B>FINANCIAL AND OPERATIVE INDICATORS OF MIDSTREAM AND</B></P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><FONT style=font-size:9pt;color:#00B1F1><B>TELECOMMUNICATIONS SEGMENT</B></FONT></P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman;color:#000000'><IMG src=tgs_41.jpg width=539 height=273 alt='Picture 13' title='Picture 13'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>35</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:7.1pt;color:#0070C0'><B>5. Conduction of Operations and Safety</B></P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style=font-size:10pt;color:#00B0F0><B>Pipeline system operated by tgs in Argentina</B></FONT><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B> </B><B><IMG src=tgs_42.jpg width=346 height=489 alt='Picture 3' title='Picture 3'></B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Pipeline Investment Works</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The gas pipeline system operated by <B>tgs</B> adequately met both the natural gas transportation demand and the regulatory entity&#8217;s requirements. To ensure service continuity without future interruptions, an efficient management of the pipeline system entails the performance of assessment, maintenance, inspection, mitigation and prevention tasks to avoid failures that may have a negative impact.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Throughout the 2023, we conducted integrity assessment tasks, such as in-line inspections along 2,559 km of pipelines and pipelines evaluations through direct assessment techniques, namely CIS surveys over 233 km and DCVG surveys over 255 km (on sections with no Scraper Traps). The purpose of these tasks is to identify, assess and control threats to pipelines integrity - such as external corrosion, geometrical defects, manufacture and/or construction anomalies, recoating failures, among others.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>36</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>As part of our 2023 recoating program, we executed recoating replacements over 13 km of the pipeline system. This pipeline rehabilitation program has been the most significant of this kind ever undertaken by <B>tgs</B>, not only because of its economic scope but also due to the technical deployment it has entailed. We are also carrying out recoating works at three valve facilities along the San Martin Pipeline in the province of Buenos Aires.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>We also conducted non &#8211;destructive tests by means of the use of magnetic particles, ultrasound and hardness measurements for the identification of anomalies in longitudinal weld seams, stress corrosion cracking (&#8220;SCC&#8221;), by means of which we were able to detect anomalies that could be mitigated through pipe replacements. These recoating works aim at mitigating the risk of external corrosion and/or SCC as well as to survey and correct any pipe anomaly that in the future may deteriorate the ducts and interrupt the service. This is how we extend the useful life of our facilities.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>Also in this regard, in order to mitigate corrosive activity, we carried out filling works in reinforcement sleeves to ensure integrity in high population density areas.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Within the SCC assessment and mitigation plan, we implemented an inline inspection plan with &#8220;EMAT&#8221; technology to detect cracking, having completed the inspection of 567 km in 2023. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Departing from an analysis and planning carried out by the pipeline integrity team, we conducted an assessment campaign of 157 external corrosion and geometrical defects, anomalies in longitudinal and circumferential seams, which required immediate repairs that were executed by means of the installation of 30 pipe replacements and 21 welding reinforcements.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In addition, we assessed the integrity of 10 derivations located in the Las Heras &#8211;&nbsp;Pacheco pipeline section. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>On the topic of cathodic protection, to mitigate the advance of corrosion and improve reliability, we kept strengthening the system with the installation of 2 new units and 10 current reinforcement lenses as well as the renewal of 4 facilities that were obsolete. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>All the works mentioned above&#8212; which enable us to verify the overall condition of the facility and conduct the required adjustments for their service continuity&#8212; underline our commitment to safety and to the care of our people and the environment, all main cornerstones in our pledge to render a reliable natural gas transportation service. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Being the Buenos Aires ring a high population density area and driven by our goal to minimize inherent risks in such pipeline segment, we continued with the implementation of works that mitigate third parties&#8217; damage. Thus, we continue with the expansion of the optical fiber intruder detection system by 8 further km of pipeline, in the cities of Las Heras and Ca&#241;uelas. This work complements the one completed last year, totaling an aggregate coverage of 116 km of pipeline monitored via intruder detection systems. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Likewise, in order to raise awareness among the community, we increased our facilities&#8217; signaling and &#8212;in the areas neighboring the most sensitive sections of the system&#8212; we conducted virtual and remote damage prevention activities, such as communications via radio, media and networks </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>37</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>for the public in general, meetings with municipalities and ground-breaking &amp; services companies in Buenos Aires, Neuqu&#233;n and Rio Negro. Also, we conducted door-to-door communication campaigns in Neuqu&#233;n, Rio Negro and Bah&#237;a Blanca in the communities near the pipeline, with the aim of raising awareness regarding gas pipelines&#8217; related risks and how to prevent them. </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Concerned with the safety of our people, we concluded works to adapt the grounding of 22 Cathodic Protection units, thus reducing the risk of electric shock at our facilities.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 2023, we successfully finalized the winter maintenance plan, which allows us to ensure the safe and reliable operation of pipelines. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>One of the relevant matters we dealt with this year was the entrance of the GPNK into <B>tgs&#8217;</B> transportation system at the Saturno plant. This added around 11 MMm<SUP>3</SUP>/d to the final sections systems, which implied a reorganizing of the volumes distributed through the Neuba II pipeline and its own loop. Additionally, the connection of the new pipeline Mercedes &#8211;&nbsp;Cardales generated new dynamics in the management of incremental gases in deliveries both to the Buenos Aires distribution ring and to TGN.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Regarding technological changes, we are proceeding with the definition, development and implementation of the remote operation of compressor plants. We have made progress in the technological updating of the elements with the aim of including Saturno Plant to this new transportation system control philosophy. This is the first step towards what we refer to as our &#8220;Compressor Plants Operation Updating.&#8221; </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>At the Piedrabuena and Olavarr&#237;a compressor plants, we completed the installation of new control systems in turbine compressors to replace the original Hispano Suiza systems. This will derive in greater operative reliability and improvements in units&#8217; maintenance.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>At the Belisle plant we are moving forward with the replacement of the Foxboro control system with a new control system based on PLC. We are currently working on detail engineering and purchase of supplies for their integration.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>At the Fiat and Bosque Petrificado plants we are making progress in the replacement of gas indirect heaters which will have their control systems updated, besides having the <I>envueltas</I> and coils changed. With respect to the Indirect Heater of the Fiat plant, its technology is being changed to an electric heating system.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>As part of our environmental care activities, we are still working on the installation of venting systems at the Fort&#237;n Uno, Gaviotas and La Adela plants. At the Cervantes, Belisle and Chelfor&#243; plants, we are installing biodigesters as an improvement to the current sewage effluent systems.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Works at Liquids Facilities</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We conducted works aimed at extending the useful life of pressure containers and ducts, to boost their reliability, reduce risks at facilities and meet government requirements. The scope of these tasks included several unit inspections and preventive maintenance at our facilities, including the </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>38</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>Galvan Plant dock polyducts. A further remarkable maintenance task we concluded was the replacement of the whole insulation material of the B heater, to which a new safety system was added as well.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We conducted works to improve the reliability of a steam turbine at the eco-energy plant within the execution of major maintenance works. We carried out inspections in the steam heater in line with government requirements, repaired the facilities and also executed anti-corrosive protection works in ducts and critical equipment.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Committed to the continuous and safe management of our facilities, we certified and adjusted the relief systems of 7 spheres of Planta Galv&#225;n, 2 Langmar tanks and the absorbing tank in accordance with current regulations. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Pursuing our path towards client satisfaction and environmental care, we installed a new CO<SUB>2</SUB> analyzer in the ethane product, and we assembled an air compressor for quality certification chromatographs.&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>On the other hand, we started investments to mitigate technological obsolescence risks and increase our processes&#8217; operative safety. We can highlight the following developments at the Galv&#225;n Plant: civil work conditioning of the electric distribution rooms in Galvan Plant for the new transformer, acquisition of cryogenic material for the replacement of ducts in the Cooling Plant with critical indications, adaptation of Tank V1 and the new level measurement systems for spheres 1, 2 and 3. At the Cryogenic Plant, we can point out at the following projects that required a full Cerri Complex stoppage: &nbsp;the removal of the original dashboard of the Cryogenic Plant control room, the finalization of the Safety Instrument System, new medium-voltage cells at the Cryogenic Plant and the revamping of the 110VCC electric distribution system. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Addressing the need to update operative technologies, we conducted works to enhance cybersecurity at the Galv&#225;n Plant and the Cerri Complex. Besides, in the field of safety we conducted works on the firefighting systems, of which it is worth mentioning the new firefighting central for the Cerri Complex storage, the new firefighting devices for the Langmar plant and the technological upgrading of the CO2 tank at the Cryogenic Plant.&#160;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>39</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B>Works at Vaca Muerta Facilities</B></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Making headway with the expansion of the Tratay&#233;n plant &#8212;process developed through projects required to increase our Midstream services offer&#8212;, this year we installed two conditioning units with Joule Thompson technology that increase the plant&#8217;s conditioning capacity by 7 MMm3/day. We are also executing the installation of two other Propak conditioning turbine expanders modules that in the future may be converted to natural gas processing, each of which will increase conditioning capacity by 6.6 MMm3/day. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>On the other hand, focused on the growth of the Vaca Muerta system, in 2023 we launched the works required to include auxiliary services such as a new gasoline stabilization plant (the third for this facility), the expansion facilities for natural gas inflows and outflows as well as the purchase of new land.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>As mentioned above, we completed the expansion of the Northern Vaca Muerta pipeline from Los Toldos&#160;to the Trapial field with a duct of a 32 km length and a nominal diameter of 30 inches.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Lastly, we also participated in works for third parties, putting into service several facilities such as the Vista and Tecpetrol links to the Vaca Muerta Pipeline System.</P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>6. Environment, Social and Governance Matters</B></P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We commit to running our businesses and operating our facilities in compliance with the requirements of applicable legislation and the obligations to which we willingly adhere. We strive to meet our customers&#8217; expectations, prioritizing our service quality, pollution prevention and our staff and contractors&#8217; safety and health through the continuous improvement of our management system&#8217;s efficiency. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>With that in mind, in 2023 we kept working on our Environmental, Social, Governance Plan (the &#8220;ESG Plan&#8221;), prioritizing these three main topics and action lines that will drive our purpose on this matter. This year we carried out an overall diagnosis of our ESG management and its gap with international standards (GRI, SASB, NIIF) in order to set the guidelines to manage sustainability. In terms of environmental management, we obtained a very good score of 2.3 points based on the desired result of 3.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Driven by our mission and focusing on the role we seek to play in the communities where we develop our businesses, the general goals of our ESG plan are:</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>40</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>

</TD><TD valign=top bgcolor=#E36C0A style=width:146.3pt;padding-left:5.4pt;padding-right:5.4pt><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#7F7F7F'><B>SOCIAL</B></P>

<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B>6-a) ENVIRONMENT</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>In 2023 we worked on our strategic environmental plan (2022 &#8211;&nbsp;2026), which links the environment to all phases of business processes, from strategic decision-making to risk and opportunities management, planning, design and execution of activities. The plan comprises the following strategic axes, plans and action programs:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#548DD4'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><B>1.</B></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD><B>Mitigation and adaptation to climate change</B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>Our strategic environmental plan outlines a <FONT style=color:#4F81BD><B>50%</B> <B>reduction goal</B> </FONT>for our methane emissions for the year 2030 (departing from emissions recorded in the year 2021).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>Towards that goal, we launched a comprehensive emissions action plan, including investments, improvements and good practices required to fulfil it. The plan included the following actions:</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Wingdings;margin-left:-18pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#216;</FONT></KBD><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Enhance our emissions inventory:</B></FONT> we conducted a gap analysis of our inventory against ISO 14064-1 standard (Carbon Footprint Management System). We also made progress in quantifying leakages. To such end, we created a data base with the points detected in 2022 and 2023. We also carried out quantification tests to know the measurement method. At present, we are assessing several proposals for leakage detection with existing innovating technology.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'>We also made progress in <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>S</B><B>cope 3 emissions recording </B></FONT>into our emissions inventory (emissions derived from our supply chain and that are not under our control). To this purpose, we identified all possible sources of indirect emissions and then weighted them under criteria selected to determine a gradual &nbsp;integration.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Wingdings;margin-left:-18pt'>&#216;</KBD>Implementation of a <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>technology for monitoring </B></FONT>those contributions not detected so far, in order to include them in the preventive maintenance program.&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>41</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Wingdings;margin-left:-18pt'>&#216;</KBD>We carry out tasks for the identification and elimination of currents that contribute gases to <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>flare burning</B></FONT> in normal operations conditions at the Cerri Complex and Midstream segment plants.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Wingdings;margin-left:-18pt'>&#216;</KBD>We communicated <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>the </B><B>g</B><B>ood practices work programs</B> </FONT>that may contribute to the reduction of emissions and energy efficiency. &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Wingdings;margin-left:-18pt'>&#216;</KBD>We developed a five-year investment plan to <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>introduce technology and equipment </B></FONT>that can reduce emissions. &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Wingdings;margin-left:-18pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#216;</FONT></KBD><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>We systematized the analysis of our projects and improvements&#8217; impact on the emissions inventory </B></FONT>to ensure that from the beginning they meet the criteria outlined by our emissions reductions&#8217; strategy. &nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_43.jpg width=567 height=273 alt='Imagen 1539762682' title='Imagen 1539762682'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Calibri;margin:0;color:#7030A0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#808080'>Source: Our own elaboration with internal data</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>42</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:10pt Calibri;margin:0;color:#F79646'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman;color:#000000'><IMG src=tgs_44.jpg width=567 height=326 alt='Imagen 1539762680' title='Imagen 1539762680'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#808080'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman;color:#000000'> &nbsp;</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>We are in the process of contracting a survey of the physical risks related to climate change throughout the whole company, using scenario analysis tools (modelled) with historical weather data, in line with standards applicable on this matter.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>On the topic of energy, in 2023, we devised the energy matrix based on which we will outline our energy efficiency goal. In 2024 we will proceed to implement the Energy Management System in accordance with Standard ISO 50001.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>To further mitigate and adapt to the climate change, we also worked on foresting actions and biodiversity care, underlining our commitment to these matters. To such end, we presented 3 axes:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:25.1pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:-18pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#8226;</FONT></KBD><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Foresting Policy</B></FONT>: pursuing the mitigation of greenhouse gas effects and to favor ecosystems, &nbsp;this policy sets out the guidelines for the recovery and protection of the forest existing at our facilities.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:25.1pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:-18pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#8226;</FONT></KBD><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Sustainable and Aware Foresting Plan</B></FONT>: derived from our foresting policy, we conduct a diagnosis and inventory of the species existing at each facility and define our foresting or reforesting plan.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:25.1pt;color:#000000'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:-18pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#8226;</FONT></KBD><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Biodiversity</B></FONT>: by means of this analysis we seek to identify protected areas and species of preservation value existing in the provinces reached by our businesses, in order to monitor them and promote the preservation of natural ecosystems in the sites where we conduct our operations.&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>43</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#548DD4'><B>2. Proactive environmental leadership</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>We have followed up the work of facilities leaders so that they continue deepening their roles in environmental management. Besides, we conducted training activities on the environmental strategic plan, to communicate it and drive action. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>As every year, we conducted environmental awareness programs that relate to special environmental celebrations or events, which included talks on topics of interest, trivia and information on several undertaken initiatives.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>On our <B>tgs </B>CAMPUS, environmental training material is available for our whole staff.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>We conducted a cycle of talks, within the framework of the World Environment Day (June 5), in which renowned specialists talked about the four selected topics:&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:53.85pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:16.95pt></KBD>Climate change effects on business management.&#160;&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:53.85pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:16.95pt></KBD>Foresting ecosystem services.&#160;&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:53.85pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:16.95pt></KBD>The importance of water management or water footprint.&#160;&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:53.85pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:16.95pt></KBD>Energy efficiency, energy transition and renewable energies.&#160;&#160;&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>It was an opportunity to learn and deepen our knowledge on such topics and also to provide an update on how we have addressed them at <B>tgs</B>. The talks were attended by 600 people and videos of such training days were uploaded at the tgs CAMPUS for anyone who wants to see them.&#160;&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>In 2023 we set out to devise the basis and conditions to outline an Excellence Program for all our facilities, to be launched in 2024. This program will seek to acknowledge <B>tgs</B> facilities that carry out their environmental management with leadership.</P>
<P style='font:10pt Calibri;margin:0;color:#7030A0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#548DD4'><B>3. Circular processes and consumption</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'><FONT style=color:#548DD4><B><I>Water management</I></B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>We continue driving our goal defined in our ESG Plan, through which by the year 2025 we set out to recover 80% of sewage effluents for irrigation. This year we made progress through the implementation of biodigesters at R&#237;o Negro facilities, replacing the existing absorbing pits.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'><FONT style='font:10pt Arial;background-color:#FFFFFF'>We are moving forward towards the quantification of our direct water footprint to achieve a more efficient management of this limited natural resource. This indicator is a first step in the achievement of efficient improvements in water management and awareness of our water consumption and use.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'><FONT style=color:#548DD4><B><I>Waste Management</I></B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>The goal outlined in our ESG Strategic plan is to achieve a recycling rate over 50% by 2026, which drove us to carry out several actions to minimize generated waste. &nbsp;We conducted several awareness campaigns and training activities that encouraged steps towards a paperless office, reduction of plastic items of only one use, hiring of new waste operators, disposal of electric devices in disuse, among others.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>44</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#00B0F0'><A name=_Hlk159841107 />&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B>6-b) SOCIAL</B></P>
<P style='font:12pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>HUMAN CAPITAL</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We closed a growth and expansion year that witnessed great achievements and brought us many acknowledgements. Our talent attraction strategy is based on positioning <B>tgs</B> brand both in the <I>oil &amp; gas </I>industry and other markets, seeking to attract the best talent. This year we added further and renewed proposals of agreements with universities and participation in exhibitions and employment fairs in our country. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Driven by our purpose of developing a sustainable business, we place a strong focus on our neighboring communities, generating local employment. To that end, we participated in supervised professional practices for high schools, aiming at strengthening the bond between companies and educational institutions. We also conduct employability workshops that target the students of the last years of technical schools in the provinces of Buenos Aires and Neuqu&#233;n, to provide them with tools to get higher quality jobs.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#0070C0'><B>We play a leading role in our country economic growth and we know our people are key to our success.</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We develop a healthy and challenging work environment and we watch over the integral wellbeing of our people. <FONT style=color:#4F81BD><B>We have been one of the greatest places to work in Argentina for over 10 years, as certified by the global firm Great Place to Work</B></FONT>.<FONT style=color:#4F81BD><B> </B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>This year, we also implemented our <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>On</B><B>-</B><B>Boarding Process</B></FONT>, which facilitates learning about <B>tgs</B> business in a quick and effective manner and also addresses the key issues that every employee needs to know during his/her first stage at the company. Our brand grows both in-company and externally. The adoption of good practices in our onboarding process not only promotes the early adjustment to our organizational culture, but also improves insight into our organization and strengthens our identity as employers.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In a fast-paced and challenging rhythm, we have been able to close <FONT style=color:#4F81BD><B>119 selection processes, including new employees&#8217; recruitment as well as internal promotions</B></FONT> <FONT style=color:#4F81BD><B>opportunities.</B><B> </B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>This year we reactivated <FONT style=color:#4F81BD><B>our Ambassadors Program, the aim of which is to build a network of transforming agents who can communicate the desired culture to fulfil our dream.</B></FONT> This team undertakes initiatives that make them leaders in <B>tgs</B> culture and communication, representing our values and the cornerstones of an open, dynamic and cooperative management.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>45</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Learning at tgs </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We are proud of our achievements. The learning process acts as leverage to our strategy and is a key factor to develop knowledge and organizational capacities. For this reason, we decided to go far beyond designing a simple online courses platform and thus gave birth to <B>tgs</B> Campus, our learning ecosystem. <B>tgs</B> Campus has<FONT style=color:#0070C0><B> been distinguished by a special mention by the </B></FONT>IAPG, as an outstanding Human Resources innovation and practice. </P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Growing and leading at tgs</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>At <B>tgs</B> we promote the culture of empowerment, in which every person plays the leading role in his/her own performance. &nbsp;Through a cycle of planning, follow-up and assessment, each worker proposes objectives aligned to the business management, plans his/her own continuous improvement, assesses his/her own results and conduct and thinks along with his/her supervisors about his/her &nbsp;learning. Thus, performance processes provide information for the decision-making with respect to learning, development and compensation. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Likewise, we <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>set up Talent Committees</B></FONT> to survey our workers&#8217; current and projected capacity and assess their potential to take on positions of greater complexity in the future. Thanks to this process and through a detailed analysis of competences, we identify the potential leaders of the organization, manage talent and conduct a strategic planning regarding the needs of work teams and our business. This facilitates decision-making on the topic of promotions, transfers, turnovers, project team and replacement charts.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The result of this work provides us with <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>talent matrixes for all the departments</B></FONT> and complete <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>sustainability maps</B></FONT> for leadership positions in our company. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>With our eyes set on future development, <FONT style=color:#4F81BD><B>we have hired Young Professionals in the sixth edition of the program </B><B>named after them</B><B>.</B></FONT> This initiative undoubtedly inspires us to think of the leaders of our <B>tgs</B> dream, adding new energies and profiles, challenging us to achieve a high-level performance.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We have devised several programs that aim at identifying organizational performances in line with our target organizational culture. In this sense, the programs &#8220;+Energy&#8221; and &#8220;Plus!&#8221; continued throughout the 2023. </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>46</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Number of workers as of December 31, 2023</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:14.2pt'> <IMG src=tgs_45.jpg width=250 height=182 alt='Picture 12' title='Picture 12'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:113.4pt;color:#0070C0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman;color:#000000'><IMG src=tgs_46.jpg width=362 height=22></FONT><B> &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;Workers breakdown by gender</B><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman;color:#000000'> </FONT></P>
