<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:127.6pt;color:#0070C0'><B><IMG src=tgs_47.jpg width=293 height=415 alt='Picture 15' title='Picture 15'></B>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:10pt Verdana;color:#0070C0'><B>Diversity and inclusion:</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Changes in the social context gave rise to the need of addressing diversity, inclusion and free of violence spaces.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>This year we set up the Committee of Diversity and Inclusion, which raised awareness of the topic throughout the whole organization. We started with the design of an activity under the framework of Women&#8217;s Month and later a parenting workshop. After that, we conducted two awareness meetings on topics related to diversity, inclusion, violence, discrimination and harassment at the </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>47</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>workplace with the participation of our Management Committee, Human Resources and Institutional Relations. These are clear steps towards organizational changes leading to actual inclusion.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>This approach to diversity and inclusion started with an overall diagnosis conducted in the year 2022, along with an action plan focused on the development of inclusive spaces, free from violence.&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>As we have outlined in our ESG Plan, we keep performing actions that turn us into a more diverse company. For instance, we have promoted women to positions commonly held by men as shift supervisors, gas dispatch operators, plant leaders and truck load operators. On the other hand, we are in the process of developing the protocols for dealing with cases of violence and harassment.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Relations with Unions</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Our 2023 calendar was busy with meetings with union representatives. Whereas we had initially foreseen quarterly meetings, given our country&#8217;s inflationary context in the second half of the year we were forced to hold monthly collective bargaining negotiations meetings. Outstanding goodwill from both parties &#8212;the company and union representatives&#8212; allowed talks to be channeled into an appropriate framework in which salaries were collectively agreed in a satisfactory manner for both parties, preventing direct action measures.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Thus, this year, we entered conventional and collective bargaining agreements which were submitted before the Ministry of Work and Social Security for their respective approvals.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Occupational Health</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Taking care of the safety and health of our people is one of the cornerstones of our performance. &nbsp;As a prevention initiative, we conducted several activities to increase labor accidents awareness and prevention, focusing on accidents related to upper limbs as they accounted for 38% of recorded labor accidents over the last 20 years.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Whereas we are aware that our tasks are conducted in a natural environment that in certain circumstances may entail a threat to the health and safety of our people, we always bear in mind our goal to preserve and respect the environment. Therefore, along with the Malbr&#225;n Institute we coordinated actions that allow us to raise awareness on the importance and management of accidents produced by poisonous animals through training conducted either onsite or online.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Another 2023 outstanding initiative has been the recording of health checkups and controls for our own employees or contractors&#8217; staff. Thanks to this tool and its follow-up, occupational health has gained further control over and scope of action to improve the health of both our personnel and our contractors&#8217; staff.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We have increased the functionalities of our internal health self-management tool. This year we added the function &#8220;aptitude for the role&#8221;, which updates personnel information whenever health checkups are conducted at the different facilities. Besides helping us to manage the health of each worker, this tool generates personalized recommendations and allows us to assess if our workers </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>48</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>are fit to perform tasks such as: driving heavy or light vehicles, height jobs, confined spaces or perform as brigade volunteers.&#160;</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We inaugurated a new space at the Galv&#225;n Plant to perform preventive health checkups to hazardous loads drivers, targeting all the drivers who daily load products at such facility. This measure allows us to make progress in accidents prevention and in the detection of any anomaly that may impact on their health.&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>We launched the new Wellbeing Program, which addresses psychological factors and career transition issues, including health aspects that impact on the wellbeing of our people.&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B> &#8220;+Energy Program&#8221; </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>In the year 2019 we created the acknowledgement program known as </FONT><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana;color:#0070C0'><B>&#8220;+Energy&#8221;, </B></FONT><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>which underlines and acknowledges the achievements of our workers who transmit <B>tgs</B> culture through their individual, teamwork and/or their commitment with the organization. Throughout this year, we have acknowledged: &#160;</FONT></P>
<P align=center style='font:11pt Calibri;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#F79646'><B>692</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#4F81BD'><B>workers for their outstanding actions</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#4F81BD'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#F79646'><B>250</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#4F81BD'><B>for their participation in teams </B></P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#F79646'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#F79646'><B>93</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#1F497D'><FONT style=color:#4F81BD><B>acknowledgements to track record in the Company</B></FONT></P>
<P align=center style='font:11pt Calibri;margin:0;color:#1F497D'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Benefits program</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>We offer an outstanding and very competitive benefits package: &#8220;</FONT><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana;color:#4F81BD'><B>Plus!&#8221;, </B></FONT><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>designed to address the needs of all our staff. Organized by segment, the program offers creative and specific options that consider family, health promotion and care, recreation, loans and insurance. These benefits ensure a flexible and comfortable work environment and have been devised to support all our employees and their families in every life stage. </FONT></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>49</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:11pt Calibri;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:8pt;color:#242424'><FONT style='font:10pt Verdana;color:#0070C0'><B>SAFETY AND HEALTH</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><A name=_Hlk158298025 />Safety is one of the values that stands out in our performance. Focused on prevention and risk minimization, we strive to generate commitment to a self-care culture in safety. We understand Occupational Safety and Health as a consequence of the engagement of all the persons who are part of our company and interact with us, so its scope reaches our own staff, third parties&#8217; personnel, suppliers and the community on the whole. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Aware of the relevance of our workers&#8217; safety and health, in 2022 we prepared our Health and Safety Strategic Plan approved by the Management Committee. It is made up of three strategic axes: </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Wingdings;margin-left:-18pt'>&#252;</KBD>Preventive Leadership Culture for an Interdependent Organization &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Wingdings;margin-left:-18pt'>&#252;</KBD>Risk-based industrial processes for high reliability facilities&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Wingdings;margin-left:-18pt'>&#252;</KBD>An organization prepared for contingencies, focused on loss minimization&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>This plan is of mandatory compliance at all organization levels: processes, stakeholders and workers. It seeks to achieve a high degree of commitment to obligations related to safe and healthy work conditions throughout the whole production chain, by means of activities of prevention, identification, assessment and control of labor risks. The ultimate purpose of the plan is to avoid incidents, professional illnesses and situations that affect service quality and response to emergencies.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>We also take care of labor hygiene by means of several programs whose main goals are to identify, assess and control physical, chemical and biological risk agents of a labor origin. To that end, in 2023 we increased our investment in equipment and measurement instruments in terms of Safety and Health.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:35.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B>SAC Culture program as value&#160;</B></P>
<P style='font:9pt Segoe UI;margin:0'><FONT style='font:10pt Arial;color:#7030A0'>&#160;</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;color:#0070C0;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana;color:#000000'>The program Safety, Environment and Quality Culture (SEQ or &#8220;SAC&#8221;, for its acronym in Spanish) as Value is a key cornerstone within our Labor Risk Prevention Programs. This initiative is part of the axis &#8220;Preventive Leadership Culture for an Interdependent Organization&#8221; within <B>tgs</B><B>&#8217;</B> Health and Safety Strategic Plan.&#160;&#160;This axis seeks to consolidate good practices in terms of safety and health that can improve our performance. In this line, we have a platform on health and safety at work for our employees, raising awareness in the area and boosting commitment to it. Among these training programs, we may mention </FONT><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'><B>Safe and Sustainable Vehicles</B><B> Driving Program and Brigade Volunteers Training Program</B></FONT><FONT style=color:#000000><B>.</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'>The scope of our commitment to safety and health reaches our contractors. Therefore, we implemented initiatives such as the standardization of health and safety requirements, an interactive training system and the creation of an assessment matrix. We have also strengthened communication through regular meetings and we have introduced updates, such as a work permits </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>50</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>system. Our strategy aims at compliance with safety standards, continuous improvement and adaptation to current technologies, showing our commitment to safety, quality and sustainability in projects with the engagement of third parties.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-left:53.5pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Quality Management System</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The Safety, Health, Environment and Quality Policy outlines the commitment of the Management on these matters. It is the reference on which the Integrated Management System is based (SGI) in accordance with ISO 45001:2018, ISO 14001:2015 and ISO 9001:2015 and is aligned to the organizational dream or challenge and the desired culture.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The Policy is posted at the company website: <FONT style='color:#0000FF;border-bottom:1px solid #0000FF'>https://www.tgs.com.ar/our-company/environment</FONT>. <B>tgs </B>has Environment, Safety and Quality Strategic Plans to ensure the fulfilment of commitments undertaken in the Safety, Health, Environment and Quality Policy. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#548DD4'><FONT style=color:#000000>At&#160;<B>tgs</B><B>,</B>&#160;we carry out our processes in accordance with the following principles:</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:35.7pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-17.85pt'>&#183;</KBD>Context and stakeholders&#8217; due diligence to determine and address emerging risks and opportunities.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:35.7pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-17.85pt'>&#183;</KBD>Identification of hazards and risk control to prevent injuries and provide safe and healthy work conditions.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:35.7pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-17.85pt'>&#183;</KBD>Environmental protection, including pollution prevention and minimization of environmental impacts derived from our activities.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:35.7pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-17.85pt'>&#183;</KBD>Fulfilment of our clients&#8217; needs and expectations, prioritizing the quality of our products and services, competitiveness and profitability.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:35.7pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-17.85pt'>&#183;</KBD>Compliance with legal and other requirements.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:35.7pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-17.85pt'>&#183;</KBD>Clear and transparent communication that facilitates channels for queries and the participation of all workers at all company levels.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:35.7pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-17.85pt'>&#183;</KBD>Culture of achievement, through the management of goals and indicators.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:35.7pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-17.85pt'>&#183;</KBD>Development and growth of our employees, promoting improvement in their competences and raising of their awareness.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>In 2023, we successfully passed the Maintenance Audit of ISO 14001:2015 / ISO 9001:2015 and ISO 45001:2018. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>Besides, we formed a group of candidates to enlarge the internal auditors pool in accordance with ISO Standards, for which we conducted an assessment and equalization of behaviors based on the personal profile. Several of these auditors have already started their participation in the internal audits&#8217; programs of the year 2023.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>51</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>SUSTAINABILITY MANAGEMENT</B></P>
<P style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Our Sustainability Policy strengthens our operative system reliability, focusing on three cornerstones: &nbsp;environment, social and corporate governance. It seeks to generate socioeconomic value in the mid and long-term for the communities where our facilities are located, far beyond meeting mandatory regulations, </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Our social investment programs strategy and development follow the guidelines of the United Nations 2030 Agenda. Through a thorough work we selected 9 of the 17 Sustainable Development Goals (SDG) considered priority for our company, to be able to focus and empower our efforts to contribute to specific goals.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'>Towards SDG N&#176; 4 related to Quality Education, we leveraged the following programs:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'> &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&#8220;<FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Learn a Trade</B></FONT> <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Program in Bah&#237;a Blanca </B><B>and</B><B> Neuqu&#233;n</B></FONT>&#8221;: This program has been conducted since 2007 in cooperation with La Piedad Technical Institute (&#8220;ITLP&#8221;, according to its acronym in Spanish). Its aim is to impart training in trades to unemployed young people, empowering them through the acquisition of skills. It was expanded to Neuqu&#233;n with the cooperation of the San Jos&#233; Obrero School. It offers courses of carpentry, 3d printing, metal mechanic and basic welding.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>&#8220;</B><B>Schools Infrastructure Improvement</B><B>&#8221;</B><B> Program</B><B>:</B><B> </B></FONT>Started in 2018, this program aims at improving public educational institutions to increase graduation rates, reduce grade repetition and school drop-out and strengthen socio-emotional skills. We cooperate with government authorities and&#8212; after works are conducted&#8212; we impart awareness workshops to generate a sense of belonging among students. The program aims at strengthening educational quality and the local community. &#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Scholarship</B><B> program &#8211;&nbsp;</B><B>( </B><B>&#8220;S</B><B>upport me</B><B>&#8221;</B><B>)</B><B>:</B></FONT>&#160;This scholarship program, driven by <B>tgs</B>, is operated at the San Jos&#233; Obrero school in Neuqu&#233;n. Its objective is to increase opportunities for young people in vulnerability &nbsp;contexts. Scholarships comprise specialized areas such as Industrial Mechanics, Electricity, Carpentry and more. The equitable distribution of scholarships between genders shows our commitment to give a significant boost to education and the development of talented young people. &#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Support in Education:</B></FONT>&#160;For the 15&#186; consecutive year, <B>tgs</B>&#160;cooperates with the Cruzada Patag&#243;nica to provide people from isolated rural communities with access to education. It seeks to benefit over 370 students in agrotechnical secondary studies, providing them with free housing accommodations near their educational sites.&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>One of our educational programs has been acknowledged by the Argentine Business Council for Sustainable Development (&#8220;CEADS&#8221;, according to its acronym in Spanish) within the initiative known as &#8220;<FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Connecting Companies with SDGs</B></FONT>&#8221;, where we have presented actions that contribute specifically and mainly to the SDG N&#176;4 as well as to the SDG N&#176; 8: Decent work and economic </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>52</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>growth. The </FONT><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana;color:#0070C0'><B>Women in Circle Program</B></FONT><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>, endorsed an active commitment to gender equality in the community development. To fulfil it, an alliance was established with the foundation </FONT><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana;color:#242424'>Fundaci&#243;n Estudios Patag&#243;nicos &nbsp;and we imparted two trade training activities for which we sought a majority participation of women. </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>Another initiative that&#8212;as Women in Circle&#8212; integrates education and work is the program translated as <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>&#8220;More work</B><B>,</B><B> </B><B>less recidivism&#8221;,</B></FONT> through which people deprived from their freedom can get access to training in trades, seeking to reduce their levels of recidivism through work opportunities. Thus, decent work is promoted, and training opportunities are offered through projects aiming at the responsible use of natural resources, boosting innovation and entrepreneur spirit, solidarity actions and social responsibility. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>Social Investment Programs represent what we consider proper involvement with the community where we are present, to generate a positive impact on the people who live in the vicinities. We broaden our look to integrate new approaches, such as development in culture, sports and personal and socio-economic aspects.&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>The <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>After-Match Program &#8211;&nbsp;Sports downtown</B></FONT>, is focused on girls and boys and on the practice of sports to relate, meet, generate and develop good values. Since this year, we are cooperating with DUBA (United Disabled People from Bah&#237;a), an institution that strives to include people with disabilities in the practice of several adapted sports, helping them to better themselves, increase their self-esteem, improve their self-appraisal and integrate them to society. &#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>Committed to safety in the community, we executed several communication actions within the framework of <FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Damage Prevention </B><B>P</B><B>rograms</B></FONT>, with the aim of preventing incidents at natural gas facilities and pipelines. Actions included training at town halls and mainly the &#8220;Door to Door&#8221; contact with neighbors who live in the areas next to the pipelines to provide information and ensure prevention.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>In June we presented the 12&#176; edition of the ESG Report, presenting GRI and SASB indicators, thus renewing once again our commitment to the Global Compact Principles. The 2022 ESG Report was acknowledged and prized at the Eikon&#160;Prizes and at the Social Ecumenic Forum.&#160;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>In December a strong storm hit the city of Bah&#237;a Blanca and brought severe damage throughout the whole city, destroying houses, schools, clubs, parks and squares. As a company that has been part of such community for over 30 years, we offered to make a contribution that allows the city to start repairing the material damage. We thus donated 64 tons of plate and sheet and we engaged in repair works at 6 schools, some of which we had already supported some years before and others that are close to our plants. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'>Lastly, it is worth mentioning that through our Foundation, in 2023 we supported different non-profit organization projects, fire brigades, hospitals, indigenous&#160;communities, etc. </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>53</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#242424;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman;color:#000000'><IMG src=tgs_48.jpg width=567 height=171 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B>6-c) GOVERNANCE</B></P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#00B0F0'><B>Corporate </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style=color:#4F81BD><B>Our ethical and transparent performance is one of our </B><B>main</B><B> </B><B>cornerstones,</B><B> </B></FONT>so we permanently strive to implement the best Corporate Governance practices, in line with market international trends and local and foreign regulations in force, to protect and maintain our investors and the community on the whole and to foster transparency in our actions. Over the last years, the legal framework protecting minority investors has considerably widened, particularly in the United States and several European countries. Since then, Corporate Governance has become of more and more relevance to ensure the transparency of the actions of any company listed in the stock exchange before the eyes of their investors and the community on the whole.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>TGS Governance and Auditing Structure </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Board of Directors</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>Our Board of Directors runs the operations of our Company. <B>tgs</B> Bylaws stipulate that our Board of Directors shall be constituted by a minimum of nine regular directors and nine alternate directors and a maximum of eleven regular directors and eleven alternate directors. By virtue of the Corporate Bylaws and the Argentine Corporate Law, the Board of Directors shall meet at least once every three months.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#000000'>On December 18, 2019, <B>tgs&#8217; </B>Board of Directors<B> </B>approved its internal rules of operations, which include guidelines for the diligent fulfillment of its duties. The Board of Directors currently in office was appointed by the Ordinary, Extraordinary and Special Shareholders Meetings dated April 19, 2023 (&#8220;Shareholders Meeting 2023&quot;) and its distribution of roles and responsibilities was also decided on the meeting held on the same date. It shall be in office until the Shareholders Meeting analyzes the financial statements as of December 31, 2023. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>On November 30, 2023, Messrs. Ignacio Amigorena and Lucas Gobbo, both Regular Members of the Board appointed by the 2023 Shareholders Meeting, communicated to the Company their resignation from their positions, in both cases on personal grounds, being effective as from </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>54</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>November 30, 2023. On December 1, 2023, Mr. &nbsp;Luis &#193;ngelo Vergara Guarnizo, Alternate Director communicated in a note dated November 30, 2023 his resignation from his position, also on personal grounds, effective as from the same date. On December 21, 2023 the Board of Directors of <B>tgs</B> accepted the mentioned resignations and Mrs. Cindy Signorini took office as Regular Director.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_49.jpg width=558 height=228 alt='Picture 8' title='Picture 8'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Members of the Management</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:7pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>The members of <FONT style=font-size:10pt><B>tgs</B></FONT> Management as of the date of the issuance of the present Annual Report are:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_50.jpg width=618 height=117 alt='Picture 9' title='Picture 9'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Audit Committee</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>The Audit Committee is our controlling body, set up in accordance with the provisions of Section 284 of Argentine Corporate Law. The Corporate Bylaws dictate the creation of an Audit Committee made up of three regular and three alternate members. In line with the Corporate Bylaws, two of the regular Statutory Auditors and their replacements must be appointed by Class &#8220;A&#8221; shares holders. The remaining Statutory Auditors and his/her replacements shall be appointed by the remaining holders of ordinary shares. The members of the Audit Committee are appointed at the Annual Ordinary Shareholders&#8217; Meeting and perform their roles for the period of one fiscal year, renewable for the same term.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_51.jpg width=401 height=59 alt='Picture 10' title='Picture 10'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>55</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Executive Committee</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>Its role is to ensure an optimal corporate management under the terms of Section 269 of the Argentine Corporate Law. In accordance with the Corporate Bylaws&#8217; provisions, it is made up of by 4 members, who were appointed by the Board of Directors held on April 19, 2023 for the term of one year until the Shareholders Meeting considers the financial statements as of December 31, 2023. The current members are Luis Alberto Fallo, Horacio Jorge Tom&#225;s Turri, Mar&#237;a Carolina Sigwald and Pablo Vi&#241;als Blake.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Audit Committee</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>In accordance with the Argentine Public Offering Law, companies that offer their shares to the public must constitute an Audit Committee, a body of at least three regular directors and same number of alternate directors, most of which shall act independently. The present Audit Committee is as follows:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_52.jpg width=382 height=98 alt='Picture 11' title='Picture 11'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Corporate Governance Policies</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>We permanently seek to develop and improve our internal Policies and Procedures, adhering to the best international practices, with the main objective of protecting and enhancing our Company&#8217;s value for all our shareholders.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>tgs</B> Corporate Governance practices are regulated by applicable Argentine law (particularly, the Argentine Corporate Law N&#186; 19,550 and its amendments), the standards issued by the CNV and other entities and our bylaws. Besides, as TGS is listed in the Securities and Exchange Commission (&#8220;SEC&#8221;) and its shares are listed in the New York Stock Exchange (&#8220;NYSE&#8221;), we are also subject to the standards and regulations of both the NYSE and the SEC.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>Our corporate governance goals are to ensure:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>Greater transparency in our performance, through the outlining of a culture of integrity and clarity in our business management.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>Adequate supervision through the continuous improvement of our internal control structure, leadership in compliance with regulations and adoption of policies addressed to achieve effective risk management.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>Proper allocations of accountability. Clear delineation of the scope of responsibility undertaken by the Board members and Managers of our Company, related to compliance with internal policies and regulations.&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>56</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>Among the measures implemented by <B>tgs</B> to ensure transparency in our actions, we can point out at &#8212;&nbsp;among others&#8212; the following components articulated into an Integrity Program, in accordance with the provisions of Law on Corporate Criminal Liability N&#176; 27,401:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Code of Conduct</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#1F497D'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>It governs the behavior of our Directors, Statutory Auditors, members of the Management and employees of both TGS and its controlled company. Furthermore, we seek to ensure acceptance of the Code by our suppliers, independent contractors, consultants and clients. The Code of Conduct outlines the indispensable ethics, transparency and honesty of people who work at the Company. Among other things, it declares the prohibition to forge or tamper with any information or documentation, behave in a manner that may generate damage to our Company or allow a person to obtain any personal benefit that goes against the interests of the Company. It also stipulates that those who release information to the market must have full knowledge of and abide by CNV and SEC regulations and are under the obligation to report without misrepresentations.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#0070C0'><FONT style=font-size:10pt;color:#00B0F0><B>Antifraud Policy</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>The Board of Directors of <B>tgs</B> has issued an Antifraud Policy, the aim of which is to outline roles and responsibilities and detect irregularities that may arise in connection with the activities performed by our Company. Said Policy establishes the mechanisms devised for the reception of infringements reports, such as the Ethics Line through which employees, clients, suppliers and other stakeholders may anonymously inform about any violation to the Code of Conduct.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Dividends Distribution Policy</B></P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>Through the Dividends Distribution Policy, the Board of Directors has set out guidelines that aim at maintaining an adequate balance between the amounts to be distributed as dividends and <B>tgs&#8217;</B> investment plans. Decisions shall be made seeking to ensure a reasonable management of business and balance in <B>tgs</B> financial condition.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>In accordance to the Transition Agreement 2023, if <B>tgs</B> should decide on the payment of dividends, it would have to obtain prior approval from ENARGAS and the Ministry of Economy.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Sustainability Policy</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>It provides the overall guidelines for conveying our Sustainability vision into the development of our strategies and action programs, focused on business continuity and promoting bonds with our stakeholders.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Best Stock Market Practices Policy </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>It has been adopted to ensure greater transparency in purchase or sale transactions or any kind of operations on negotiable securities listed in stock markets, preventing any person in our Company from obtaining any kind of advantage or economic benefit from the use of privileged information.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>57</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Policy of Related Parties&#8217; Approval and Disclosure </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>By means of this Policy, we have devised mechanisms to detect and disclose transactions with related parties, under the terms of Argentine Public Offering Law N&#176; 26,831. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Anti-Money Laundering Policy </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>This policy aims at preventing and detecting unusual or suspicious transactions, in accordance with the definitions of applicable law, accounting standards and international standards on this issue. It is applicable to all <B>tgs&#8217;</B> directors, statutory auditors and employees.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Policy of Recovery of Compensations Erroneously Awarded to Executive Officers in Financial Information</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>In 2023, we outlined this policy to meet the standards approved by the USA SEC for companies listed in NYSE and the National Association of Securities Dealers Automated Quotation (&#8220;NASDAQ&#8221;). The mentioned standards aimed at regulating an eventual recovery of compensations (bonds) erroneously awarded (&#8220;clawback&#8221;) to members of the Management Committee. Our policy was approved by the Board of Directors of <B>tgs</B> on November 6, 2023. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'><FONT style='font:10pt Verdana;color:#00B0F0'><B>Cybersecurity Policy</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>The SEC has issued new requirements that seek to regulate the information that companies disclose regarding material cybersecurity incidents as from December 18, 2023. On account of said requirements, at the Board of Directors Meeting held on November 6, 2023 we approved amendments to the Cybersecurity Policy and the Systems Emergency Plan. The policies seek not only to comply with the current regulations on the topic but also to provide transparency in the disclosure and manage cybersecurity incidents more efficiently. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>The Board of Directors expressly delegates in the Executive Committee the assessment of the incident materiality and if applicable the reporting of the incident. In order to determine cybersecurity incidents materiality, the following impacts shall be assessed: economic-financial or business, legal or regulatory, company reputation, people safety, quality and rendering of services, environment and /or stakeholders. We also conducted the annual revision of the cybersecurity risks matrix.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>tgs Risk Management</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>One of the pillars of <B>tgs</B> strategic planning is the permanent assessment of the environment, to guarantee the adaptation of the Company to an uncertain context in continuous transformation. Risk management is, in particular, one of the essential tools to fulfil the need to adapt to the environment.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>58</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'><B>tgs</B> has a Global Risk Management Policy approved by the Board of Directors of the Company. Said Policy establishes a continuous and systematic risk management that ensures treatment of them with effectiveness and efficiency, so as to guarantee an adequate follow-up of all those events that may negatively affect the objectives of the Company. Thus, this Policy develops a framework and a procedure for risk management in the different processes and activities of the Company, outlining criteria for identification, analysis, risk assessment as well as treatment and follow-up of same. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>The Policy establishes that the ultimate responsibility falls upon the members of the Management, whereas the responsibility for the implementation, maintenance and improvements corresponds to the Planning and Risk Department.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>The Company Management, based on its sustained commitment with risk management, seeks to include it in the organizational culture, integrating it to the usual management of each process.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>The Audit Committee performs a regular monitoring of the company risk management, following up the results of the different performed assessments, the issuance of internal regulations, the advance status of undertaken plans and the several new developments on the matter.</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_53.jpg width=558 height=205 alt='Imagen 1539762654' title='Imagen 1539762654'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>Among the risks managed by the Company, we can identify facilities&#8217; risks, projects&#8217; risks, environmental hazards and aspects, fraud risk, risk of corruption with the public sector, cyber security risks and strategic risks. The latter include all those risks whose occurrence may prevent the fulfillment of the Company strategic objectives and are monitored in a direct and permanent manner by the Management, to guarantee an adequate response through timely decision-making. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>Thus, the usual process of strategic risk management contemplates that once certain risk has been identified, the existent controls and mitigating factors must be surveyed, weighed and mapped based on impact and likelihood factors. We also determine the &#8220;risk appetite&#8221; or acceptable level so that &#8212;according to the gap between such risk level and the performed assessment&#8212;, we can establish the need to implement specific action plans, which are then followed-up to determine their advance status and effectiveness in light of the pursued objectives.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>59</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'><B>tgs</B> investments plan is based on risk priority, contemplating among others risks of different natures: operative, environmental, safety, impact on community, non-compliance with requirements, cybersecurity.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>In 2023, we kept consolidating processes related to our corporate risk management, seeking to engage all the required areas in order to build a true risk management culture. In this direction, in November 2023, the external Audit conducted to the Integrated Management System under standards ISO 9001:2015, ISO 14001:2015 and ISO 45001:2018, concluded that <B>tgs</B> has a Strategic Quality Plan that contemplates the management of Hazards, Aspects and Risks as goal, which is measurable, controlled, communicated and updated on a regular basis. With reference to thinking based on risk, the activity showed the gathering of risks and opportunities applied to the different organization processes, along with their assessment and linking to the budget process.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>Finally, after in 2022 we had defined risk management as a priority in the design of the ESG Strategic Plan, in 2023 we started mapping the main ESG strategic risks. We will deepen this process in 2024, through the analysis of risks and opportunities on ESG management and their impact on our sustainability strategy.</P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#0070C0'><B>7. Financial Assets</B><A name=_Hlk151463164 /></P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Shareholding Structure as of December 31, 2023 </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><B>tgs</B> shares are listed in the Argentine Stock Exchanges and Markets (&#8220;BYMA&#8221;, according to its initials in Spanish) in the domestic market and in the form of American Depositary Receipts (&#8220;ADR&#8221;) &#8212;representing 5 shares each&#8212; in the NYSE, which are registered in the SEC and have been listed since November 1994.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><B>tgs</B> has 794,495,283 issued shares, out of which 752,761,058 shares are in circulation. As of December 31, 2023, our treasury stock amounts to 41,734,225 of our own shares, representing 5.25% of our common stock.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Our main shareholders are:</P>

<P align=justify style='font:7pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:7pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'>(1)</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Included as &#8220;Other financial assets at amortized cost, non-current.&#8221;&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>Our<FONT style=color:#0070C0><B> </B></FONT>indebtedness&#8217;<FONT style=color:#0070C0><B> </B></FONT>maturities profile is as follows:</P>
<P style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-left:28.35pt'><IMG src=tgs_56.jpg width=444 height=453 alt='Imagen 1539762659' title='Imagen 1539762659'>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>63</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#00B0F0'><B>Financial Overview throughout the year 2023</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Our main liquidity sources were our operations cash flows, the sale of our temporary investments and loans. Our operations cash flows have been affected over the last years by a deficient natural gas transportation tariff adjustment, particularly in view of the rocketing inflation that struck our economy (for further information see &#8220;Tariff Overview&#8221; of the present annual report). </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-2.15pt'>As in former years, to mitigate the impact of the fall of international reserves, during 2023 the BCRA adopted measures that restricted the normal operations of the Foreign Exchange Market. These restrictions did not affect the payment of the 2018 Bonds interests, but they did affect payments of imports, which was translated in payment delays and taking of loans. &nbsp;For further information, see <B><I>&#8220;2. Macroeconomic Scenario - 2.1. Argentine Context&#8221;.</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Therefore, in 2023, we were compelled to obtain further financing for the amount of US$ 72 million and EUR 0.7 million (whereas same year we cancelled US$ 22 million and EUR 1 million). As of December 31, 2023, our short-term indebtedness with financial entities was at US$ 60 million.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>In 2023, the United States dollar exchange rate went up by 356%, from $ 177.16 as of December 31, 2022 to $ 808.45 as of December 31, 2023. On the other hand, prices evolution measured by the CPI rose by 211.4% year-over-year.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>Net cash flows applied to investment activities amounted to Ps. Ps. 206,428 million, mainly attributable to higher payments related to the acquisition of PPE given our commitment to the development of Vaca Muerta and financial collocations not considered as funds in accordance with the Accounting Standards IFRS.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>Our financial strategy was influenced by the existing restrictions and the macroeconomic conditions that shaped the year 2023. If necessary, <B>tgs</B> has access to short-term financial loans in the domestic market and to Argentine capital markets. For further information, see <B>&#8220;<I>Financial Sources&#8221;.</I></B></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>64</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In order to preserve the value of dollars in cash, they are invested in low risk and high liquidity financial assets offered by financial institutions with high credit score and in private bonds of first-class Argentine companies. In 2023, we prioritized <B><I>dollar-linked</I></B> fund collocations to mitigate the exchange rate risk in our net liabilities in dollars and the impact of inflation on availabilities denominated in Argentine pesos.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt;color:#000000'>The table below presents our fund applications as of December 31, 2023:</P>

</TD><TD valign=bottom bgcolor=#2F75B5 style=width:54.55pt;padding-left:3.5pt;padding-right:3.5pt><P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#7F7F7F'><B>12/31/2023</B></P>
</TD></TR>
<TR style=height:12.75pt><TD valign=bottom style='width:271.45pt;padding-left:3.5pt;padding-right:3.5pt;border-top:0.5pt solid #000000;border-bottom:0.5pt solid #000000'><P style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#000000'>Cash and Banks</P>

</TD><TD valign=bottom bgcolor=#BDD7EE style='width:54.55pt;padding-left:3.5pt;padding-right:3.5pt;border-bottom:3px double #000000'><P align=right style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>430,056 </B></P>
</TD></TR>
</TABLE>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><B>tgs</B> financial position is still solid, due to a strong cash generation, a relatively low level of indebtedness and revenues denominated in United States dollars in the segments of Liquids Production and Commercialization and Midstream. However, the situations described above &#8212;&nbsp;and the impact of the main macroeconomic variables on the international financial markets &#8212;may negatively affect our financial condition and that of our clients&#8217;, which might derive into the deterioration of our capacity to generate cash to meet our financial obligations and capital expenditures. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>As mentioned above, on October 11, 2023, the CNV approved the expansion of the bonds program to a maximum amount of US$ 2,000 million and the extension of its maturity up to January 3, 2029, which was essential for the refinancing of the 2018 Bonds expiring on May 2025 and the financing of the investment projects that we are currently analyzing both in the domestic and international market &#8212;as long as the financing conditions are adequate&#8212;.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In the short term, the most significant factors that impact on cash flows generation are: (i) international liquid prices, (ii) liquids production levels (iii) price of natural gas used as PTR, (iv) dollar exchange rate variations, (v) dollar exchange rate and (vi) tariffs of the regulated natural gas transportation segment.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>To minimize the negative impact of the exchange rate on our financial indebtedness, <B>tgs</B> adopted a conservative approach. Hence, as of December 31, 2023, 85% of our funds collocations overseas are denominated in United States dollars or adjusted by said variable.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>65</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:0pt;margin-right:-7.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>8. ANALYSIS OF FINANCIAL CONDITION AND RESULTS OF OPERATIONS FOR THE YEAR ENDED DECEMBER 31, 2023</B><FONT style=font-family:Arial><B> </B></FONT><B><SUP>(1)</SUP> </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The following discussion of the financial condition and results of operations of the Company should be read in conjunction with the Company's consolidated financial statements as of December 31, 2023 and 2022, and for the years ended December 31, 2023 and 2022. These consolidated financial statements have been prepared in accordance with International Financial Reporting Standards (&#8220;IFRS&#8221;), issued by the International Accounting Standards Board (&#8220;IASB&#8221;) adopted by the Comisi&#243;n Nacional de Valores (&quot;CNV&quot;) through the provisions of Title IV, Chapter I, Section I, Article 1 &#8211;&nbsp;B.1 of the Rules of the CNV (&quot;New Text 2013&quot; or &quot;NT 2013&quot;) and considering Resolutions No. 1,660 and No. 1,903 issued by the ENARGAS.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>These consolidated financial statements were jointly audited by Price Waterhouse &amp; Co. S.R.L., and Pistrelli, Henry Martin y Asociados S.R.L.</P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Effects of inflation</B></P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>On December 3, 2018, Law No. 27,468 was enacted, sanctioned on November 15, 2018 by the National Argentine Congress. Among other measures, this law abolishes Presidential Decree No. 1,269/02 &#8212;amended by Presidential Decree No. 664/2003 &#8212;&nbsp;through which the controlling entities (among them the CNV) had been instructed not to accept inflation adjusted financial statements. On December 26, 2018, the CNV issued Resolution No. 777/2018, by virtue of which it instructs companies that offer their shares to the public to apply the financial statements restatement method to a stable currency in line with the provisions of IAS 29 &#8220;Financial Reporting in Hyperinflationary Economies&#8221;.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In accordance with such standards, the restatement of financial statements was restarted as from July 1, 2018. In line with the restatement method, non-currency assets and liabilities are restated by an overall price index issued by the Argentine Federation of Professional Councils in Economic Sciences (&#8220;Argentine Federation&#8221;) since their acquisition date or last inflation adjustment (March 1, 2003).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Likewise, comparative information included in the financial statements has also been restated, but this fact has not modified the decisions based on the financial information corresponding to those fiscal years.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>For further information, see &#8220;Note 4. Significant Accounting Policies &#8211;&nbsp;d) Restatement to constant currency &#8211;&nbsp;Comparative Information&#8221; to the consolidated financial statements as of December 31, 2023.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#7F7F7F'><SUP>(1)</SUP> Not covered by Independent Auditors&#8217; Report except for items 8.d., 8.e., 8.f. and 8.h. </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:10pt Times New Roman;margin:0'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>66</FONT></P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
</DIV>
<DIV style=margin-left:85.05pt;width:432.35pt><HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;color:#0070C0'><B>8.a. Results of operations</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The following table presents a summary of the consolidated results of operations for the years ended December 31, 2023 (&#8220;FY2023&#8221; or &#8220;2023&#8221;) and December 31, 2022 (&#8220;FY2022&#8221; or &#8220;2022&#8221;):</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#000000'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_57.jpg width=540 height=219 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Activities of the Company in FY2023 and FY2022</B></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Revenues</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Total revenues for FY2023 decreased by Ps. 59,534 million compared to FY2022, mainly as a result of the decrease in revenues from the Production and Commercialization of Liquids and Natural Gas Transportation business segment, an effect that was partially offset by the increase in revenues from the Midstream business segment.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_58.jpg width=540 height=186 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><FONT style='border-bottom:1px solid #0070C0'><B>Natural Gas Transportation</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>During FY2023 revenues from the Natural Gas Transportation business segment accounted for 22% of tgs total revenues (25% for FY2022). 81% of the total revenues corresponded to firm contracted capacity services for both years.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Revenues from the Natural Gas Transportation segment during FY2023 reached Ps. 99,052 million (Ps. 126,567 million in FY2022). The Ps. 27,515 million decrease is due to the lack of tariff adjustment and the negative impact that the evolution of inflation has on tariffs.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>69</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>On April 27, 2023, ENARGAS by means of Resolution No. 186/2023 approved the tariff charts contemplating a tariff increase of 95%. On April 29, 2023, by means of Decree No. 250/2023, the proposed addendum to the transitory renegotiation agreement (the &quot;2023 Transitory Agreement&quot;) approved by the Board of Directors of tgs was ratified, allowing the entry into force of the tariff charts.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Previously, effective March 1, 2022, we received a nominal increase of 60%.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The 2023 Transitory Agreement additionally allows, among other matters and with the prior authorization of the Ministry of Economy, tgs to pay dividends (previously restricted).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>For more information regarding the Company's tariff situation, see &quot;4. Businesses in 2023. Natural Gas Transportation&quot;, of this Annual Report.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><FONT style='border-bottom:1px solid #0070C0'><B>Liquids Production and Commercialization </B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Revenues from the Liquids Production and Commercialization segment amounted to Ps. 265,413 million in 2023 (Ps. 59,120 million lower than in 2022). This negative effect was mainly due to the fall in international prices and the negative variation in the real foreign exchange rate. These effects were partially offset by the improvement in the price of ethane.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Total volumes sold were practically similar in FY 2023 and 2022, registering an increase in the volumes of ethane sold, an effect that was partially offset by the lower tons shipped, both in the domestic and foreign markets, of propane and butane.</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_59.jpg width=540 height=133 alt='Picture 4' title='Picture 4'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>For more information regarding the evolution of the businesses in this business segment during 2023, see &#8220;4. Businesses in 2023. Production and Commercialization of Liquids&#8221;, of this Annual Report.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><FONT style='border-bottom:1px solid #0070C0'><B>Midstream and Telecommunications</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Midstream and telecommunications revenues increased by Ps. 27,100 million in 2023 compared to 2022. This increase is due to higher revenues corresponding to natural gas transportation and conditioning services in Vaca Muerta. These effects were partially offset by the negative variation in the real foreign exchange rate.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>For more information regarding the evolution of the businesses in this business segment during 2023, see &#8220;4. Businesses in 2023. Midstream and Telecommunications&#8221;, of this Annual Report.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>70</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Cost of sales and administrative and selling expenses</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Cost of sales, administrative and selling expenses corresponding to FY2023 decreased by Ps. 13,162 million, 3.8% compared to the same period of the previous year. This variation is mainly due to the decrease in the cost of natural gas processed at the Cerri Complex (mainly due to a decrease in the price, measured in constant Argentine pesos), and taxes, fees and contributions (mainly due to a decrease in tax on exports, a drop in prices and turnover tax). These effects were partially offset by an increase in labor costs.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The following table shows the main components of operating costs, administrative and selling expenses and their main variations for FY2023 and FY2022:</P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Other operating results recorded a negative variation of Ps. 521 million mainly due to the recovery of provisions carried out in 2022.</P>

<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>Net financial results</B></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In FY2023, financial results showed a negative variation of Ps. 62,941 million with respect to FY2022. The breakdown of net financial results is as follows:<A name=OLE_LINK10 /></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_61.jpg width=549 height=252 alt='Picture 2' title='Picture 2'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>This negative variation is mainly due to the higher negative net foreign exchange difference after the variation in the exchange rate registered in mid-December 2023.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>71</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The selling peso/US dollar ended exchange rate closed at a value of Ps. 808.45 per US dollar as of December 31, 2023, representing an increase of 356% (or Ps. 631.29 per US dollar) compared to the exchange rate observed at the closing of FY2022. As of December 31, 2022, said rate had increased by 72% (or $74.44 for each US dollar) regarding its quote at the closing of the FY2021. The net liability position in US dollars of <B>tgs</B> has been reduced in FY2023.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Likewise, as a consequence of the acceleration of inflation and the net active monetary position, the net monetary position recorded a loss in FY2023, as opposed to the profit recorded during FY2022.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The effects mentioned above were partially offset by the positive variation in results generated by financial assets.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-left:18pt;color:#0070C0'><B>8.b. Liquidity </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The Company&#8217;s primary sources and application of funds during FY2023 and FY2022 were the following:</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_62.jpg width=540 height=122 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Cash Flows provided by operating activities increased by Ps. 79,289 million, mainly due to lower income tax payments and a lower variation in working capital. </P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_63.jpg width=540 height=103 alt='Picture 2' title='Picture 2'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
