<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Cash flows used in investing activities increased by Ps. 81,194 million mainly driven by higher acquisitions of capital assets within the framework of Midstream projects and financial assets not considered funds according to Accounting Standards IFRS. </P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_64.jpg width=540 height=56 alt='Picture 3' title='Picture 3'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>

<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>72</P>

<P align=justify style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:3pt;margin-left:21.3pt;color:#0070C0'><B>8.c. Fourth quarter 2023 (&#8220;4Q2023&#8221;) vs Fourth quarter 2022 (&#8220;4Q2022&#8221;)</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The following table presents a summary of the consolidated results of operations for 4Q2023 and 4Q2022:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_66.jpg width=540 height=241 alt='Picture 5' title='Picture 5'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>During 4Q2023, the Company obtained comprehensive loss of Ps. 23,461 million, compared to comprehensive income of Ps. 16,697 million obtained in the same period of 2022.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Total revenues for 4Q2023 decreased Ps. 20,882 million compared to the same period of the previous year.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Revenues from the Natural Gas Transportation segment in 4Q2023 decreased by Ps. 6,493 million, compared to 4Q2022.  This negative variation was due to the fact that the tariff increases received effective as of March 1, 2022, of 60% nominal and as of April 29, 2023 of 95% nominal did not manage to offset the negative effect that the variation in the Index Consumer Price Index (&#8220;CPI&#8221;) had on inflation-adjusted revenues.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>For the Liquids Production and Commercialization segment, revenues decreased by Ps. 26,124 million in 4Q2023, mainly as a consequence of the fall in international reference prices, the lower volumes dispatched and the decrease in the exchange rate measured in homogeneous currency. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Regarding the total volumes dispatched from the Cerri Complex, they decreased by 19% or 65,057 tons, highlighting the decrease in exports and the drop in the ethane sold due to the impossibility of PBB Polisur S.R.L. to take the product during the second half of December.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>73</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The breakdown of volumes dispatched by market and product and revenues by market is included below:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_67.jpg width=540 height=143 alt='Picture 6' title='Picture 6'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The Midstream segment reported an increase of Ps. 11,735 million, mainly explained by higher natural gas transportation and conditioning services in Vaca Muerta and midstream. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>Cost of sales and administrative and commercialization expenses for 4Q2023 amounted to Ps. 79,554 million (compared to Ps. 92,791 million in 4Q2022), which represented a decrease of Ps. 13,237 million. This variation is mainly due to lower costs of natural gas processed at the Cerri Complex (lower price measured in homogeneous currency and lower consumption due to a decrease in production). This effect was partially offset by higher labor costs and depreciation.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>The following table shows the main components of operating costs, administrative and commercialization expenses and their main variations for 4Q2023 and 4Q2022:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_68.jpg width=549 height=120 alt='Picture 2' title='Picture 2'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>In 4Q2023, financial results suffered a negative variation of Ps. 63,673 million, compared to those registered in the 2022 period. This variation is mainly due to the higher negative exchange difference and the loss on monetary position, effect of the net active monetary position. These effects were partially offset by the higher results obtained from the change in the fair value of financial assets.</P>

<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:11pt Verdana;margin-top:12pt;margin-bottom:3pt;text-indent:-18pt;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-24.75pt;color:#0070C0'><B>9. Board of Directors&#8217; Proposal regarding the application of Results</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Verdana;margin:0'><A name=OLE_LINK12 />&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>The chart below provides a breakdown of our retained earnings as of December 31, 2023 and our proposal for its distribution. Our proposal includes, after the reversal of the Balance of the former &#8220;Reserve for Future Investments, Buyback of our Own Shares and/or Payment of Dividends&#8221; constituted by tgs Ordinary Shareholders Meeting dated April 19, 2023, the creation of a further &#8220;Reserve for Future Investments, Buyback of our Own Shares and/or Payment of Dividends&#8221;. In light of our new businesses outlook and the challenges we are facing in this oncoming fiscal year, it might be destined to:  (i) future investmens related to the projects and businesses outlined in the Company&#8217;s business plan and and/or (ii) the payment of future dividends subject to the financial condition of the Company and the macroenconomic variables and/or (iii) the buyback of shares within the plan approved by the Board of Directors of the Company. &nbsp;The mentioned reserve might be fully or partially distributed by the Board of Directors &#8212;as long as its use is not required in accordance with the provisions of subsections c) and e) of item 11, section 3, Chapter III Title IV of the CNV Standards (N.T. 2013), which with reference to treasury stock, based on the destination, opportunity and amount determined by the Board and approved by the Shareholders Meeting , in line with a reasonable management &nbsp;criteria as outlined by applicable regulations and considering for such purposes, the economic &nbsp;financial condition of the Company and its future outlook.<FONT style='font-family:Book Antiqua'> &#160;</FONT></P>

<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><B>1.</B></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD><B>BUSINESS DESCRIPTION</B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Business Overview</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;text-indent:14.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Transportadora de Gas del Sur S.A. (&#8220;<B>tgs</B>&#8221; or the &#8220;Company&#8221;) is one of the companies created as a result of the privatization of Gas del Estado S.E. (&#8220;GdE&#8221;). <B>tgs</B> commenced operations on December 28, 1992 and it is mainly engaged in the Transportation of Natural Gas, and Production and Commercialization of natural gas Liquids (&#8220;Liquids&#8221;). TGS&#8217;s pipeline system connects major natural gas fields in southern and western Argentina with natural gas distributors and industries in those areas and in the greater Buenos Aires area. The natural gas transportation license to operate this system was exclusively granted to <B>tgs</B> for a period of thirty-five years (&#8220;the License&#8221;). TGS is entitled to a one-time extension of ten years provided that it has essentially met the obligations imposed by the License and by <I>Ente Nacional Regulador del Gas</I> (National Gas Regulatory Body or &#8220;ENARGAS&#8221;). The General Cerri Gas Processing Complex (the &#8220;Cerri Complex&#8221;), where TGS processes natural gas to obtain liquids, was transferred from GdE along with the gas transmission assets. <B>tgs</B> also provides midstream services, which mainly consist of gas treatment, removal of impurities from the natural gas stream, gas compression, wellhead gas gathering and pipeline construction, operation and maintenance services. In addition, telecommunications services are provided through the subsidiary Telcosur S.A. (&#8220;Telcosur&#8221;). These services consist of data transmission services through a network of terrestrial and digital radio relay.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Subsequently, the corporate purpose of the Company was modified to incorporate the development of complementary activities, incidental, linked and / or derived from natural gas transportation, such as the generation and commercialization of electric power and the provision of other services for the hydrocarbon sector in general.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Major Shareholders</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'><B>tgs&#180; </B>controlling shareholder Compa&#241;&#237;a de Inversiones de Energ&#237;a S.A. (&#8220;CIESA&#8221;), holds 51% of the common stock of the company, the National Social Security Administration (&#8220;ANSES&#8221;) holds 24% and the remaining 25% is held by the investing public (<B>tgs</B> has 5.25% of the shares in the portfolio).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>CIESA is under joint control of: Pampa Energ&#237;a S.A. (&#8220;Pampa Energ&#237;a&#8221;) with 50% and Grupo Inversor Petroqu&#237;mica S.L. (&#8220;GIP&#8221;) and PCT L.L.C. with the remaining 50%. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The following table shows the organizational structure, shareholders and related parties of <B>tgs</B> as of December 31, 2023:</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>5</P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Economic context</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'>General framework</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The Company operates in a complex economic context whose main variables have recently had strong volatility as a result of political and economic events at the national level.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>2023 was a year in which the Argentine economy went through high levels of volatility and uncertainty. On the one hand, at the beginning of the year there was a record drought that implied a drop of approximately US$ 20,000 million in the liquidation of foreign currency by the agro-export sector, negatively impacting not only the accounts of the Argentina Central Bank (&#8220;BCRA&#8221;) but also in tax collection. Additionally, as in any election year, political uncertainty has also generated deep macroeconomic imbalances.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>This situation led the administration of former President Alberto Fern&#225;ndez to adopt various measures including, among others: (i) the issuance of new exchange restrictions, (ii) the establishment of the Tax for an Inclusive and Solidarity Argentina (&#8220;PAIS&#8221; according to its initials in Spanish), applicable to the acquisitions by Argentine residents of foreign currency intended for the purchase of certain goods and services from abroad, and other taxes in order to reduce the fiscal deficit and (iii) the freezing of certain internal prices.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The low level of reserves in the BCRA has led the national government to restrict access to the Free and Single Exchange Market (&#8220;MULC&#8221; according to its initials in Spanish) in order to contain the demand for dollars and the flight of foreign currency, which means for the </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>6</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P align=right style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-21.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>English translation of the original prepared in Spanish for publication in Argentina</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>(Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>_____________________________________________________________________________________________________</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>company a restriction when operating their businesses. These restrictions involved the implementation of an import payment control system subject to a series of limits determined by the Ministry of Commerce and the Federal Administration of Public Revenues (&#8220;AFIP&#8221; according to its initials in Spanish).</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>On October 22, 2023, the general elections were held in Argentina. The result yielded a new conformation of Congress as of December 10, 2023 (in a minority for the president-elect). On November 19, 2023, the runoff election was held, in which the opposition candidate for &#8220;La Libertad Avanza&#8221; political party, Javier Milei, was elected president.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>After taking office, the current administration launched a package of emergency measures aimed at relaxing controls and deregulating the economy, with the main objective of reducing the fiscal deficit. Among the main measures adopted we can mention:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Announcement of an economic plan centered on the review of fiscal, exchange and monetary policy in which, among other issues: (i) a sharp cut in public spending is shown along with an increase in certain taxes, (ii) the price of the US dollar was raised against the Argentine peso from $/US$ 350 to $/US$ 800 and a monthly devaluation of 2%, (iii) reduction of energy and transportation subsidies and (iv) limits the monetary issue and modifies the Treasury financing program in order to reduce the BCRA's liabilities.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:50.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Issuance of the Decree of Necessity and Urgency (DNU) No. 70/2023 - &#8220;Bases for the Reconstruction of the Argentine Economy&#8221; that declares the public emergency in economic, financial, fiscal, administrative, pension, tariff, health and social matters until on December 31, 2025. This decree promotes economic deregulation and the insertion of Argentina in world trade through the adoption of international standards for the trade of goods and services.&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>On December 27, 2023, the National Executive Branch sent the Draft Law of Bases and Starting Points for the Freedom of Argentines (the &#8220;Omnibus Law&#8221;) for consideration by the National Congress. On February 3, 2024, this law obtained half a sanction from the Chamber of Deputies. On February 6, 2024, the government was forced to return the Omnibus Law to committees in the middle of the article-by-article vote on the law, when seven of the first thirteen points put up for consideration did not gather the necessary number of votes. for your approval. In this way, the bill will return to committees with its original wording and must be debated from the beginning again. As of the date of issuance of these Consolidated Financial Statements, it is not possible to predict the impact that the Omnibus Law may have on our financial situation, equity and results of operations.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Among the main macroeconomic variables of 2023, we can mention:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>GDP drop in the third quarter of 2023 (latest information available) by 0.8%;&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>accumulated increase of 211.4% in the CPI.&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>increase to 40.2% in the poverty rate.&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>increase in the official exchange rate (BNA selling rate) to $808.45 per US dollar (with the gap with the &#8220;counted with settlement&#8221; dollar during much of the year being approximately 125% and as of December 31, 2023, 20%).&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>A fiscal deficit equivalent to 3% of GDP.&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>7</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P align=right style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-21.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>English translation of the original prepared in Spanish for publication in Argentina</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>(Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>_____________________________________________________________________________________________________</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Restrictions on the MULC</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>BCRA regulations</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The low level of reserves in the BCRA has led the national government to maintain very restrictive exchange controls for the purchase of foreign currency in the MULC for much of the year.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>In order to regulate access to the MULC in order to contain the demand for dollars, the BCRA established a series of requirements that require the request for prior authorization from the BCRA for certain transactions, among them are the following:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Payment of dividends to non-residents;&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Payment of imports of certain goods from abroad or cancellation of debts arising from their importation, except for some exceptions expressly provided for in the applicable regulations;&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>The formation of external assets; and&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>The payment of financial loans to non-residents.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>All of these measures have been intensifying since April 2020, including measures linked to the trading of stock assets by companies.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Effective as of December 13, 2023, the BCRA issued Communication &#8220;A&#8221; 7917 in which a series of modifications are established for the purposes of access to the MULC. Between them:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>The payment systems for imports of goods and services (known as &#8220;SIRA&#8221; and &#8220;SIRASE&#8221; declarations) and the need to validate transactions in the &#8220;Single Foreign Trade Current Account&#8221; computer system are eliminated.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>For new debts for imports, access to the MULC may be granted, without the need for prior approval from the BCRA, to make deferred payments for new imports of goods with customs entry registration according to the schedule established by type of goods.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><A name=_Hlk161753960 /><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Regarding the debt prior to December 12, 2023, the AFIP and the BCRA regulated the use of &#8220;BOPREAL&#8221; (acronym in Spanish for Bond for the Reconstruction of a Free Argentina). The purpose of this bond is to pay off the importers' debts, for which they had to previously register in a registry enabled for such purpose at the AFIP.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><FONT style=color:#000000>For more information about Argentina's exchange rate policies you can visit the Central Bank website: </FONT><FONT style='color:#0000FF;border-bottom:1px solid #0000FF'>www.bcra.gov.ar</FONT>.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Tax regulations</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Law No. 27,541 on Social Solidarity and Productive Reactivation in the Framework of the Public Emergency (B.O. 12/23/2019) and its modifications created the so-called PAIS Tax, which established a tax applicable to acquisitions by Argentine residents of currency foreign currency intended for the purchase of certain goods and services from abroad.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>On July 24, 2023, Decree No. 377/23 was issued, later modified by Decree No. 29/2023 (effective as of December 13, 2023), by which it extends the PAIS Tax to the following operations:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>The acquisition abroad of personal, cultural and recreational services (does not include educational teaching). 30% rate.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Importation of luxury goods. 30% rate.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>The acquisition abroad of services included in the decree (technical, legal, accounting, managerial services, business services in general, charges for the use of intellectual property, advertising, engineering, audiovisual services, among others). 25% rate.&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>8</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P align=right style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-21.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>English translation of the original prepared in Spanish for publication in Argentina</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>(Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>_____________________________________________________________________________________________________</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>The acquisition abroad of freight services and other transportation services for import or export operations of goods. Rate of 17.5% (previously 7.5%).&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>The import of merchandise, with the exception of goods linked to energy generation whose scope has not yet been regulated. Rate of 17.5% (previously 7.5%).&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Said decree was regulated on July 25, 2023 by General Resolution No. 5,393/23 of the AFIP in which, among other issues, the entry of a payment on account of the PAIS Tax is provided for in the case of acquisition of goods from the 95% of the total final tax to be paid. Said payment on account must be entered by the importer at the time of officialization of the import destination. The balance for the remaining 5% PAIS tax must be paid at the time of accessing the MULC at the time of payment abroad, with the intervening bank acting as collection and settlement agent.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Export settlement</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>On October 3, 2023, the Ministry of Energy issued Resolution No. 808/2023, through which exports of commercialized products are included within the Export Increase Program created by Decree No. 576/22. by the Society.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>According to said program, the Company must enter 75% of the value of its exports in foreign currency into the country. The remaining 25% may be settled in pesos through the purchase of negotiable securities. To qualify for the scheme, exports must be settled between October 2 and October 20, 2023, and the effective export date must not be later than November 30, 2023.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Subsequently, through Decree No. 28/2023, the proportion mentioned above was modified, and currently 80% of the value of its exports must be imported into the country in foreign currency. The remaining 20% <FONT style=font-family:Arial>&#8203;&#8203;</FONT>may be settled in pesos through the purchase of negotiable securities, the validity of the rule not being specified.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The context of volatility and uncertainty continues as of the date of issuance of these consolidated financial statements. Government measures already implemented, or those that may be implemented in the future, could eventually affect the results of operations, financial and equity situation of the Company.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Likewise, the Company's management permanently monitors the evolution of the variables that affect its business, to define its course of action and identify the potential impacts on its equity and financial situation. The reforms proposed by the new government began their legislative discussion process. It is not possible to predict at this time its evolution or new measures that could be announced. Likewise, the Company cannot guarantee that the aforementioned macroeconomic difficulties or the adoption of new measures by the Argentine Government to control inflation may affect its operations and financial situation.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The Company's financial statements must be read in light of these circumstances.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>9</P>

(&#8220;Telcosur&#8221;) and CTG Energ&#237;a S.A.U. (&#8220;CTG&#8221;) its consolidated subsidiaries, which are jointly referred to as &#8220;<B>tgs</B>&#8221; or &#8220;the Company&#8221;.</P>

<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><B>3.</B></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD><B>BASIS OF PRESENTATION</B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>These consolidated financial statements have been prepared in accordance with International</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Financial Reporting Standards issued by the International Accounting Standards Board (&#8220;IASB&#8221;) and the International Financial Interpretations Committee (&#8220;IFRIC&#8221;), jointly the Accounting Standards&#8220;IFRS&#8221;.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The CNV, in Title IV, Chapter III, Article 1 of the Rules has provided that listed companies must submit their consolidated financial statements by applying Technical Resolution No. 26 of the Argentine Federation of Professional Councils of Economic Sciences (&quot;FACPCE&quot;), which adopts the International Financial Reporting Standards (&quot;IFRS&quot;) issued by the IASB, its amendments and circulars for the adoption of Accounting Standards IFRS that the FACPCE dictates in accordance with the provisions of that Technical Resolution.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The preparation of the consolidated financial statements in conformity with Accounting Standards IFRS requires management to make accounting estimates and assumptions that affect the reported amounts of assets and liabilities and the disclosure of contingent assets and liabilities at the date of the consolidated financial statements, as well as the reported amounts of revenues and expenses during the reporting fiscal year. Estimates are used when accounting for the allowance for doubtful accounts, income taxes, provisions for legal claims and others, depreciations and recoverable value of assets. Actual results could be significantly different from such estimates.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The presentation in the statement of financial position distinguishes between current and non-current assets and liabilities. The assets and liabilities are those expected to be realized or settled within twelve months after the end of the reporting period under review, and those held for sale. The fiscal year begins on January 1 and ends on December 31 of each year. The economic and financial results are presented on a fiscal year basis. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The consolidated financial statements are stated in thousands of Argentine pesos (&#8220;Ps.&#8221;) which is the functional currency of the Company, unless otherwise stated. For further information, see Note 4.c).</P>

<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C1'><B>4.a) New accounting standards</B></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C1'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C1'><B>4.a 1) New standards and interpretations issued by the IASB effective for the years</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C1><B>beginning on or after January 1, 202</B><B>3</B><B> adopted by the Company</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The new accounting standards, amendments and interpretations issued by the IASB that became effective as of January 1, 2023 and that have not had an impact on the Company&#8217;s consolidated financial statements are the following:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C1'><B>Classification of debt as current and non-current (amendment to IAS 1)</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C1'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt'>On January 23, 2020, the IASB issued the classification of debt as current and non-current, which amends IAS 1 Presentation of Financial Statements. These amendments affect the requirements of IAS 1 for the presentation of debt.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:14.2pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt'>Specifically, it clarifies the criteria for classifying debt as non-current. The date of application of the amendment was set for fiscal years beginning on or after January 1, 2023, with retroactive application. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt'>This amendment did not have a significant impact on the Company&#180;s consolidated financial statements.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C1'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C1'><B>IAS 12: &#8220;Income tax&#8221;</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>IAS 12 &#8211; Income Tax specifies how an entity accounts for income tax, including deferred tax,</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>which represents tax to be paid or recovered in future periods.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>The amendments clarify that the exemption defined in the standard to recognize deferred</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>taxes when assets or liabilities are recognized for the first time is not applicable in the case</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>of leases and provisions for decommissioning.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>The amendments are effective for annual reporting periods beginning on or after January 1,</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>2023 and early application is permitted.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:28.35pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:28.35pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>This amendment did not have a significant impact on the Company&#180;s consolidated financial statements.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C1'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C1'><B>IAS 8: &#8220;Accounting Policies, Changes in Accounting Estimates and Errors&#8221;</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:28.35pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C1'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>In February 2021, the IASB issued amendments to IAS 8, introducing a definition of &#8220;accounting estimates&#8221;. The amendments clarify the distinction between changes in accounting estimates and changes in accounting policies and the correction of errors. They also clarify how entities use measurement techniques and inputs to develop accounting estimates.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>The modifications are effective for annual reporting periods that began on or after January 1, 2023.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>The amendments did not have a significant impact on the Company&#180;s consolidated financial statements.</P>

<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C1'><B>IAS 1: Disclosure of accounting policies &#8211;&nbsp;Practice Statement 2</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:28.35pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>In February 2021, the IASB issued amendments to IAS 1 and Accounting Standards IFRS Practice Statement 2, Making Materiality, providing guidance and examples to help entities apply disclosure materiality judgments on accounting policies. The purpose of the amendments is to help entities provide information about accounting policies that is more useful, by replacing the requirement that entities disclose their &quot;significant&quot; accounting policies with &quot;material&quot; accounting policies and by adding guidance on how Entities apply the concept of materiality when making decisions about disclosures regarding accounting policies.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:28.35pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>The modifications are effective for annual reporting periods that began on or after January 1, 2023.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>The amendments did not have a significant impact on the Company&#180;s consolidated financial statements.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C1'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C1'><B>Amendments to IAS 12 - International Tax Reform &#8211; Pillar Two model rules</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C1'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>In December 2021, the Organization for Economic Co-operation and Development (OECD) released the Pillar Two model rules (the Global Anti-Base Erosion Proposal, or &#8216;GloBE&#8217;) to reform international corporate taxation. Large multinational enterprises within the scope of the rules are required to calculate their GloBE effective tax rate for each jurisdiction where they operate. They will be liable to pay a top-up tax for the difference between their GloBE effective tax rate per jurisdiction and the 15% minimum rate.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>In May 2023, the IASB made narrow-scope amendments to IAS 12 which provide a temporary relief from the requirement to recognise and disclose deferred taxes arising from enacted or substantively enacted tax law that implements the Pillar Two model rules, including tax law that implements qualified domestic minimum top-up taxes described in those rules.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>The modifications are effective for annual reporting periods that began on or after January 1, 2023.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C1'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>As of the date of these Financial Statements, the adoption of the aforementioned amendments has not had effects since there is no tax regulatory framework related to that reform in the jurisdictions in which the Company operates.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C1'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C1'><B>4.a.2) New Accounting standards IFRS issued that are not yet effective for the year beginning January 1,</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:28.35pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C1'><B>2023.</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>The accounting standards IFRS that potentially have an impact on the Company, which are not mandatory and have not been adopted early in the year beginning January 1, 2023, are listed below.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt'>On August 15, 2023, CNV General Resolution No. 972/2023 was published in the BO, which indicates that the early application of IFRS and/or its modifications will not be admitted, unless the CNV specifically admits it.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C1'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C1'><B>IAS 7 &#8220;Statement of cash flows&#8221; and Accounting standards IFRS 7 &#8220;Financial instruments &#8211;&nbsp;Disclosure&#8221;</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:14.2pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt'>In May 2023, the IASB has issued new reporting requirements on supplier financing arrangements. The modifications include new requirements for qualitative and quantitative </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>12</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P align=right style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-21.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>English translation of the original prepared in Spanish for publication in Argentina</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>(Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>_____________________________________________________________________________________________________</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>information regarding financing agreements with financial providers that allow evaluating the effects of these agreements on liabilities, cash flows and exposure to liquidity risk.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:28.35pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt'>Supplier financing arrangements are those characterized by: (i) a financial provider paying the amount that an entity owes to its commercial supplier and (ii) the entity pays the financial provider, according to the terms of the agreement.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt'>The amendments are effective for annual reporting periods beginning on or after January 1, 2024. The amendments do not require disclosure of comparative information.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt'>It is expected that the application of said standard will not have a significant impact on the Company&#180;s consolidated financial statements.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>IAS 21 &#8220;Effects of variations in foreign currency exchange rates&#8221; &#8211;&nbsp;Absence of Convertibility</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:28.35pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt'>On August 15, 2023, the IASB issued the modification to IAS 21 called &#8220;Non-Convertibility&#8221; to provide guidelines in the latter case. The amendment requires entities to apply a consistent approach to evaluating whether a currency can be exchanged for another currency, and if not, determining the exchange rate to use for measurement purposes and the disclosures that need to be provided in its financial statements.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt'>Once the absence of convertibility between currencies has been identified, the exchange rate must be estimated that represents that which would be obtained in an orderly transaction between market participants and that reflects economic conditions. These modifications do not specify a methodology for estimating the exchange rate to be used, but rather it must be developed by each entity.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt'>The amendments are effective for annual reporting periods beginning on or after January 1, 2025. Early adoption is permitted. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:28.35pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C1'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.b) Consolidation</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.b.1) Subsidiaries</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Subsidiaries are all entities over which the Company has control. The Company controls an entity when the Company is exposed to, or has rights to, variable returns from its involvement and has the ability to affect those returns through its power over the entity. Subsidiaries are fully consolidated from the date on which control is transferred to the group. They are deconsolidated from the date that control ceases. For this purpose and unless there are specific requirements, it is generally considered that <B>tgs</B> has control, when it has a participation greater than 50% of the available voting rights.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The accounting policies of the subsidiaries are consistent with the accounting policies adopted by the Company.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Inter-company transactions, balances and profit or loss from transactions between group companies are eliminated. Unrealized profit/loss are also eliminated. </P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.b.2) Associates</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Associates are entities over which the group has significant influence but not control, generally accompanying a shareholding of between 20% and 50% of the voting rights. Investments in associates are accounted for using the equity method of accounting. Under the equity method, the investment is initially recognized at cost, and the carrying amount is increased or decreased to recognize the investor&#8217;s share of the profit or loss of the investee after the date of acquisition. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The Company accounted for the investments in its associates, under the equity method on the basis on the financial statements as of September 30, 2023, of <I>Gas Link S.A</I>. (&#8220;Link&#8221;) and <I>Transporte y Servicios de Gas en Uruguay SA</I> (&#8220;TGU&#8221;). Accounting policies of associates have been changed where necessary to ensure consistency with the policies adopted by the Company. The Company&#8217;s management is not aware of any significant subsequent events which affected the financial statements as of September 30, 2023, of Link and TGU from this date to December 31, 2023. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Unrealized gains and losses resulting from transactions between <B>tgs</B> and the associate or joint venture are eliminated to the extent of the interest in the associate or joint venture. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>In the table below, associates are disclosed, together with the percentage of shareholding and voting as of December 31, 2023 and 2022:</P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>As indicated in &#8220;Note 23 &#8211;&nbsp;Associates and Joint Arrangement&#8221;, on August 7, 2017, the Company proceeded to create a UT (similar to a joint operation) <I>with SACDE Sociedad Argentina de Construcci&#243;n y Desarrollo Estrat&#233;gico S.A.</I> (&#8220;SACDE&#8221;) (&#8220;UT&#8221;). This operation is evaluated as a joint agreement under the provisions included in Accounting standards &#8220;IFRS 11 - Joint Arrangements&#8221; since the parties have joint control of the operation, meaning that the decisions of the relevant activities are taken under the unanimous consent of the parties.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The Company has defined that the UT constitutes a joint operation given that it grants its participants a percentage of the rights over the assets and liabilities arising from each contract. Accordingly, the Company recognizes its share in the jointly operated assets, liabilities, revenues, costs and expenses.</P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.c) Foreign currency translation</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.c.1) Functional and presentation currency</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The consolidated financial statements are presented in thousands of Argentine Pesos, which is the Company&#8217;s functional currency. Each subsidiary or associate determines its own functional currency based on the currency of the primary economic environment in which these entities operate.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.c.2) Transactions and balances</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Assets and liabilities and foreign currency transactions are translated into the functional currency using the exchange rate in effect at the close of each year or prevailing at the date of the transaction, as applicable. Foreign exchange gains and losses are recognized in the Statement of Comprehensive Income. The exchange rate applied to monetary transactions and balances in foreign currency corresponds to that of the MULC as quoted by Banco de la Naci&#243;n Argentina.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Foreign exchange gains and losses resulting from the cancellation of such transactions or from the year-end measurement of monetary assets and liabilities denominated in foreign currencies are recognized in the Statement of Comprehensive Income.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.c.3) Associates</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The functional currency of the associate company TGU, and the subsidiary company TGSLatam, both located abroad, is the US dollar, because it is the currency in which they substantially generate their income and incur their expenses. Assets and liabilities were converted into Argentine pesos using the exchange rate prevailing at the end of each year, their common stock and retained earnings at their historical exchange rates and results at average exchange rates.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.d) Restatement to constant currency - Comparative Information</B></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.d.1) Regulatory framework</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The consolidated financial statements as of December 31, 2023, including comparative figures, have been restated to take into account changes in the general purchasing power of the Company&#8217;s functional currency (the Argentine peso) in accordance with IAS 29 &#8220;Financial Reporting in hyperinflationary economies&#8221; (&#8220;IAS 29&#8221;) and CNV General Resolution No. 777/2018. As a result, the financial statements are stated in terms of the current unit of measurement at the 2023 balance sheet date.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>IAS 29 requires that the financial statements of an entity that reports in the currency of a hyperinflationary economy, regardless of whether they are based on the historical cost method or the current cost method, are expressed in terms of the current unit of measurement at the closing date of the reporting period. In order to conclude on the existence of a hyperinflationary economy, the standard details a series of factors to be considered, among which is a cumulative inflation rate over three years that approaches or exceeds 100%.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The accumulated inflation in three years is over 100%. Likewise, both the National Government projections and other available projections indicate that this trend will not be reversed in the short term.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>15</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>

<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>To evaluate the aforementioned quantitative condition, and also to restate the financial statements, the CNV has established that the series of indexes to be used for the application of IAS 29 is determined by the FACPCE. This series of indexes combines the National Consumer Price Index (&quot;CPI&quot;) as of January 2017 (base month: December 2016) with the Domestic Wholesale Price Index (&quot;IPIM&quot;), both published by the Institute National Statistics and Census (&quot;INDEC&quot;) until that date. For the months of November and December 2015, for which there is no information from the INDEC on the evolution of the IPIM, the variation in the CPI of the Autonomous City of Buenos Aires was applied.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Considering the aforementioned index, inflation was 211.40% and 94.79% in the years ended December 31, 2023 and 2022 respectively.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.d.2) Restatement mechanism</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The financial statements must be adjusted to consider changes in the general purchasing power of the currency, so that they are expressed in the current unit of measurement at the end of the reporting period. Said requirements also include all the comparative information of the financial statements, without modifying the decisions made based on the financial information corresponding to those financial years.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The figures as of December 31, 2022, which are presented in these Consolidated Financial Statements for comparative purposes, arise from the restatement to the current unit of measure of the Financial Statements as of said dates, in accordance with IAS 29.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Restatement of the balance sheet</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:-7.05pt;margin-left:28.35pt'>i. Monetary items (those with a fixed nominal value in local currency) are not restated, since they are already expressed in the current unit of measurement at the closing date of the reporting period. In an inflationary period, maintaining monetary assets generates loss of purchasing power and maintaining monetary liabilities generates a gain in purchasing power, provided that such items are not subject to an adjustment mechanism that compensates to some extent for these effects. The monetary loss or gain is included in the result of the period in which it is reported.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:-7.05pt;margin-left:28.35pt'>ii. The non-monetary items measured at their current values at the end of the reporting period are not restated for the purpose of their presentation in the balance sheet, but the adjustment process must be completed to determine in terms of a homogeneous unit of measurement the results produced by the holding of these non-monetary items.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:-7.05pt;margin-left:28.35pt'>iii. Non-monetary items measured at historical cost or at a fair value as of a date prior to the closing date of the reporting period are restated by coefficients that reflect the variation in the general price level from the date of acquisition or revaluation to the closing date, proceeding then to compare the restated amounts of those assets with the corresponding recoverable values.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:-7.05pt;margin-left:28.35pt'>iv. The restatement of non-monetary assets in the terms of the current unit of measurement at the end of the reporting period without an equivalent adjustment for tax purposes, results in a temporary taxable difference and the recognition of a deferred tax liability whose counterparty is recognized in the result of the period. For the closing of the subsequent period, the deferred tax items are restated for inflation to re-determine the charge to the result of the next period.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:-7.05pt;margin-left:28.35pt'>v. When the capitalization of costs for loans in non-monetary assets in accordance with IAS 23 is applicable, the portion of those costs that compensate the lender for the effects of inflation is not capitalized.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Restatement of the Comprehensive Income Statement</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Revenues and expenses (including interest and foreign exchange differences) are restated from the date of their booking, except for those items of the result that reflect or include in </P>
