<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>(Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>_____________________________________________________________________________________________________</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>their determination the consumption of assets measured in purchasing power of a date before the consumption booked, which are restated based on the date of origin of the asset to which the item is related (for example, depreciation and other consumption of assets valued at historical cost); and also those results that arise from comparing two measurements expressed in purchasing power currency of different dates, for which it is necessary to identify the amounts compared, restate them separately, and make the comparison, but with the amounts already restated.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The result of the exposure to the change in the purchasing power of the currency (monetary results) is presented in a separate line and reflects the effect of inflation on the monetary items.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Restatement of the statement of changes in equity</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>As of the transition date (January 1, 2016), the Company applied the following special rules:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:-7.05pt;margin-left:28.35pt'>i. The components of the capital stock were restated from the dates they were contributed.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:-7.05pt;margin-left:28.35pt'>ii. Reserved earnings were maintained at the date of transition at their nominal value (legal amount without restatement).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:-7.05pt;margin-left:28.35pt'>iii. The restated unallocated results were determined by the difference between the net assets restated at the transition date and the rest of the initial equity components expressed as indicated in the preceding sections.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:-7.05pt;margin-left:28.35pt'>iv. After the restatement at the transition date, all the components of the equity are restated by applying the general price index from the beginning of the period, and each variation of those components is restated from the date of contribution or from the moment in which is added by any other means.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Restatement of the statement of cash flows</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>IAS 29 requires that all items in this statement should be restated in terms of the current unit of measurement as of the closing date of the period for which it is reported.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The monetary result generated by cash and cash equivalents is presented in the statement of cash flows separately from cash flows from operating, investing and financing activities, as a specific item of the reconciliation between cash and cash equivalents at the beginning and at the end of the year.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-22.7pt;margin-left:51.9pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.e) Financial instruments</B></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.e.1) Financial assets</B></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Recognition and initial measurement</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Financial assets are classified, at the time of initial recognition, as:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;text-align:right;margin-left:0pt;width:0pt;font:9pt Verdana'>i.</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Financial assets subsequently measured at amortised cost, and&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;text-align:right;margin-left:0pt;width:0pt;font:9pt Verdana'>ii.</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Financial assets subsequently measured at fair value (either with changes in other comprehensive income or with changes in results).&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The classification of financial assets at initial recognition depends on the financial asset&#8217;s contractual cash flow characteristics and the Company&#8217;s business model for managing them. For additional information, see Note 16.2.1.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Subsequent measurement</B></P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>17</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P align=right style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-21.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>English translation of the original prepared in Spanish for publication in Argentina</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>(Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>_____________________________________________________________________________________________________</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>After initial recognition, financial assets are measured according to their initial classification according to the following categories:</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Financial assets measured at amortized cost </I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>It is the most relevant category used by the Company, financial assets are classified and measure at amortized cost if both of the following conditions are met:</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:21.25pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:14.15pt></KBD>The financial asset is held within a business model with the objective to hold financial assets in order to collect contractual cash flows; and&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:21.25pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:14.15pt></KBD>The contractual terms of the financial asset give rise on specified dates to cash flows that are solely payments of principal and interest on the principal amount outstanding. &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Financial assets measured at amortized cost are subsequently measured using the effective interest method. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Gains and losses are recognized in the Statement of Comprehensive Income under financial results when the asset is derecognized, modified or impaired. </P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Financial assets at fair value through OCI (Debt instruments)</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Corresponds to financial assets that are maintained in a business model whose objective is achieved by obtaining contractual cash flows and selling them.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Unrealized gains or losses arising from changes in fair value are recognized as other comprehensive income, except for the accrual of interest, exchange rate difference and the impairment of such assets that are recognized as financial results in the Statement of Comprehensive Income. At the time the asset is written off, the accumulated gain or loss is recognized as a financial result and it is eliminated from the respective reserve.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>As of December 31, 2023 and 2022, the Company has not recognized any financial assets under this category.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Financial assets designated at fair value through OCI (equity instruments) </I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Upon initial recognition, the Company can elect to classify irrevocably its equity investments as equity instruments designated at fair value through OCI when they meet the definition of equity under IAS 32.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Gains and losses on these financial assets are never recycled to profit or loss. Dividends are recognized as other income in the Statement of Comprehensive Income when the right of payment has been established, except when the Company benefits from such proceeds as a recovery of part of the cost of the financial asset, in which case, such gains are recorded in OCI. Equity instruments designated at fair value through OCI are not subject to impairment assessment.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>As of December 31, 2023 and 2022, the Company has not recognized any financial assets under this category.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Financial assets at fair value through profit or loss </I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>In the event that financial assets are not classified according to the aforementioned categories, they will be subsequently measured at fair value, presenting gains or losses arising from changes in fair value in the income statement within financial results in the year in which they are originated.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>18</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P align=right style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-21.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>English translation of the original prepared in Spanish for publication in Argentina</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>(Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>_____________________________________________________________________________________________________</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Impairment of financial assets </B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The Company applies the Expected Credit loss (&#8220;ECL&#8221;) model for those financial assets accounted for at amortized cost or at fair value through OCI. The ECL is based on the difference between the contractual cash flows due in accordance with the contract and the cash flows that the Company expects to receive, discounted at an approximation of the original effective interest rate. To this end, the Company evaluates various factors, including credit risk, historical trends and other available information.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The application of this model implies recognition of:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:54pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>Expected credit losses within of 12 months: these are expected credit losses that result from possible default events within 12 months after the filing date; and&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:54pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>Expected credit losses during the life of the asset: these are expected credit losses that result from possible events of default during the expected life of a financial instrument.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>In case a loss allowance is recognized, the carrying amount of the asset is reduced through an impairment account and the amount of the loss is presented in the Statement of Comprehensive Income at the time it occurs. Subsequent recoveries of amounts previously written off are credited in the same line item. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The impairment tests performed on accounts receivable are described in Note 4.h).</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.e.2) Financial liabilities</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Includes trade payables, loans, other payables and certain payroll and social security taxes payable. </P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Recognition and initial measurement</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Financial liabilities are classified, at initial recognition, as subsequently measured at amortized cost or at fair value through profit or loss, as appropriate.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>All financial liabilities are initially recognized at fair value net of transaction costs.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>They are classified in current liabilities, except those whose maturity exceeds twelve months, which are classified as non-current liabilities.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Subsequent measurement</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Financial liabilities at fair value through profit or loss</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Includes financial liabilities held for trading. As of December 31, 2023 and 2022, there are no instruments classified in this category.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Financial liabilities measured at amortized cost</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The Company includes financial liabilities with fixed or determinable payments that are not quoted in an active market. Current liabilities are included, except those whose maturity exceeds twelve months including premiums, discounts and direct expenses, which are included as non-current liabilities. They are measured using the effective interest method. As of December 31, 2023 and 2022, all of the Company&#8217;s financial liabilities were classified in this category.</P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>_____________________________________________________________________________________________________</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Offsetting of financial instruments</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Financial assets and liabilities are offset when there is a legally enforceable right to offset the recognized amounts and there is an intention to settle on a net basis, or realize the asset and settle the liability simultaneously.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.f) Derivative financial instruments</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Derivative financial instruments are recognized at their fair value at inception and subsequently measured at their fair value and disclosed as assets or liabilities depending if it is gain or loss. The results of derivative financial instruments are classified under &#8220;Financial gain / expenses&#8221; in the statement of comprehensive income, or in the other comprehensive income if hedge accounting is applied.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Derivative financial instruments are measured in accordance with Accounting Standard IFRS 13 &#8220;Fair value measurement&#8221;.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The method of recognizing the resulting gain or loss depends on whether the derivative is designated as a hedging instrument or not and, according to the nature of the item being hedged. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.g) Inventories</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Inventories consist of natural gas (in excess of the &#8220;Line Pack&#8221; classified as property, plant and equipment) in the Company&#8217;s pipeline system, and the liquids stored obtained from natural gas processing at the Cerri Complex.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Inventories are measured at the lower of cost restated for the inflation effects as mentioned in Note 4.d. or net realizable value. Cost is determined using the weighted average cost method. The cost of inventories includes expenditure incurred in purchasing and production and other necessary costs to bring them to their existing location and condition. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The net realizable value is the estimated selling price in the ordinary course of business less the estimated cost of completion and the estimated costs to make the sale.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The assessment of the recoverable value of these assets is made at each reporting date, and the resulting loss is recognized in the statement of comprehensive income when the inventories are overstated.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.h) Trade receivables, contract assets and other receivables</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Trade receivables are amounts due from customers for goods and services performed in the ordinary course of business. Contract assets are unbilled amounts due to customers related to works in progress. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Trade receivables, contract assets and other receivables are recognized initially at fair value and subsequently measured at amortized cost using the effective interest method, less allowance for trade receivables.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The Company applies the simplified approach to measuring expected credit losses for trade receivables, contract assets and other receivables. For this purpose, customers have been grouped based on shared credit risk characteristics, the existence of guarantees, historical credit losses experienced and the existence of judicial proceedings aimed at obtaining payment. Once each group was defined, an expected uncollectibility rate calculated based on historic default rates adjusted to future economic conditions was defined.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>If a devaluation is recognized, the book amount of the asset is reduced through a provision account and the amount of the loss is recognized in the Statement of Comprehensive Income </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>20</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>

<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>at the time it occurs. If in subsequent periods the amount of the impairment loss decreases, the reverse of the same is also recorded in the Statement of Comprehensive Income.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.i) Cash and cash equivalents</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Cash and cash equivalents includes cash in hand, deposits with banking institutions and other short-term, highly liquid investments with original maturities not exceeding three months and without being subject to a risk of a significant change of value.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.j) Property, plant and equipment (&#8220;PPE&#8221;)</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Assets transferred from the privatization of GdE:</B> </FONT>The value of these assets was determined based on the price paid for the acquisition of 70% of the Company&#8217;s common stock, which amounted to U.S.$ 561.2 million. This price was the basis to determine a total value of common stock of U.S.$ 801.7 million, which, when added to the debt assumed under the Company&#8217;s privatization agreement (the &#8220;Transfer Agreement&#8221;) of U.S.$ 395 million, resulted in a total value for PPE of U.S.$ 1,196.7 million. Such value, converted at the exchange rate in effect as of the date of the Transfer Agreement, has been restated for the effects of inflation as mentioned in Note 4.d, and less accumulated depreciation.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:0.2pt;margin-left:14.2pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Line pack:</B></FONT> It represents the natural gas in the transportation system that is necessary to keep the system at operating capacity, valued at acquisition cost and restated for the effects of inflation as mentioned in Note 4.d.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Materials</B></FONT>: Materials are recognized at historical cost restated in accordance with Note 4.d. Consumption is restated based on the origin date of the acquisition of the asset.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:0.2pt;margin-left:14.2pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Other items of PPE:</B></FONT> have been valued at acquisition cost restated for the effects of inflation as mentioned in Note 4.d, and net of accumulated depreciation. They include, mainly, all the investments made to achieve system integrity and public safety equal to those required by international standards. Such investments included, among others, the costs of survey programs related to internal and external pipeline inspection, cathodic protection, pipeline replacement and recoating, and the facilities affected to the Production and Commercialization of Liquids and Midstream segment.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:0.2pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>PPE additions are recorded at acquisition or construction cost less accumulated depreciation and impairment losses (if applicable), except land, which is recorded at historical cost acquisition minus any impairment (if applicable), all this restated for the effects of inflation as mentioned in Note 4.d. The cost includes the cost of replacing significant components and the borrowing costs derived from loans that finance its construction to the extent that the requirements for recognition as assets are met.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:0.2pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>Subsequent costs restated for the effects of inflation as mentioned in Note 4.d. are included in the carrying amount of the asset or recognized as a separate asset, as appropriate, only when it is probable that future economic benefits associated with the item will flow to the Company and the cost of the item can be reliably measured. The carrying amount of a replaced component is written off. In the same way, when a major maintenance is carried out, they are added to the cost of the equipment if the recognition criteria are satisfied, eliminating any remaining non-depreciated remaining value restated for the effects of inflation as mentioned in Note 4.d, if any, of previous overhaul. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:0.2pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>In this sense, Resolutions No. 1660/2000 (&#8220;Resolution 1660&#8221;) and No. 1903/2000 (&#8220;Resolution 1903&#8221;) issued by ENARGAS include definitions about the costs that should be considered as improvements or maintenance expenses. All other repairs and maintenance are charged to the statement of comprehensive income when incurred.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:0.2pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>In accordance with IAS 23, the Company capitalizes financial expense on long term construction projects, until the moment in which the asset is in conditions for its use. Capitalization of borrowing costs is carried out considering the provisions of IAS 29, recording as an expense in the Statement of Comprehensive Income the part of the borrowing costs </P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>(Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>_____________________________________________________________________________________________________</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>that compensates for inflation during the same period. For the years ended December 31, 2023 and 2022, there have not been capitalized borrowing costs.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:0.2pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>Depreciation related to natural gas transportation assets is computed under the straight-line method over the estimated useful lives of the specific assets, which are not exceeding the maximum useful lives established by ENARGAS through Resolutions 1660 and 1903.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:0.2pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>For depreciation of all other PPE, the Company uses the straight-line method of depreciation based on the useful life assigned to each item. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:0.2pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>Major maintenance costs are depreciated according to the estimated time until the next major maintenance planned. Regarding the capitalized financial costs, they are depreciated based on the remaining useful lives of those components of PPE that originated such capitalization.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:0.2pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>The assets&#8217; residual values and useful lives are reviewed, and adjusted if appropriate, at each reporting date. For further information, see Note 12.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:0.2pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>The result generated by the disposal of PPE components is recognized in the year in which it is generated.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:0.2pt;margin-left:14.2pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Impairment of non-financial assets:</B></FONT> The Company assesses at each reporting period whether there is an indication that an individual component or a group of PPE may be impaired. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:0.2pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>If any indication exists, the Company estimates the asset&#180;s recoverable amount. An asset&#180;s recoverable amount is the higher of the fair value less costs to sell that asset, and its value-in-use. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:0.2pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>That amount is determined for and individual asset, unless the asset does not generate cash inflows that are largely independent of those from other assets or groups of assets; in which case, the cash flows of the group of assets that form part of the cash-generating unit (&#8220;CGU&#8221;) to which the belong are taken.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:0.2pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>Where the carrying amount of an individual asset or CGU exceeds its recoverable amount, the individual asset or CGU, as the case may be, is considered impaired and is written down to its recoverable amount. Impairment losses are recognized in the consolidated statement of comprehensive income.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>In assessing value in use, the estimated future cash flows are discounted to their present value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current market assessments of the time value of money and the risks specific to the asset for which the estimates of future cash flows have not been adjusted. To such end, the Company makes estimates and assumptions of the economic conditions that will prevail throughout the useful life of the assets.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>As a result of the factors mentioned above, actual cash flows and values could vary significantly from projected cash flows and the values derived from the discounting techniques used.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>Impairment losses, if any, are recognized in the statement of comprehensive income.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;text-indent:-0.55pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>As of December 31, 2023 and 2022, the book value of PPE did not exceed its recoverable value.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Infrastructure used in the natural gas transportation service:</B></FONT> for its measurement and disclosure, the Company has evaluated the application of Interpretation No. 12 &#8220;Service Concession Agreements&#8221; (IFRIC 12) that sets the guidelines for the accounting of private entities that provide public services through a service concession agreement or a contract of a similar nature.</P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>_____________________________________________________________________________________________________</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>Considering the current terms and conditions of the License, <B>tgs</B> concluded that the License is outside the scope of IFRIC 12, as it is considered in substance to provide for an indefinite term because the infrastructure will never revert to the grantor and due to the characteristics of renewal of the License that give a similar result to what which would result from having obtained a perpetual right to operate the infrastructure.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt'>The evaluation carried out and the conclusions reached by <B>tgs</B> are consistent with those of other transportation and natural gas distribution companies in Argentina that are subject to the similar regulations and license agreements. The evaluation was carried out jointly, when the transportation and distribution companies adopted the Accounting Standards IFRS in Argentina in 2012, together with the FACPCE, the Buenos Aires Stock Exchange (<I>Bolsas y Mercados Argentinos</I> -&#8220;BYMA&#8221;) and the CNV, considering the contributions of ENARGAS with respect to the regulatory aspects of the License agreements. In this regard, the CNV issued General Resolution No. 613/2012, ratifying that IFRIC 12 does not apply to natural gas transportation and distribution licenses established under the regulatory framework described in Note 17.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.k) Leases</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Leases are recognized as a right-of-use asset and a corresponding liability at the date on which the leased asset is available for use by the Company. Each lease payment is allocated between the liability and the finance cost. The finance cost is expensed over the lease term to produce a constant periodic interest rate on the remaining balance of the liability for each period. The right-of-use asset is depreciated over the shorter of the useful life of the asset or the term of the contract.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially measured based on the present value of the lease. Liabilities include the net present value of the following lease payments:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>a)</B> </FONT>Fixed payments, less any incentive receivable;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>b)</B> </FONT>Variable lease payments;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>c)</B> </FONT>Amounts expected to be collected as a guarantee of the residual value;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>d)</B> </FONT>The exercise price of the lease option; and</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>e)</B> </FONT>Penalty payments for termination of the lease.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Lease payments are discounted using the interest rate implicit in the lease. If such rate cannot be determined, the Company's incremental borrowing rate is used, which is the rate that <B>tgs</B> would have to pay to borrow the funds necessary to obtain an asset of similar value in a similar economic environment.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Right-of-use assets are measured at cost which comprises the following:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>a)</B> </FONT>The amount of the initial measurement of the lease liability;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>b)</B> </FONT>Any lease payments made on or before the commencement date less any lease incentives received;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>c)</B> </FONT>Any initial direct costs; and</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>d)</B> </FONT>Restoration costs</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>These assets, which are subject to the risk of impairment, are depreciated on a straight-line basis over the shorter of the useful life of the leased asset or the lease term. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Payments associated with short-term leases and low-value assets are recognized on a straight-line basis as an expense in the Statement of Income. Short-term leases are those with a term of 12 months or less. Low-value assets comprise computer equipment, vehicles, small items of office furniture and real estate.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The Company has rights to use assets that are shown as part of Property, plant and equipment (see Note 12). Lease liabilities are shown under Financial Debt (see Note 13). For further information regarding the expense related to short-term and low value leases and the interest expense on lease liabilities, see Note 8.j. and 8.k., respectively.</P>

<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.l) Loans</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Loans have been initially recorded at fair value net of direct attributable transaction costs. Subsequently, loans are valued at their amortised cost. Liabilities are disclosed as non-current when their maturity exceeds twelve months.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.m) Trade payables</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Trade payable are initially recognized at fair value. Subsequently they are measured at amortised cost using the effective tax method. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.n) Income tax and deferred income tax</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Income tax</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Income tax includes current tax and deferred income tax. Income tax is presented in the Statement of Comprehensive Income.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The current income tax is calculated on the basis of tax regulations in force at each reporting period. Management periodically evaluates positions taken in tax returns with respect to situations in which tax regulations are subject to interpretation and establishes provisions if applicable. As of December 31, 2023, and 2022, there are no provisions booked for this concept.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The Company has calculated income tax charges using the deferred tax method, which considers the effect of temporary differences between the financial statement carrying amounts of existing assets and liabilities and their respective tax bases.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>For the purposes of determining the deferred assets and liabilities, the tax rate that is expected to be in effect at the time of their reversal or use has been applied to the identified temporary differences, considering the legal regulations sanctioned on the date of issuance of these financial statements.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>On December 29, 2017, the National Executive Branch promulgated Law No. 27,430 (the &#8220;Law 27630&#8221;) on Tax Reform. It has introduced several changes to the text of the Income Tax Law. Law 27630 establishes a new treatment with the application of the following scale, updated for the years beginning on January 1, 2023, maintaining the 7% rate on dividends.</P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>A deferred tax is recognized on the temporary differences arising from investments in subsidiaries and associates, except for deferred tax liabilities where the Company is able to control the timing of the reversal of the temporary difference and it is probable that the reversal will not occur in the foreseeable future.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Deferred tax assets and liabilities are offset if the Company has a legally enforceable right to offset recognized amounts and when deferred tax assets and liabilities relate to income tax levied by the same tax authority on the same taxable entity or different taxable entities that intend to settle tax assets and liabilities on a net basis. Deferred tax assets are recognized to the extent that it is probable that future taxable income will be generated against which the temporary differences can be used.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The assets and liabilities generated by the application of the deferred tax were valued at their nominal amount considering the restatements for inflation mentioned in Note 4.d) and are classified as non-current assets or liabilities.</P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>_____________________________________________________________________________________________________</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.o) Provisions</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The Company has recorded provisions related to legal actions, judicial court, claims and administrative proceedings, including interpretive questions of the current legislation and those of regulatory nature.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Provisions for legal claims and/or claims by third parties (&quot;legal claims and others&quot;) are recorded at the expected cancellation value when the Company has a present obligation as a result of a past event, it is probable that an outflow of resources will be required to settle the obligation; and the amount has been reliably estimated. Estimates are reviewed and adjusted, as the Company obtains additional information to be received.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.p) Revenue recognition from contract with customers</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Revenue is measured at the fair value of the consideration received or to be received, and represents amounts receivable for goods and/or services supplied. Revenue is recognized when the control of goods or services is transferred to the customer and the consideration is determined by an amount that reflects the consideration that the Company expects to receive.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Tax on exports and turnover tax are disclosed as Selling Expenses.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The following are the accounting policies of the Company for the recognition of revenue of each of the business segments defined by our management:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Natural Gas Transportation</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Natural Gas transportation services includes: (i) firm natural gas transportation, whose revenues are recognized when the capacity is reserved and paid for regardless of actual usage by the customer, (ii) interruptible natural gas transportation and exchange and displacement services whose revenues are recognized when services are rendered and (iii) the operation and maintenance service of the assets affected by the natural gas transportation service corresponding to the expansions promoted by the National Government and whose ownership corresponds to the trusts created for such purpose whose revenues are recognized when services are rendered.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The applicable rates arise from the tariff tables published by ENARGAS. Thus Company&#8217;s revenues are recognized by the amount for which it will be entitled to receive as consideration.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>At the end of each month, <B>tgs</B> recognizes, over the time, its revenues from sales equivalent to the firm reserved capacity, the volumes of natural gas transported under the modalities of interruptible and exchange and displacement and by the operation and maintenance services. In return, a trade receivable is recognized which represents an unconditional right that the Company has to receive the consideration owed by the customer. On the other hand, the billing of the service is done monthly and according to the guidelines established by ENARGAS, the consideration is received within said calendar month.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Liquids Production and Commercialization</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>This business segment includes: (i) production and commercialization of liquids, and (ii) other liquid services.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>25</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P align=right style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-21.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>English translation of the original prepared in Spanish for publication in Argentina</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>(Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>_____________________________________________________________________________________________________</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Liquids Production and Commercialization in the domestic market</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>In the domestic market, <B>tgs</B> sells the production of propane and butane to LPG retailers in the framework of the programs created by the National Government to supply the domestic market. The sale prices are determined by the ex-Secretary of Hydrocarbon Resources (&#8220;SHR&#8221;). For more information, see Note 17 - Regulatory framework &#8211;&nbsp;b) Regulatory framework for non-regulated segments - to these consolidated financial statements. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The price of those tons of propane and butane that are not sold within the framework of the aforementioned programs is determined by the Secretary of Energy based on the international reference prices.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Regarding ethane sales, they are made to PBB Polisur S.R.L. (&#8220;PBB&#8221;), the only customer to whom this product is sold. To estimate transaction price, the Company uses the most probable amount method. In this regard, the Company only recognizes those transactions where it is highly probable that they will not be reversed in the future.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Liquids Production and Commercialization in the foreign market</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>In the foreign market, the Company markets propane, butane and natural gasoline to international traders (&#8220;traders&#8221;) and other clients of worldwide renown, some of them through trucks.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>These sales are made under short-term contracts (less than one year), with the price determined as reference to international prices plus / minus a fixed amount per ton sold. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>For both domestic and foreign market sales, <B>tgs</B> transfers control and recognizes revenues when the products are delivered to the customer and therefore the product has been accepted and there is no evidence of the existence of pending obligations by the Company. It is at that moment when a trade receivable is recognized given that the receipt of the consideration is unconditional and only the passage of time is the only requirement for receiving the consideration owed by the customer.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Other liquids services</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The Liquids production and commercialization segment also comprises reception, storage and dispatch of the liquids from the facilities located in Puerto Galv&#225;n.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Revenues are recognized when the service is effectively rendered, that is, after the dispatch to each vessel. The price is agreed by the parties being a fixed amount per ton of product dispatched, there being no variable components in them. These services are billed monthly, at which time an unconditional right to receive the consideration from the client arises.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Midstream</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The services included in the Midstream segment consist mainly in (i) treatment, removal of impurities and natural gas compression, including the collection and transport of natural gas (ii) inspection and maintenance of pipelines and compressor plants, (iii) services of steam generation for electricity production and management services for expansion works and steam generation for the production of electricity and (iv) natural gas transportation services in Vaca Muerta. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Revenues from sales of this business segment are recognized over the time in the period in which the service is provided. The sale price is determined according to what arises from the contractual conditions agreed between <B>tgs</B> and its customers. In all cases, the recognition and billing of sales income is made on a monthly basis so that at that time a sales credit is recorded.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Revenues from the participation in the joint venture, which correspond to the construction activities provided by the joint venture, are recognized based on the stage of completion of </P>

<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>the contractual activity (percentage of completion method), considering the estimated final margin of the work. To apply the percentage of completion method, the revenue recognized at the end of the period will correspond to the total contractual revenue multiplied by the actual percentage of completion, based on the proportion of the total direct contractual costs incurred to date, and the total direct contractual costs, including the estimated costs to complete the construction. Costs incurred in excess of the costs associated with revenue are recognized in Contract assets.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Telecommunications</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Revenues from the provision of Telecommunications services are recognized in the statement of comprehensive income at the time of effective performance of the service. The sale price is determined according to what arises from the contractual conditions agreed between <B>tgs</B> and its customers. The consideration is determined as a fixed monthly amount. In all cases, the recognition and billing of sales income is made on a monthly basis so that at that time a sales receivable is recorded.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Financial components</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The Company does not have any contracts where the period between the transfer of the promised goods or services to the customer and payment by the customer exceeds one year. As a consequence, the Company does not adjust any of the transaction prices for the time value of money.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.q) Subsidies</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>As part of its participation in propane and butane supply programs in the local market carried out by the National Government, (for more information see &#8220;Note 17 - Regulatory Framework - b) Regulatory framework for non-regulated segments&#8221;), the Company receives from the Secretary of Energy a series of subsidies that are recognized in accordance with the provisions of IAS 20 &#8220;Accounting for government grants and disclosures of government assistance&#8221; because they correspond to economic compensations calculated as the difference between the sale prices of the products determined in accordance with the legislation in force and the reference prices calculated by the Secretary of Energy.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Subsidies are recognized at fair value whenever there is reasonable assurance that will be received and that the product has been delivered. They are presented within the caption &#8220;Revenues&#8221; of the statement of comprehensive income.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.r) Contract liabilities</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Mainly consist of pre-payments for services made by customers in order to finance the works to render the service<FONT style=color:#222222>. </FONT>Contract liabilities are recognized initially at their fair value. Subsequent to initial recognition, advances &nbsp;from customers are measured at their amortised cost based on the projections of the services to be provided that cancel the advances, restated for the inflation effects as mentioned in Note 4.d).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.s) Equity accounts</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The activity in the Equity accounts reflects resolutions adopted by Shareholders in their meetings, or the effects of the laws or regulations in force. The equity accounts are restated for the inflation effects according to what is mentioned in Note 4.d), except the account Capital stock which is maintained at its original value.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Common stock and adjustment to common stock</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The common stock consists of contributions made by shareholders represented by shares and comprises outstanding shares at their face value, net of treasury shares mentioned below.</P>

<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Common stock accounts were restated in constant currency as mentioned in Note 4.d). Common stock account was kept at original value and the adjustment arising from such restatement is shown under &#8220;Inflation Adjustment to common stock&#8221;.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Common stock adjustment is not distributable in cash or in kind but may be capitalized through issuance of shares. In addition, this balance may be used to compensate accumulated losses.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><FONT style=background-color:#FFFFFF><B>Treasury shares and adjustments to treasury shares</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Corresponds to the reclassification of the nominal value and corresponding restatement in constant peso (Inflation Adjustment to Common Stock) of shares issued and repurchased by the Company in market transactions, as required by the current regulations of the CNV.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Own equity instruments that are reacquired (treasury shares) are recognized at cost restated for the inflation effects as mentioned in Note 4.d) and deducted from equity. No gain or loss is recognized on the purchase, sale or cancellation of the Company&#8217;s treasury shares. Any difference between the carrying amount and the consideration, if reissued, is recognized as a premium on common stock or additional paid-up capital.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Additional paid-up capital</B></P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Corresponds to the difference between the carrying value of the treasury shares and the quoted value at the time were distributed (for more information see Note 19.b). It is restated according to what is mentioned in Note 4.d).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Legal Reserve</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>Pursuant to the provisions of the Argentine Business Association Law and the CNV, the Company is required to set up a legal reserve by providing at least 5% of the aggregate amount of net income for the year, prior year adjustments, transfers of other comprehensive income to retained earnings and accumulated losses of prior years, when this aggregate amount exceeds zero until the legal reserve equals 20% of the sum of Capital stock and Adjustment to capital stock balances.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Distribution of dividends</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The cash dividend is recognized as a liability in the Company&#8217;s financial statements in the year in which they are approved <FONT style=background-color:#FFFFFF>by the shareholders of the Company or the Board of Directors</FONT> according to the powers delegated by the Shareholders&#8217; Meeting, as appropriate.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Retained earnings</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>The outstanding balance of retained earnings includes accumulated gains or losses which were not allocated to a specific purpose reserve and, when positive, may be distributed pursuant to the decision of the Shareholders provided these retained earnings are not subject to legal restrictions, as mentioned above &#8220;Legal reserve&#8221;.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B>4.t) Basic and diluted earnings per share</B></P>
