<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:14.4pt'>As of the date of the issuance of these consolidated financial statements, there are no <B>tgs</B> instruments outstanding that imply the existence of potential ordinary shares, thus the basic net income per share for the years ended on December 31, 2023 and 2022 matches the diluted net income per share. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><B>5.</B></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:21.2pt></KBD><B>CRITICAL ACCOUNTING ESTIMATES </B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The preparation of financial statements in conformity with professional accounting standards requires the Company to make accounting estimates that affect the reported amounts of assets and liabilities and the disclosure of contingent assets and liabilities at the date of the financial statements and the reported amounts of revenues and expenses during the reporting period. The making of such estimates involves <B>tgs</B> using assumptions and presumptions that are based on a number of factors, including past trends, events known at the date of issuance of these financial statements, and expectations of future events and their outcomes.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>5.a) Impairment of PPE</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>As mentioned in Note 4.j), the Company periodically evaluates the existence of significant events or changes that could have an adverse effect on the Company or will take place in the near future that could affect the recoverable amount of PPE. These evaluations are performed at the lowest level for which identifiable cash flows exist, i.e., for each CGU. The Company considers each business segment as a CGU.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>When assessing whether an impairment indicator may exist, <B>tgs</B> evaluates both internal and external sources of information, such as the following:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:50.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:50.2pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>Whether significant decreases in the market values of PPE elements took place.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:50.2pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>Whether prices of the main products and services that are marketed decreased.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:50.2pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>Whether significant changes in the regulatory framework were introduced.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:50.2pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>Whether operating costs suffered a materially increase.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:50.2pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>Whether evidence of obsolescence or physical damage has occurred.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Whether the macroeconomic situation in which <B>tgs</B> carries out its activities, including significant variations in the sale prices of products, raw materials, interest rates, etc, has worsen. &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Since August 2019, the main macroeconomic and business variables in Argentina suffered a significant deterioration. This situation was aggravated in 2020 by the negative consequences that COVID had on Argentina's economic situation which led the Argentine Government to take a series of measures even affecting the regulatory framework of the natural gas transportation segment (see Notes 1 and 17). Given these indicators of impairment of the book value of PPE, the recoverable value of Natural Gas Transportation segment CGU has been calculated based on its value in use as of December 31, 2023.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The value in use of PPE is sensitive to significant variation in the assumptions applied, including the adjustment of future tariffs determined by the Argentine Government in the Natural Gas Transportation segment. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The value in use is calculated based on the basis of discounted future cash flows. The projected cash flows are prepared taking into account significant assumptions relating to: (i) discount rate, (ii) estimates of future tariff adjustments and the recognition of cost adjustments, (iii) expected macroeconomic variables such as interest rates, inflation, foreign exchange rates, among others. The discount rate is based on a weighted average cost of capital (&quot;WACC&quot;).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>In performing the analysis for the Natural Gas Transportation segment, the Company considered among others: (i) the status of the negotiations with the Argentine Government, (ii) the contractual rights derived from the License, (iii) Management&#180;s expectations </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>29</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P align=right style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-21.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>English translation of the original prepared in Spanish for publication in Argentina</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>(Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>_____________________________________________________________________________________________________</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'><FONT style='font:9pt Verdana'>regarding the transitional tariff increase to be granted until the new RTI is concluded, (iv) Management&#180;s expectation of the outcome of the new RTI process and (v) the impact of a cost monitoring scheme that allows for semi-annual adjustments to current tariffs.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The Company has prepared, for the Natural Gas Transportation segment, three different estimates of the expected cash flows by sensitizing its main variables and assigning probabilities of occurrence based on experience and considering the current socio-economic context, as follows:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'>a)</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Base scenario: probability of occurrence assigned 50%.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'>b)</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Optimistic scenario: probability of occurrence assigned 35%.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>c)</B> </FONT>Pessimistic scenario: probability of occurrence assigned 15%.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>In order to make the comparison between the expected cash flow and the book value of the assets assigned to the Natural Gas Transportation segment, the Company has used a weighting of the scenarios, in accordance with the probabilities mentioned above, to determine the expected value in use. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>As of December 31, 2023 and 2022, based on the foregoing, the Company has not determined the need to record an additional impairment charge to the one that counts. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>As of December 31, 2023, the book value of the PPE related to the Natural Gas Transportation cash-generating unit amounted Ps. 571,160,601.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The estimated recoverable amounts of PPE items are sensitive to significant variation in the assumptions applied. In either case, there can be no assurance with certainty that the actual cash flows arising from these circumstances will be in line with the assumptions applied in determining the values in use. Therefore, significant differences could arise in the future in relation to the estimated values in use.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>5.b) Provisions for legal and other claims</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The Company has recorded certain contingent liabilities related to legal, judicial or extrajudicial actions, claims and administrative proceedings, including those of a legal and regulatory nature. The Company records liabilities when their occurrence is probable and when a reliable estimate of their amount can be made. Provisions are based on events known to the Company at the date of issuance of its financial statements, their probability of occurrence, its estimates of the outcome of such matters and the experience of its legal advisors in contesting, litigating and settling other matters. To the extent that there are more elements of judgment that allow improving the evaluation of contingencies, there will be changes in the estimates of future charges, which could have an impact on the Company's future results and its economic and/or financial situation.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;color:#0070C0'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:-21pt'><B>6.</B></KBD><B>SUPPLEMENTAL CASH FLOW INFORMATION</B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>For purposes of the consolidated statement of cash flows, the Company considers all highly liquid temporary investments with an original maturity of three months or less at the time of purchase to be cash equivalents. The cash flow statement has been prepared using the indirect method, which requires a series of adjustments to reconcile net income for the period to net cash flows from operating activities.</P>

<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;text-indent:28.35pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Accounting standards IFRS 8 &#8220;Operating Segments&#8221; requires an entity to report financial and descriptive information about its reportable segments, which are operating segments or aggregations of operating segments that meet specified criteria. Operating segments are components of an entity about which separate financial information is available that is evaluated regularly by the chief operating decision maker (&#8220;CODM&#8221;) in deciding how to allocate resources and in assessing performance. The Company&#8217;s CODM is the Board of Directors.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>As of December 31, 2023, the Company changed the name of the Other Services business segment to Midstream. This modification does not alter the criteria for assigning income, costs, expenses, assets and liabilities adopted in the past or the comparative information presented.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The Company analyzes its businesses into four segments: (i) Natural Gas Transportation Services, subject to ENARGAS regulations, (ii) Liquids Production and Commercialization, (iii) Midstream and (iv) Telecommunications. These last three business segments are not regulated by ENARGAS. Liquids Production and Commercialization business segment is regulated by the SE.</P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'> &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The authorization for the public offering of the 2017 Program was granted by the CNV through Resolutions No. 17,262 and 18,938 dated January 3, 2014 and September 15, 2017, respectively. On October 31, 2018, through Provision No. DI-2018-55-APN-GE#CNV, the CNV granted the extension of the term of the 2017 Program until January 3, 2024.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The Ordinary General Shareholders' Meeting held on August 15, 2019 decided to increase the 2017 Global Program from US$ 700 million to US$ 1,200 million. This increase was authorized by the CNV on October 9, 2019 through Resolution RESFC-2019-20486-APN-DIR#CNV.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>On October 11, 2023, CNV approved the extension of the maximum amount of the Program from US$ 1,200 million to US$ 2,000 million and the extension of the validity period were approved. of the Program for an additional 5 years from the expiration of the term, with the new expiration of the program being January 3, 2029.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Funds obtained by the Company are applied to:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:63.8pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:-14.15pt'>i.</KBD>The repurchase of the Class 1 Notes (the &quot;2014 Notes&quot;) for US$ 86,511,165;&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-21.25pt;margin-left:70.9pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>ii. the cancellation and total redemption of the 2014 Notes for US$ 120,786,581;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-14.15pt;margin-left:63.8pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>iii. and use the balance of net proceeds to make investments in capital expenditures.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The value of such financial debt is based on its amortized cost calculated as cash flows discounted at an effective rate of 7.088%.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>During 2022, the Company repurchased its marketable debt for a nominal value of USD 29.7 million for which it paid Ps. 7,637,322. This transaction generated a result of (Ps. 3,208,025), recognized in the financial results of the Statement of Income.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-22.7pt;margin-left:51.9pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Lease liability</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>It corresponds to the financing obtained for the acquisition of the assets corresponding to the treatment and compression plant located in the R&#237;o Neuqu&#233;n area. Said agreement was entered into on August 11, 2016, with Petrobras (currently Pampa Energ&#237;a) and consists of the payment of 119 consecutive monthly installments of US$ 623,457 without taxes and a purchase option for the same amount payable at the end of the 120th month of validity of the agreement. contract.</P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;color:#0070C0'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:-21pt'><B>14.</B></KBD><B>INCOME TAX AND DEFERRED TAX</B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I> Tax adjustment for inflation</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt'>Law No. 27,468 establishes that in the net taxable income of the periods beginning on or after January 1, 2018, the adjustment for inflation obtained by the application of the income tax law may be deducted or incorporated into the tax result for the fiscal year. This adjustment will proceed only if the percentage variation in the IPC, will accumulate (a) a percentage higher than 100% in the 36 months prior to the end of the year, or (b) regarding the first, second and third fiscal year that starts from its effective date, an accumulated variation of the IPC that exceeds 55%, 30% or 15% of said 100%, respectively. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt'>For the fiscal years ended December 31, 2023 and 2022, the CPI has exceeded the 100% threshold mentioned above, so the Company has measured the tax charge to earnings for the years ended December 31, 2023 and 2022 considering the application of the adjustment for fiscal inflation. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt'>According to the Solidarity Law, the positive or negative result generated by the application of the inflation adjustment corresponding to the first and second fiscal year beginning on January 1, 2019 will be charged in a sixth in that fiscal period and the five sixths remaining in equal parts in the following 5 fiscal periods. As of January 1, 2021, 100% of the adjustment may be deducted/taxed in the year in which the effect is determined.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>On November 16, 2022, the Senate of the Nation approved the 2023 budget bill under Law No. 27,701, which already had half a sanction from the Chamber of Deputies.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The 2023 budget law incorporates article 195 to the income tax law establishing that in the event that the adjustment for tax inflation is applicable and a positive adjustment (profits) arises from it, the first and second fiscal years starting on 1 January 2022 inclusive, they may allocate 1/3 in that fiscal period and the remaining 2/3, in equal parts, in the 2 immediately following fiscal periods. For the deferral to be appropriate, it will be necessary for companies to make an investment in the purchase, construction, manufacture, processing or definitive import of fixed assets (except automobiles), during each of the 2 fiscal periods immediately following that of the calculation of the first third of the period in question, for an amount equal to or greater than $30,000,000,000.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>As of December 31, 2023, the Company projected investments in PPE in accordance with the requirements mentioned above, determining that the amounts exceed what is established by law.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'><I> </I><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B><I>Adjustment of acquisitions and investments made in fiscal years beginning on or after January 1, 2018</I></B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt'>A cost adjustment mechanism is established for assets acquired or investments made in fiscal years beginning on or after January 1, 2018. The adjustment will be made based on the percentage variations of the IPIM. </P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Extraordinary tax RG N&#176; 5268/2022</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt'>On August 16, 2022, RG No. 5268/2022 was published through which the AFIP ordered an extraordinary advance of income tax. The extraordinary advance payment constitutes an additional payment on account to that provided for in the general advance payment regime that can be added to the latter. For fiscal year 2022, the Company paid the AFIP Ps. 10,487,129 for this concept.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>47</P>

<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;color:#0070C0'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:-21pt'><B>16.</B></KBD><B>FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT</B>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt;color:#0070C0'><B>16.1 Financial risk factors</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>The Company&#8217;s activities and the market in which it operates expose it to a series of financial risks: market risk (including foreign exchange risk, interest rate risk, and commodity price risk), credit risk and liquidity risk. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>The Company's risk management framework establishes that a risk map is determined that measures the potential impact of each of them on the financial situation and results of operations. Based on this, the Executive Officers are responsible for defining the policies, procedures, limits and measures aimed at mitigating the impact of said risks.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>The sensitivity analyzes included below are based on the change in one of the factors while all others remain constant. In practice, this is unlikely to happen, and changes in several factors can be correlated, for example, in variations in the interest rate and variations in the foreign currency exchange rate.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>Sensitivity analysis only provides limited vision, at one point in time. The actual impact on the Company's financial instruments could vary significantly with respect to the impact shown in the sensitivity analysis.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:6pt;margin-left:36pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:6pt;margin-left:36pt;color:#0070C0'><B>16.1.1 Risk associated with exchange rates</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Exchange rate risk management</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>In view of the main impacts of the aforementioned situation and those detailed in Note 1 to these Consolidated Financial Statements, the Company has implemented a series of measures to mitigate their impact. In this sense, the Company's Management permanently monitors the evolution of the situations that affect its business, in order to determine the possible actions to be taken and identify the eventual impacts on its equity and financial situation. The Company considers that its current financial position </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>49</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=right style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>English translation of the original prepared in Spanish for publication in Argentina</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'><FONT style=font-size:9pt>will allow it to comply, in the short term, with its foreign currency commitments. The Company's financial statements should be read in light of these circumstances.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>The Company is primarily exposed to the fluctuation of the exchange rate of the U.S. dollar against the Argentine Peso due to the fact that almost its entire financial indebtedness is denominated in U.S. dollars. The exposure to other currencies is not significant.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>As regards to the revenue derived from the Natural Gas Transportation segment, the tariffs charged by the Company are currently denominated in Argentine pesos. On the other hand, revenues in US dollars derived from the Liquids Production and Commercialization segment accounted for approximately 87% of the segment&#8217;s total revenues for the years ended December 31, 2023 and 2022. Total operating cost denominated in Argentine Pesos accounted for 81% and 83% for the years ended December 31, 2023 and 2022, respectively.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>As of December 31, 2023 and 2022, 35% of total revenues are denominated in Argentine pesos for both years.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'><B>tgs</B>' financial risk management policies are defined with the objective of mitigating the impact of exchange rate fluctuations on the Company's foreign currency position. To this end, alternative investment evaluations are regularly carried out to diversify tgs' investment portfolio among instruments denominated in U.S. dollars or, although denominated in Argentine pesos, to obtain positive returns in real terms.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>Additionally, if deemed appropriate, the Company enters into derivative financial instruments that allow hedging the fluctuation of the U.S. dollar on the positions in such currency in the long term. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>However, the Company, in order to mitigate the impact on the future variation of the exchange rate, has placed funds in assets denominated in U.S. dollars. As of December 31, 2023, 91% of the Company's fund placements are denominated in U.S. dollars. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>For further information regarding the Company's foreign currency position see &quot;Note 18. Foreign currency assets and liabilities&quot;.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>Management of the Company estimates that, based on the net liability position as of December 31, 2023 and 2022, a 10% appreciation in the exchange rate of the U.S. dollar against the Argentine peso, with all other economic-financial variables stable, could have resulted in a pre-tax loss of Ps. 6,378,820 and Ps. 6,152,241, respectively. A 10% depreciation of the U.S. dollar against the Argentine peso would have an equal and opposite effect on the Statement of Comprehensive Income. This sensitivity analysis is theoretical as the actual impacts could differ significantly and vary over time.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>In order to mitigate the exchange rate risk, during fiscal year 2023, <B>tgs</B> entered into both forward purchase operations of US dollars, as well as investments in mutual funds linked to the US dollar in order to hedge exposure to the risk associated with the exchange rate that derives from its financial debt.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.15pt;margin-right:28.35pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.15pt;margin-right:28.35pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.15pt;margin-right:28.35pt;color:#0070C0'><B>16.1.2 Interest rate risk</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>Interest rate risk management seeks to reduce financial costs and limit the Company's exposure to increases in interest rates. <B>tgs</B>' exposure to risks associated with interest rate variations is limited given that all of its financial debt is subject to fixed interest rates. Information regarding the Company's financing is disclosed in Note 13.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>50</P>

<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=right style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>In addition, the main objective of the Company's financial investment activities is to obtain the highest return by investing in low-risk and highly liquid instruments. The Company maintains a portfolio of cash equivalents and short-term investments comprised of investments in mutual funds and deposits in interest-bearing bank accounts, public and private securities. The risk of these instruments is low since they are mostly short-term and highly liquid in recognized financial institutions.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>As a consequence of the application of IAS 29, maintaining monetary assets generates loss of purchasing power, provided that such items are not subject to an adjustment mechanism that compensates to some extent the loss of purchasing power. This loss of purchasing power is included in the result of the period under gain on the net monetary position. On the contrary, maintaining monetary liabilities generates a gain in purchasing power, which are also included in such line item.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>The Company's risk management policies are defined with the objective of reducing the impact of the loss of purchasing power. During the 2023 and 2022 fiscal years the Company has maintained a liability monetary position. As a consequence, <B>tgs</B> has recorded a net gain from exposure to inflation in the monetary items.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>The following table shows a breakdown of the Company&#8217;s fixed-rate and floating-rate financial assets and liabilities as of December 31, 2023 and 2022:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_125.jpg width=506 height=66 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>In view of the nature of the Company&#8217;s financial assets which bear variable interest, an immediate 100 basis points decrease in the interest rate would not have a significant impact on the total value of the financial assets.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:6pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:6pt;margin-left:36pt;color:#0070C0'><B>16.1.3 Commodity price risk</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>Commercial operations performed by the Company in its Liquids Production and Commercialization business segment are affected by a number of factors beyond its control, including changes in the international prices of the products sold, and government regulations on prices, taxes and other charges, among others.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>The sales prices of exported propane, butane and natural gasoline are determined according to international reference prices (Mont Belvieu for propane and butane and NWE ARA for natural gasoline). Additionally, most of the total sales of propane and butane that are made in the domestic market are made at prices set by the Secretary of Energy for the different market segments. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>These prices have historically fluctuated in response to macroeconomic conditions and changes in supply and demand, which could affect <B>tgs</B>' profitability. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>Based on volume of sales for the years ended December 31, 2023 and 2022, <B>tgs</B> estimated that, other factors being constant, a decrease of US$ 50 per ton in the international price of LPG and natural gasoline, respectively, would have decrease the Company&#8217;s net comprehensive income in its Liquids Production and Commercialization segment in Ps. 13,547,004 and Ps. 9,987,506, respectively. On the other hand, an increase of US$ 50 per ton in the international price would have had the opposite effect.</P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><B>16.1.4 Credit risk</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:36pt'>The Company&#8217;s exposures to credit risk takes the form of a loss that would be recognized if counterparties failed to, or were unable to, meet their payment obligations. These risks may arise in certain agreements in relation to amounts owed for physical product sales, the use of derivative instruments, and the investment of surplus cash balances. This risk mainly results from economic and financial factors or from a possible default of counterparty.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:36pt'>The Company is subject to credit risk arising from outstanding receivables, cash and cash equivalents and deposits with banks and financial institutions, and from the use of derivative financial instruments. The Company&#8217;s policy is to manage credit exposure to trading counterparties within defined trading limits. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:36pt'>To measure the expected credit loss, receivables from sales have been grouped according to their characteristics in terms of credit risk and the time that has elapsed since maturity.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:36pt'>On this basis, the provision for losses for the year ended December 31, 2023 for trade receivables was determined as follows:</P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Trade and other receivables</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:10pt;margin-left:36pt'>If any of the Company&#8217;s customers are independently rated, these ratings are used. Otherwise, if there is no independent rating, the Company assesses the credit quality of the customer taking into account its financial position, past experience and other factors. The Company may seek cash collateral, letter of credit or parent company guarantees, as considered appropriate.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt'>As of December 31, 2023 and 2022, current and non-current sales receivables, net of allowance for doubtful accounts, amounted to:</P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt'>The credit risk on cash and cash equivalents and other financial placements is limited since <B>tgs</B> has short-term fund placement policies whose main objective is to obtain an adequate return based on market characteristics and minimizing risk exposure. The Company's maximum exposure to credit risk will be given by the carrying value of assets included in cash and cash equivalents and other financial assets measured at amortized cost.</P>

<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><B>16.1.5 Liquidity risk</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>This risk involves the difficulties that <B>tgs</B> may have in meeting its commercial and financial obligations. To this end, the expected cash flow is regularly monitored.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'><B>tgs</B> has policies for borrowing funds whose main objective is to cover financing needs at the lowest cost according to market conditions. One of the Company's main objectives is to have financial solvency. Given the current conditions of the financial market, the Company believes that the availability of resources and the positive cash flow from operations are sufficient to meet its current obligations, despite having credit lines for borrowing funds.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>Additionally, a methodology is used for the analysis and assignment of credit limits to the different financial entities in order to minimize the associated liquidity risk. In line with this, the Company invests its liquid funds in financial entities with an adequate credit rating.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>Below is a detail of the maturities of the Company's financial liabilities corresponding to: commercial debts, remunerations, other debts and financial debts as of December 31, 2023 and 2022. These estimates are made on the basis of information available at the end of each year and may not reflect actual amounts in the future. <P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><B>16.1.6 Capital management risk</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>The Company's objectives in managing capital are to safeguard the Company's ability to continue as a going concern, to achieve an optimal cost of capital structure and to support the investment process in order to provide returns to shareholders and benefits to other stakeholders.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'><B>tgs</B> seeks to maintain a level of cash generation from its operating activities that will enable it to meet all of its commitments.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>The Company monitors capital on the basis of the leverage ratio. This ratio is calculated as total financial debt (including &quot;current financial debt&quot; and &quot;non-current financial debt&quot; as shown in the Statement of Financial Position) divided by total capital. Total capital is calculated as &quot;Shareholders' Equity&quot;, as shown in the Statement of Changes in Shareholders' Equity, plus total financial debt. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>

<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'><B>16.2.2</B><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman;color:#000000'> </FONT><B>Fair value measurement hierarchy and estimates</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>According to Accounting Standards IFRS 13, the fair value hierarchy introduces three levels of inputs based on the lowest level of input significant to the overall fair value. These levels are:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:6pt;margin-bottom:6pt;margin-left:32.2pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#183;</FONT></KBD><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Level 1</B></FONT>: includes financial assets and liabilities whose fair values are estimated using quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets for identical assets and liabilities. The instruments included in this level primarily include balances in mutual funds and public or private bonds listed on the <I>Bolsas y Mercados Argentinos S.A.</I> (&#8220;BYMA&#8221;). Mutual funds mainly invest in highly liquid instruments with low price risk.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:32.2pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#183;</FONT></KBD><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Level 2</B></FONT>: includes financial assets and liabilities whose fair value is estimated using different assumptions quoted prices included within Level 1 that are observable for the asset or liability, either directly (i.e. as prices) or indirectly (for example, derived from prices). Within this level, the Company includes those derivate financial instruments for which it was not able to find an active market.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:32.2pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:32.2pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#183;</FONT></KBD><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>Level 3</B></FONT>: includes financial instruments for which the assumptions used in estimating fair value are not based on observable market information.&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:36pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>During 2023 and 2022, there were no transfers between the different hierarchies used to determine the fair value of the Company's financial instruments or reclassifications between categories of financial instruments.</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The tables below show different assets at their fair value classified by hierarchy as of December 31, 2023 and 2022:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'> <FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_137.jpg width=576 height=65 alt='Picture 11' title='Picture 11'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;text-indent:28.35pt;margin-left:14.2pt'> &nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt'><IMG src=tgs_138.jpg width=576 height=70 alt='Picture 12' title='Picture 12'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The fair value amount of the financial assets is the price that would be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market participants at the measurement date.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>As of December 31, 2023, the carrying amount of certain financial instruments used by the Company, in cash, cash equivalents, other investments, accounts receivable and payable and short-term obligations is representative of fair value due to the short-term nature of these instruments. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The estimated fair value of Non-current loans is estimated based on quoted market prices. The following table reflects the carrying amount and estimated fair value of the 2018 Notes at December 31, 2023, based on their quoted market price: </P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><B>17. REGULATORY FRAMEWORK</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;text-indent:28.35pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><B>a.</B></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD><B>Regulatory framework of the Natural Gas Transportation Segment</B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>General Aspects</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Regarding <B>tgs</B>&#8217; Natural Gas Transportation business, it is regulated by Law No. 24,076 (&#8220;the Natural Gas Act&#8221;), its regulatory Decree No. 1,738/92 and the Regulatory framework for the transportation and distribution of natural gas in Argentina. The Natural Gas Act created ENARGAS, which is entitled, among other things, to set the basis for the calculation, monitoring, approval of tariffs and the power to verify compliance with the Natural Gas Law and its regulations. On January 28, 2016, Resolution No. 7 of the Ministry of Energy and Mining (&#8220;MINEM&#8221;) of Argentina repealed the Resolution 2000/2005 of the former Ministry of Federal Planning, Public Investment and Services (&#8220;ex MPFIPyS&#8221;) which provided that all tariff increases should have the prior intervention of the Undersecretary of Coordination and Management Control.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'><B>tgs&#180; </B>License has been granted for an original period of 35 years beginning on December 28, 1992. However, the Natural Gas Act provides that <B>tgs</B> may apply to ENARGAS for a renewal of its license for an additional period of ten years. ENARGAS should evaluate at that time the performance of <B>tgs</B> and raise a recommendation to the Executive Branch. At the end of the period of validity of the license, 35 or 45, as appropriate, the Natural Gas Act requires a call for a new tender for the granting of a new license, in which <B>tgs</B>, provided the Company fulfilled substantially with the obligations resulting from the license, would have the option of matching the best offer that the Government received in this bidding process.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On September 8, 2023, <B>tgs</B> submitted to ENARGAS the request to initiate the procedure contemplated in Law No. 24,076 by which, once the regulated procedures and administrative procedure established for this purpose have been completed, <B>tgs</B> is granted the extension of the license term for ten years from the expiration date of its initial term (December 28, 2027) for the provision of the gas transportation service, contemplating the entire scope of the license approved by Decree No. 2458/92.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>In that presentation, <B>tgs</B> requested that the planned measures be adopted regarding performance evaluation and public hearing, so that once the regulated procedures and administrative procedure established for this purpose have been completed, <B>tgs</B> is granted an extension of the license term for ten years from the expiration date.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>As of the date of issuance of these Financial Statements, the procedure continues without having received a formal response from ENARGAS.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Tariff situation</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Prior the enactment of the Public Emergency Law, and according to the Regulatory Framework, transportation tariffs were to be calculated in US dollars and converted into Argentine pesos at the time the customer was billed using the exchange rate prevailing at the date of the billing. The basic natural gas transportation tariffs charged by <B>tgs</B> had been established at the time of the privatization of GdE and were to be adjusted, subject to prior authorization, in the following cases: (i) semi-annually to reflect changes in the US producer price index (&#8220;PPI&#8221;) and (ii) every five years according to efficiency and investment factors determined by ENARGAS. The &#8220;efficiency factor&#8221; was a reduction to the base tariff resulting from future efficiency programs while the &#8220;investment factor&#8221; increased the tariffs to compensate the licensees for future investments which were not repaid through tariffs. Also, subject to ENARGAS approval, tariffs were to be adjusted to reflect non-recurrent circumstances or tax changes, other than income tax.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>The terms and conditions as described in the precedent paragraph in connection with tariff adjustments contemplated within the Regulatory Framework are no longer effective since </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>59</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=right style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>English translation of the original prepared in Spanish for publication in Argentina</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'><FONT style=font-size:9pt>the enactment of the Public Emergency Law in early 2002, which, among other provisions, eliminated tariff increases based on US dollar exchange rate fluctuations, foreign price indexes or any other indexing procedure and established a conversion rate of one peso to one US dollar for tariffs. The Public Emergency Law also granted the Executive Branch power to renegotiate contracts entered into with private utility companies, pursuant to the framework included in such law as long as it is in force, which expired on December 31, 2017, after several extensions. </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Between July 2003 and March 2018, the Company received a series of temporary tariff increases under the framework of the Integral Tariff Review (&#8220;RTI&#8221; for its acronym in Spanish) process initiated after the Public Emergency Law was passed.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>On March 27, 2018, the Executive Branch of the Argentine Government issued the Decree No. 250/2018 (the &#8220;Decree 250&#8221;) ratifying the 2017 Integral Agreement. This decree represents the conclusion of the RTI process and the completion of the 2017 Transitional Agreement, and thus, the final renegotiation of the license after seventeen years of negotiations. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>The 2017 Integral Agreement sets the guidelines for the provision of the natural gas transportation service until the end of the License. Among these guidelines:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:71.45pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>The RTI Process, which will culminate in the signing of the integral agreement, was approved. As a result of this RTI, a new tariff schedule was also approved. This new tariff schedule applicable to the Company determined a total tariff increase of 214.2% and 37%, in the event that it had been granted in a single installment as of April 1, 2017, on the tariff of the natural gas transportation service and the CAU, respectively.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:71.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:6pt;margin-left:71.45pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>A Five-Year Investment Plan is approved. Resolution 4362 obliged <B>tgs</B> for the execution of the Five-Year Plan, which requires a high level of essential investments for the operation and maintenance of the pipeline system, to provide quality, safe and reliable service. The Five-Year Plan shall be for the period from April 1, 2017 to March 31, 2022 and will amount to Ps. 6,786,543, expressed in December 31, 2016 currency terms. &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:6pt;margin-left:71.45pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD>A non-automatic six-month adjustment mechanism for the natural gas transportation tariff and the investment commitments were approved. This adjustment must be approved by ENARGAS and for its calculation, the evolution of the WPI published by INDEC will be considered. &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:71.45pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'>&#183;</KBD><B>tgs</B> and its shareholders must withdraw any claim against the Government related to the natural gas transportation business, including the arbitration proceedings before the ICSID. The Company desisted from it on June 26, 2018.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:71.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Semi-annual tariff increase</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>This increase is granted within the framework of the semi-annual tariff adjustment of the natural gas transportation service in accordance with the provisions of the RTI process.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>In the public hearing held on September 4, 2018, in which the Company requested, based on the variation of the WPI recorded for the period February - August 2018, a tariff increase of approximately 30%. Considering the hearing, on September 27, 2018, ENARGAS issued Resolution No. 265/2018 which determined a 19.7% tariff increase effective as of October 1, 2018.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>This increase was determined by ENARGAS based on the simple average of the WPI, the Construction Cost Index for the period February and August 2018 and the Salary Variation Index between December 2017 and June 2018.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>60</P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>It is noteworthy that ENARGAS supported the determination of the aforementioned tariff increase in the provisions of Resolution 4362, which, among other issues, provided that under certain circumstances and macroeconomic conditions, such as the significant devaluation occurred after April 2018, ENARGAS may use other indexes than the WPI to determine the tariff increase. <B>tgs</B> notified ENARGAS its disagreement with respect to the methodology for calculating the semi-annual adjustment.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>On March 29, 2019, ENARGAS issued Resolution No. 192/2019 (&quot;Resolution 192&quot;) that approved, effective as of April 1, 2019, a 26% increase in the tariff chart applicable to the public service of natural gas transportation by <B>tgs</B> in force as of March 31, 2019.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>In accordance with current regulations, ENARGAS has considered the evolution of the wholesale price index update index (&quot;WPI&quot;) between the months of August 2018 and February 2019, in order to define the semi-annual adjustments applicable to <B>tgs</B> tariffs.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Regarding the semi-annual tariff adjustment that was to be enforced as from October 1, 2019, on September 3, 2019, the SE issued Resolution No. 521/2019 (&#8220;Resolution 521&#8221;) postponing it to February 1, 2020.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The deferral also meant that the Company reviewed and fit, in equal proportion to the revenue that is no longer received, the execution of the Five-Year Investment Plan. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On December 16, 2020, Decree of Necessity and Urgency No. 1,020/2020 (&#8220;Decree 1,020&#8221;) was issued, through which the start of the renegotiation of the RTI concluded in 2018 within the framework of the Solidarity Law was determined.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>According to that decree, the renegotiation period could not exceed 2 years from the date of entry into force. Until then, the current renegotiation agreements were suspended. Said renegotiation remained in the power of ENARGAS ad-referendum of the PEN. The renegotiation process was to culminate with the signing of a Definitive Agreement on the RTI on December 16, 2022. However, on December 7, 2022, Necessity and Urgency Decree No. 815/2022 was published in the BO. (the &#8220;Decree 815&#8221;) which extended the renegotiation period for 12 months from the expected expiration of the period provided for in Decree 1020, and said process must now be completed on December 16, 2023.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Additionally, the Solidarity Law provides for the administrative intervention of ENARGAS, recently extended through Decree 1,020, later extended by Decree 815.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On March 16, 2021, the public hearing convened by ENARGAS was held for the purposes of considering the Transitional Tariff Regime (&#8220;RTT&#8221;) in accordance with the provisions of Decree 1,020. In this regard, <B>tgs</B>, without renouncing the full percentage of tariff recomposition that corresponds to it, alternatively presented at this hearing a proposal for a tariff increase calculated by 58.6%, as of April 1, 2021. It was calculated based on the financial needs to meet the operating and financial costs, capital investments and taxes, which were calculated considering the evolution of the inflation rate for a period of 12 months from its inception. Such requested increase contemplated only the necessary funds to meet the obligations as a licensee.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Additionally, in such public hearing, <B>tgs</B> denied and dismissed the arguments raised in it that considered that the current natural gas transportation tariff is not fair and reasonable given the alleged existence of serious defects in the administrative acts derived from the processing of the last Regime Comprehensive Tariff (&#8220;RTI&#8221;) made for <B>tgs</B>.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On April 28, 2021, ENARGAS sent to <B>tgs</B>, a Transitory Agreement Project 2021 within the framework of the provisions of Decree 1,020. Said project provided:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&#8226; The non-granting of a temporary tariff increase, keeping the tariff schedules approved by ENARGAS in April 2019 unchanged.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>61</P>
