<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&#8226; It established that from May 2021 and until the Definitive Renegotiation Agreement is in force, ENARGAS will proceed to re-calculate the transportation tariffs at that time in force with effect from April 1, 2022.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&#8226; It did not establish a mandatory investment plan.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt;color:#000000'>&#8226; The prohibition of: (i) distribution of dividends, (ii) an early cancellation of financial and commercial debts contracted with shareholders, and (iii) the acquisition of other companies or granting loans.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On April 30, 2021, through a note sent to that body, <B>tgs</B> stated that, given the context in which it develops its activities and the proposed terms and conditions, it was not feasible for the Company to sign the 2021 Temporary Agreement Project.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On June 2, 2021, ENARGAS issued Resolution No. 149/2021 (the &#8220;Resolution 149&#8221;) approving an RTT for the year 2021 (the &#8220;RTT 2021&#8221;) for <B>tgs</B> effective as of that date. On the other hand, the Ministry of Economy and ENARGAS issued Joint Resolution No. 1/2021 where they approved the actions taken in the renegotiation process developed by ENARGAS in the terms of Decree No. 1020/2020, indicating that it was not feasible to arrive to an agreement on a transitional tariff adjustment.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>In July 2021, <B>tgs</B> filed appeals for reconsideration with the PEN, the Ministry of Economy of the Nation and ENARGAS with an appeal or hierarchical subsidy in accordance with the respective powers of each of these bodies in the dictation of the entire regulatory plexus associated with Resolution 149, (Decree 1020/20, Decree 353/21, and Joint Resolution No. 1/2021), requesting that the RTT be declared null and void and the RTI be restored. We recall that the RTT began with Decree 1020/20, which set the guidelines to carry out the renegotiation of the RTI, later issuing Joint Resolution 1/21, which sets the RTT, and Decree 353/21, which approved Joint Resolution 1 /21, and culminates with Resolution 149, which approved the new tariff charts.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>The challenges are based on: i) the illegality of Decree 1020 because it does not follow the lines of the delegation provided for in Law 27,541, and as a Decree of Necessity and Urgency it does not comply with the requirements of the Constitution for the issuance of this regulation; ii) the extension of the emergency beyond that provided by Congress; iii) the failure to carry out the tariff renegotiation as provided for in Law 24. 076; iv) the disregard of the principle of fair and reasonable tariffs, and the acquired rights of <B>tgs</B> in the License, the Contractual Adjustment Agreement and the RTI; and v) the suspension of the RTI for reasons of public interest, which merits the recognition of the compensations, provided by both the Administrative Procedures Law and the Basic Rules of the License.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>The restrictions imposed on the management and management of the Company, which have no legal justification, are also questioned, given that the emergency declared by Law No. 27,541 only empowered the National Executive Power to renegotiate the RTI and not the License.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>The challenges and the request for reinstatement of the RTI have been made without prejudice to the right of <B>tgs</B> to pay the compensation that corresponds to it for not having complied with the RTI since April 2019.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On November 15, 2021, the Company filed a Prior Administrative Claim before ENARGAS and the Ministry of Economy. The purpose of said presentation is to request the compensation that corresponds to <B>tgs</B> for the lack of application of the semi-annual adjustment methodology established in the RTI approved by Resolution 362 between October 1, 2019 and June 1, 2021.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>In addition, payment of compensation for the damages suffered by <B>tgs</B> due to the freezing caused by the failure to apply the six-monthly adjustment methodology established in the RTI in the aforementioned period is requested.</P>

<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On January 19, 2022, a new public hearing was held, within the framework of ENARGAS Resolution No. 518/2021, whose purpose was to deal with a transitory tariff adjustment within the framework of Decree 1020/2020. In such hearing, with the purpose of reaching a definitive agreement on the renegotiation and recomposing the license economic-financial equation, <B>tgs</B> requested a transitory tariff adjustment applied in two stages for the year 2022 for a total of 106%, due to the evolution of operating costs and the main macroeconomic indicators.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Subsequently, on February 1, 2022, <B>tgs</B> received from ENARGAS a proposal for a Transitional Renegotiation Agreement (the &#8220;2022 Transition Agreement&#8221;) which was approved by our Board of Directors on February 2, 2022 and by the corresponding government agencies on February 18, 2022. The 2022 Transition Agreement includes, in some aspects, similar terms to the 2021 RTT with the particularity that it grants <B>tgs</B> a 60% tariff increase effective as from March 1, 2022 (the &#8220;RTT 2022&#8221;).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>The 2022 Transition Agreement was ratified by the PEN through Decree No. 91/2022, which entered into force as from February 23, 2022. On February 25, 2022, the ENARGAS issued Resolution No. 60/2022 by which it put into effect the tariff schedules that contemplate the RTT 2022.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>It should be noted that, in accordance with the provisions of the 2022 Transition Agreement, <B>tgs</B> committed not to initiate new claims, appeals, actions, lawsuits or demands of any kind; and/or to suspend, keep suspended or extend the suspension of all appeals and claims filed that are related in any way to the Renegotiation of the Comprehensive Tariff Review in force, Law No. 27,541, Decrees No. 278/20 and No. 1020.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Under Decrees 1020 and 815, on December 7, 2022, ENARGAS issued Resolution No. 523/2022 which called for a public hearing on January 4, 2023 to, among other issues, consider the transitory adjustment of tariffs for the public natural gas transportation service.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On March 16, 2023, the TGS Board of Directors approved the proposed addendum to the transitional renegotiation agreement (the &quot;2023 Transition Agreement&quot;) sent by ENARGAS. This addendum was subsequently ratified by the PEN through Decree No. 250/2023 of April 29, 2023. Previously, on April 27, 2023, ENARGAS issued Resolution No. 186/2023 through which the new tables were published. current tariffs.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>The 2023 Transition Agreement has similar conditions to the 2022 Transition Agreement and includes:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>- As of April 29, 2023, a temporary rate increase of 95% on the natural gas transportation rate and the CAU.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>- During its term, <B>tgs</B> may not under any circumstances: a) Distribute dividends; nor b) Cancel in advance directly or indirectly financial and commercial debts contracted with shareholders, acquire other companies or grant credits, unless the credits benefit the users or are granted to contractors that do not fall within the aforementioned assumptions. In the event that <B>tgs</B> deems it appropriate to proceed in a direction contrary to the provisions of paragraph a) above, attaching the corresponding supporting documentation, it will request ENARGAS to submit a status report to the Ministry of Economy, so that the Ministry may authorize or no, and if applicable, determine the manner and form of the request. In the event that <B>tgs</B> considers it appropriate to proceed in a direction contrary to the provisions of paragraph b) above, it must also present to ENARGAS documented grounds that support such decision, for authorization. If a covert operation to distribute dividends is noticed, the request will be made to the Ministry of Economy for its substantiation in that sphere. To this end, the Ministry will notify its decision to the applicant and ENARGAS.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On December 14, 2023, ENARGAS Resolution No. 704/2023 called for a public hearing to be held on January 8, 2024. As a result of said hearing, on February 15, 2024, ENARGAS Resolution N&#176; 52/2024 was issued by means of which the validity of the public hearing was </P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'><FONT style=font-size:9pt>ratified and ENARGAS was given a deadline of up to 30 working days as from the issuance of this Resolution to issue the transitory tariff charts that arose from the hearing.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On December 16, 2023, decree No. 55/2023 was issued declaring the emergency of the national energy sector until December 31, 2024. Among other issues, this decree: (i) establishes the beginning of the process of RTI, (ii) the intervention of ENARGAS as of January 1, 2024 and (iii) instructs the Ministry of Energy to issue the necessary standards and procedures for the sanction of market prices for the public transport service of natural gas.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#0070C0'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:-21.25pt'><FONT style='border-bottom:1px solid #0070C0'><B>b)</B></FONT></KBD><FONT style='border-bottom:1px solid #0070C0'><B>General framework for non-regulated segments</B></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Domestic market</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>The Production and Commercialization of Liquids and Midstream segments are not subject to regulation by ENARGAS, and as it is provided in the Transfer Agreement, is organized as a separate business unit within <B>tgs</B>, keeping accounting information separately. However, over recent years, the Argentine Government enacted regulations which significantly impacted on it.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>In April 2005, the Argentine Government enacted Law No. 26,020 which <FONT style=background-color:#FFFFFF>sets forth the regulatory framework for the industry and commercialization of LPG. Among other things, the Law No. 26,020 creates the </FONT>framework through which the Secretary of Hydrocarbon Resources (&#8220;SHR&#8221;) (formerly the Federal Energy Bureau) establishes regulations meant to cause LPG suppliers to guarantee sufficient supply of LPG in the domestic market at low prices. Law No. 26,020 creates a price regime pursuant to which the SRH periodically publish reference prices for LPG sold in the domestic market. It also sets forth LPG volumes to be sold in the domestic market.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>Within this framework, <B>tgs</B> sells propane and butane production to fractionators at prices determined every six months. On March 30, 2015, the Executive Branch issued Decree No. 470/2015, regulated by Resolution No. 49/2015 issued by the Federal Energy Bureau, which created the <I>Programa Hogares con Garrafa </I>(the &#8220;Households with Bottles Program&#8221;) which replaced the programs in force until that time. The Households with Bottles Program was implemented through Resolutions No. 49/2015 and No. 470/2015 issued by the Federal Energy Bureau.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>Within the framework of the Households with Bottles Program, a maximum reference price was determined for the members of the commercialization chain in order to guarantee the supply to low-income residential users, forcing producers to supply with LPG at a certain price and at a certain quantity defined for each of them. Additionally, the payment of compensation to the participating producers of the Households with Bottles Program was established.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>In this context, the Company has filed several administrative and judicial claims challenging the general regulations of the Home Plan, as well as the administrative acts that determine the volumes of butane to be sold in the local market, in order to safeguard its economic-financial situation and so that such situation does not continue in time.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'> &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Additionally, the Company participates in the Propane Gas Supply Agreement for Indiluted Propane Gas Distribution Networks (&quot;Propane for Networks Agreement&quot;) entered into with the Argentine Government and propane producing companies whereby it undertakes to supply propane to distributors and sub-distributors of indiluted propane gas through networks at a price lower than the market price, The Company receives an economic compensation for the lower revenues derived from the participation in this program, which is calculated as the difference between the agreed sales price and the reference export parity price determined by the Energy Secretariat.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>64</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=right style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>English translation of the original prepared in Spanish for publication in Argentina</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On August 10, 2021, <B>tgs</B> celebrated the eighteenth extension, valid until December 31, 2021. Regarding the agreement valid until December 31, 2022, said document was signed on June 23, 2022. During much of 2023 were carried out under the terms of the 2022 agreement in accordance with an instruction from the SE. Finally, on August 18, 2023, <B>tgs</B> signed, together with the Ministry of Energy and other LPG producers, the twentieth extension of the Propane Agreement for Networks, which was ratified by the PEN through decree 496/2023 on October 2, 2023. 2023</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>As mentioned above, participation in the Home Plan implies economic-financial losses for the Company, since under certain circumstances the sale of the products would be made at prices that are below production costs.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>As of December 31, 2023, the Argentine State owes <B>tgs</B> Ps. 4,675,875 for these concepts.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Foreign market</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>On September 3, 2018, the Executive Branch issued Decree No. 793/2018, which, between September 4, 2018 and December 31, 2020, sets an export duty of 12% on the exported amount of propane, butane and natural gasoline. This withholding is capped at $4 for each dollar of the tax base or the official FOB price.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Subsequently, on the occasion of the enactment of the Solidarity Law, an 8% cap was established for the rate applicable to hydrocarbons as of December 23, 2019.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Presidential Decree No. 488/2020 regulated the applicable tax rate on exports of certain oil and gas products, including those products that we export, establishing a range between 0% and 8% depending on the &quot;ICE Brent first line&quot; barrel price. If the price is below US$ 45, the tax rate will be zero. On the other hand, if the price is equal to or higher than US$ 60, an 8% tax rate will be paid, being variable if the price is between US$ 45 and US$ 60.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>During 2023, the Company participated, within the framework of Resolution No. 808/2023 of the Ministry of Energy, of the Export Increase Program created by Decree 576/22.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>According to said program, the Company must enter 75% of the value of its exports in foreign currency into the country. The remaining 25% may be settled in pesos through the purchase of negotiable securities. To qualify for the scheme, exports must be settled between October 2 and October 20, 2023, and the effective export date must not be later than November 30, 2023.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Subsequently, said program was extended by Decree No. 28/2023, which also modifies the settlement ratio, which is currently 80% through the MULC and the remaining 20% <FONT style=font-family:Arial>&#8203;&#8203;</FONT>through sales and purchase operations of negotiable securities.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Decree No. 2,067 / 08 (the &#8220;Decree&#8221;)</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>Through Presidential Decree No. 2,067/08, the Executive Branch created a tariff charge to be paid by: (i) the users of regulated services of transportation and / or distribution, (ii) natural gas consumers receiving natural gas directly from producers without making use of transportation systems or natural gas distribution, (iii) the natural gas processing companies in order to finance the import of natural gas. The tariff charge sets forth in this decree finance the higher price of the natural gas imports required to compensate the injection of natural gas&#160;necessary to meet national requirements (the &#8220;Charge&#8221;). When the Charge was created, <B>tgs</B> paid it in accordance with the provisions of Resolution I-563/2008, at Ps. 0.0492/m3.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>The payment of the natural gas processing tariff charge was selectively subsidized from 2008 according to the destination of the natural gas. In November 2011, however, ENARGAS issued Resolution No. 1,982/11 and 1,991/11 (the &#8220;Subsidy Beneficiaries Resolutions&#8221;) </P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>65</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=right style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>English translation of the original prepared in Spanish for publication in Argentina</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'><FONT style=font-size:9pt>which modified the list of the subsidy beneficiaries, and thus, involved a cost increase for many of our clients and for us (for certain of our consumption for our own account). The natural gas processing tariff charge increased from Ps. 0.0492 to Ps. 0.405 per cubic meter of natural gas effective from December 1, 2011, representing a significant increase in our variable costs of natural gas processing.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>In order to avoid this damage, <B>tgs</B> appealed against the Presidential Decree and the Resolutions including National Government, ENARGAS and ex MPFIPyS as defendants. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>For further information regarding the legal action filed the Company, see Note 20.b).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>On March 28, 2016, the former MINEM issued Resolution No. 28/16 (&#8220;Resolution 28&#8221;), which instructs ENARGAS to take all the necessary measures to derogate the tariff charge created by Decree No. 2,067/08 as from April 1, 2016. However, such Resolution does not repeal or declare illegitimate this decree and the Subsidy Beneficiaries Resolutions for which the judicial action is still ongoing.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#1F497D'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#0070C0'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:-21.25pt'><FONT style='border-bottom:1px solid #0070C0'><B>c)</B></FONT></KBD><FONT style='border-bottom:1px solid #0070C0'><B>Essential assets</B></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-49.6pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>A substantial portion of the assets transferred by GdE has been defined as essential for the performance of the natural gas transportation service. Therefore, <B>tgs</B> is required to keep separated and maintain these assets, together with any future improvements, in accordance with certain standards defined in the License.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:21.3pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'><B>tgs</B> may not, for any reason, dispose of, encumber, lease, sublease or loan essential assets nor use such assets for purposes other than the provision of the licensed service without ENARGAS&#180;s prior authorization. Any expansion or improvements that it makes to the gas pipeline system may only be encumbered to secure loans that have a term of more than one year to finance such extensions or improvements. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Upon expiration of the License, <B>tgs</B> will be required to transfer to the Argentine government or its designee, the essential assets listed in an updated inventory as of the expiration date, free of any debt, encumbrances or attachments. If the Company decides not to continue with the license, <B>tgs</B> will receive a compensation equal to the lower of the following two amounts:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-49.6pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:56.7pt;margin-right:2.55pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:-21.25pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>i)</B></FONT></KBD>the net book value of the essential assets determined on the basis of the price paid by the acquiring joint arrangements, and the original cost of subsequent investments carried in US dollars and adjusted by the PPI, net of accumulated depreciation according to the calculation rules to be determined by ENARGAS; or&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-21.25pt;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:51.25pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:-15.8pt'>ii)</KBD> &nbsp;the net proceeds of a new competitive bidding (the &#8220;New Bidding&#8221;).&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Once the period of the extension of the License expires, <B>tgs</B> will be entitled to participate in the New Bidding, and thus, it shall be entitled to:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:49.6pt;margin-right:8.5pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-21.2pt;margin-left:56.7pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>i)</B> &nbsp;&nbsp;</FONT>that its bid in the New Bidding be computed at an equal to the appraisal value to be determined by an investment bank selected by ENARGAS, which represents the value of the business of providing the licensed service as it is driven by the Licensee at the valuation date, as a going concern and without regard to the debts;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-21.25pt;margin-left:56.7pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0><B>ii) &nbsp;</B> </FONT>to obtain the new License, without payment, in the event that any bid submitted in the New Bidding exceeds the appraised value;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-14.15pt;margin-left:49.65pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:56.7pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:-21.25pt'>iii)</KBD>to match the best bid submitted by third parties in the New Bidding, if it would be higher than its bid mentioned in (i), paying the difference between both values <FONT style=font-family:Arial>&#8203;&#8203;</FONT>to obtain the new License; &nbsp;</P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:21.3pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On March 6, 2020, the Company's Board of Directors approved the sixth Program for the Acquisition of tgs treasury Shares in the markets where it makes a public offering of its shares (the &#8220;Sixth Repurchase Program&#8221;) for a maximum amount of Ps. 2,500 million (at the time of its creation).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:21.3pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:21.3pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Subsequently, on August 21, 2020, the Board of Directors approved a new Program for the Acquisition of tgs&#180; treasury Shares for a maximum amount to be invested in Ps. 3,000 million (at the time of its creation). This program was extended until March 22, 2021.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:21.3pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:21.3pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>The acquisition cost of the treasury shares in portfolio amounted to Ps. 34,019,849, which, together with the trading premium on treasury shares of Ps. 9,868,857, in accordance with the provisions of Title IV, Chapter III, article 3.11.c and e of the Rules, restricts the amount of realized and liquid gains mentioned above that the Company may distribute.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:21.3pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:21.3pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:-21.25pt'><B>c)</B></KBD><B>Restrictions on distribution of retained earnings</B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:21.3pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Pursuant to the General Companies Act and CNV Rules, we are required to allocate a legal reserve (&#8220;Legal Reserve&#8221;) equal to at least 5% of each year&#8217;s net income (as long as there are no losses for prior fiscal years pending to be absorbed) until the aggregate amount of such reserve equals 20% of the sum of (i) &#8220;common stock nominal value&#8221; plus (ii) &#8220;inflation adjustment to common stock,&#8221; as shown in our consolidated statement of changes in equity.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:21.3pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:21.3pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Finally, and as mentioned in subsection b of this note, the amounts subject to distribution are restricted up to the acquisition cost of treasury shares and the paid-up capital.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:21.3pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:21.3pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>In addition, according with the 2022 Transition Agreement, the Company may not distribute dividends meanwhile it will be in force.</P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>20. LEGAL CLAIMS AND OTHER MATTERS</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;text-indent:-21.25pt;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'><A name=OLE_LINK20 />&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><B>a)</B></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD><B>Turnover tax calculated on the natural gas price consumed by tgs</B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:32.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The Company has interpretative differences with several provinces regarding the liquidation of the turnover tax calculated on the natural gas used by <B>tgs</B> as fuel to render its transportation services. In this framework there have been initiated several lawsuits against <B>tgs</B>, which have adversely concluded by the Company. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>As of December 31, 2023 and 2022, the Company has recorded a provision for these concepts of Ps. 2,153.4 million and Ps. 5,161.3 million, respectively, recorded in the caption &quot;Provisions&quot;, determined based on the estimated tax and interest payable as of such date, in the event that these claims from the provinces are unfavorable for <B>tgs</B>.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><B>b)</B></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD><B>Action for annulment of ENARGAS Resolutions No. I-1,982/11 and No. I-1,991/11 (the &#8220;Resolutions&#8221;)</B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>After the issuance of the Resolutions, <B>tgs</B> filed a judicial action before the National Court of First Instance in Federal Administrative Litigation No. 1 (the &quot;Court&quot;) in order to obtain the declaration of nullity of the Presidential Decree No. 2067/08 and the Resolutions as well as the unconstitutionality of the administrative acts that created the Charge. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>On July 5, 2012, the Court issued in favor of <B>tgs</B> a precautionary measure by which the suspension of the Charge was ordered in the terms set forth in the Resolutions. This decision was appealed in different opportunities by the National Government, by virtue of which the dictating of the precautionary measure was limited to the validity of six months. However, at maturity, the Company was entitled to obtain a new precautionary measure for a similar period.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>As a result, on September 19, 2017, a new extension of the injunction was obtained (which prevented the Government to claim <B>tgs</B> of the payment of the amounts resulting from the new value of the Charge for the period between the November 2011 and March 2016), thus extending the validity until March 2018. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>On the other hand, the National Court of Appeals in Contentious Administrative Dismissal rejected the extraordinary appeal filed by the National Government against the judgment of that court that confirmed the rejection made by the Court at the request of ENARGAS to declare abstract the legal action initiated by <B>tgs</B> in accordance with the precedent &quot;Alliance&quot; issued by the Supreme Court in December 2014.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>On March 26, 2019, TGS was notified of the judgment of first instance issued by the Federal Contentious-Administrative Court No. 1 in its claim initiated by which <B>tgs</B> requested the unconstitutionality of Decree No. 2,067/08; Resolutions and of any other rule or act issued or to be issued, that is caused by the mentioned rules (the &#8220;Cause&#8221;).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>The first instance judgment declares the unconstitutionality of both Articles 53 and 54 of Law 26,784, as well as the aforementioned rules and of any other act tending to execute said provision, and consequently, the nullity of said rules (the &#8220;sentence&quot;).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>The Judgment was appealed by the National Government on March 29, 2019, and the appeal was granted on April 3, 2019, which has not been resolved as of the closing date of the year.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>On October 29, 2019, the intervening judge decided, considering what was decided in the judgment and attending to the reasons invoked by <B>tgs</B>, to extend the validity of the precautionary measure issued for six more months of processing in said ordinary process and / or until the sentence passed is firm.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>69</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>Pursuant to the isolation measures adopted by the Argentine Government under the COVID development, the judicial deadlines were suspended from March 20, 2020 until July 21, 2020. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#000000'>On December 1, 2020, the court granted a new extension of said injunction for a term of 6 months. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#000000'>On May 14, 2021, <B>tgs</B> was notified of the ruling issued by Chamber I of the Chamber in Contentious-Administrative Matters that (i) has revoked the decision by the Judge of First Instance and (ii) has imposed the costs in both instances in the order caused. <B>tgs</B> considers that it has reasonable arguments to defend its position on the substantive issue raised and, for that reason, it appealed the judgment of the Chamber.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#000000'>On June 4, 2021, <B>tgs</B> filed an extraordinary federal appeal against the judgment of the Chamber, which was contested by ENARGAS and the National State, and was granted by the Court of Appeals itself on July 14, 2021, on the understanding that &#8220;At stake is the interpretation and scope of regulations of an unquestionable federal nature, such as Decree 2067/08 and MINPLAN Resolution No. 1451/2008, ENARGAS Resolutions No. 1982/11 and 1991/11, as well as articles 53 and 54 of Law 26,784&#8221;.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#000000'>By virtue of the precautionary measure issued, and its 12 extensions obtained, as well as the favorable judgment obtained in the first instance, the existence of favorable precedents issued by the Supreme Court of Justice of the Nation (&#8220;Supreme Court&#8221;) with respect to other processors of natural gas and the granting of the extraordinary appeal that will give rise to the Supreme Court ruling on the case, the management of <B>tgs</B> and its legal advisors consider that they have solid arguments to defend their position and that it is probable that a favorable resolution will be obtained for their interests on the substantive issue raised. Therefore, no provision has been made for the eventual debt due to the increase in the charge to finance the importation of natural gas applicable to natural gas consumption related to the processing activity at the Cerri Complex for the period between the date of obtaining the measure precautionary and on April 1, 2016, date of entry into force of Resolution No. 28/2016.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;color:#000000'>This resolution has annulled the acts that determined the value of the charge established by Decree 2067, for which as of April 1, 2016, ENARGAS and the agency in charge of its collection have stopped charging the increase established by the Resolutions.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#000000'>Regarding the last extension of the precautionary measure, expired on July 1, 2021, <B>tgs</B> has not requested a new extension, due to the premature procedural stage in which the appeal filed against the judgment of the Chamber is found.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#000000'>Given the complex procedural instance, the nature of charge 2067, the background information presented in this and other legal cases initiated against charge 2067, at the date of the issuance of these Consolidated Financial Statements it is not possible to make a definitive quantification of the amount that should be pay <B>tgs</B> in the event of not obtaining a favorable ruling from the Supreme Court, while an eventual request for payment in the current circumstances may be challenged and questioned by <B>tgs</B> within the framework of the corresponding administrative and judicial instances, where the amount may be debated of the charge that is eventually required to <B>tgs</B>.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><B>c)</B></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD><B>Environmental matters</B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The Company is subject to extensive environmental regulations in Argentina. Management believes that its current operations are in material compliance with applicable environmental requirements, as currently interpreted and enforced. The Company has not incurred in any material environmental liabilities as a result of its operations to date. As of December 31, 2023 and 2022, the total amount of these provisions amounted Ps. 81.1 million and Ps. 182.2 million respectively. </P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><B>d)</B></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD><B>Others</B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.45pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>In addition to the matters discussed above, the Company is a party to certain lawsuits and administrative proceedings which involve taxation, labor claims, social security, administrative and others arising in the ordinary course of business. The Company&#8217;s Management and its legal advisors estimate that the outcome of these differences will not have significant adverse effects on the Company&#8217;s financial position or results of operations. As of December 31, 2023 and 2022, the total amount of these provisions amounted Ps. 168.4 million and Ps. 241.0 million, respectively.</P>

<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Technical, Financial and Operational Assistance Agreement</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Pampa Energ&#237;a is <B>tgs</B>&#8217; technical operator, according to the approval of ENARGAS in June 2004, and subject to the terms and conditions of the Technical Assistance Agreement which provides that Pampa Energ&#237;a is in charge of providing services related to the operation and maintenance of the natural gas transportation system and related facilities and equipment, to ensure that the performance of the system is in conformity with international standards and in compliance with certain environmental standards. For these services, the Company pays a monthly fee based on a percentage of the operating income of the Company.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>The Ordinary and Extraordinary General Shareholders&#180; Meeting held on October 17, 2019 ratified the proposal approved by the Board of Directors at its meeting on September 17, 2019 made to Pampa Energ&#237;a that implies an extension in the contract and a modification in the determination of the remuneration received by Pampa Energ&#237;a. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>Such modifications, without implying a modification in the scope of the tasks performed, mean a progressive reduction over the years in the remuneration that Pampa Energ&#237;a will receive in its role of Technical Operator.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>According to the modifications made, <B>tgs</B> will pay Pampa Energ&#237;a the greater of: (i) an annual fixed sum of US$ 0.5 million or (ii) the variable compensation that arises from applying to comprehensive income before results and income taxes for the year but after deducting also the above fixed amount) the following scheme:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:56.7pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Symbol;margin-left:-21.25pt'>&#183;</KBD>From 12/28/2019 to 12/27/2020: 6.5%&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.25pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#8226;</FONT></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.55pt></KBD>From 12/28/2020 to 12/27/2021: 6%&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.25pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#8226;</FONT></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.55pt></KBD>From 12/28/2021 to 12/27/2022: 5.5%&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.25pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#8226;</FONT></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.55pt></KBD>From 12/28/2022 to 12/27/2023: 5%&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:35.25pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><FONT style=color:#0070C0>&#8226;</FONT></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.55pt></KBD>From 12/28/2023 to 12/27/2024 and onwards: 4.5%&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'> &nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;</FONT><B>Commercial transactions</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>In the normal course of its activity, <B>tgs</B> celebrated with Pampa Energ&#237;a and other companies related to it, agreements to transfer natural gas and its richness. The price, which is denominated in US dollars, is determined according to common market practices.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>In addition, in the normal course of business, <B>tgs</B> carries out liquid sales, natural gas transportation services and other services with its associated companies, Pampa Energ&#237;a and related companies.</P>

<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Lease agreement with Pampa Energ&#237;a</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt'>As mentioned in Note 13 to these Consolidated Financial Statements, on August 11, 2016, the Company entered into a finance lease agreement with Pampa Energ&#237;a (formerly Petrobras Argentina).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:-0.05pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Key management compensation</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>The accrued amounts corresponding to the compensation of the members of the Board of Directors, the Statutory Committee and the Executive Committee for the years ended December 31, 2023 and 2022 were Ps. 1,575,206 and Ps. 1,329,757, respectively.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>

<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>The detail of significant outstanding balances for transactions entered into by <B>tgs</B> and its related parties as of December 31, 2023 and 2022 is as follows:</P>
<P align=center style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'>&nbsp;</P>

The book value of the notes is disclosed within the caption &#8220;Financial assets at fair value through profit or loss&#8221;.</P>

<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:-0.05pt'><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_144.jpg width=576 height=97 alt='Picture 19' title='Picture 19'></FONT>&nbsp;</P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:2.55pt'>The totality of the financial indebtedness of TGS and the obligations corresponding to gas purchases are denominated in U.S. dollars which have been translated into Argentine pesos at the exchange rate as of December 31, 2023 (US$ 1.00 = Ps. 808.45). The amounts to be paid in pesos could vary depending on the actual fluctuations in the exchange rate. For further information, see Note 17.a).</P>

<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:2.55pt'>The Company has not granted any additional guarantees or goods of restricted availability other than those set out in the remaining notes.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:1pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:1pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:9pt Verdana;margin-left:0pt'><B>23.</B></KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD><B>ASSOCIATES AND JOINT AGREEMENT</B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Associates with significant influence</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>Link:</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Link was created in February 2001, with the purpose of the operation of a natural gas transportation system, which links TGS&#8217;s natural gas transportation system with the Cruz del Sur S.A. pipeline. The connection pipeline extends from Buchanan (Buenos Aires province), located in the high-pressure ring that surrounds the city of Buenos Aires, which is part of TGS&#8217;s pipeline system, to Punta Lara. <B>tgs</B>&#8217;s ownership interest in such company is 49%, while Pan American Sur S.A. holds a 20.40%, Shell Argentina S.A. a 25.50% and Wintershall Dea Argentina S.A. the remaining 5.10%.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:14.2pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>TGU:</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>TGU is a company incorporated in Uruguay. This company rendered operation and maintenance services to Gasoducto Cruz del Sur S.A. and its contract terminated in 2010. TGS holds 49% of its common stock and Pampa Energ&#237;a holds the remaining 51%.</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>73</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=right style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'>English translation of the original prepared in Spanish for publication in Argentina</P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'><B>TRANSPORTADORA DE GAS DEL SUR S.A.</B></P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0;text-indent:-1.15pt;margin-right:-35.5pt'>AS OF DECEMBER 31, 2023 AND COMPARATIVE INFORMATION </P>
<P align=center style='font:8pt Verdana;margin:0'> (Stated in thousands of pesos as described in Note 3, unless otherwise stated)</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>EGS (liquidated):</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>In September 2003, EGS, a company registered in Argentina, was incorporated. The ownership was distributed between <B>tgs</B> (49%) and TGU (51%). </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>EGS owned a natural gas pipeline, which connects <B>tgs</B>&#8217; main pipeline system in the Province of Santa Cruz with a delivery point on the border with Chile. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>Since 2012 EGS discontinued its operations, deciding in January 2016 to begin the necessary steps to dissolve EGS. The Extraordinary Shareholders' Meeting of EGS held on March 10, 2016, appointed the liquidator.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>The EGS Liquidator Board held on March 31, 2023, reported the Final Distribution Project of the remainder of the liquidation. On June 26, 2023, the Extraordinary General Assembly of EGS approved the final liquidation balance for the irregular fiscal year closed on March 31, 2023 and approved the cancellation of the social registration of EGS before the General Inspection of Justice. As of the date of issuance of these consolidated financial statements, the distribution project was executed by the liquidator. As of the date of issuance of these Consolidated Financial Statements, the IGJ registered the liquidation and canceled it from the registry.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;color:#1F497D'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><B>Joint Agreement</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt;color:#0070C0'><B><I>UT:</I></B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>The Board of Directors of<B> tgs</B> approved the agreement to set up the UT together with SACDE. The objective of the UT is the assembly of pipes for the construction of the project of &quot;Expansion of the System of Transportation and Distribution of Natural Gas&quot; in the Province of Santa Fe, called by National Public Bid No. 452-0004-LPU17 by the MINEM (the &quot;Work&quot;).</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On October 27, 2017, tgs - SACDE UT signed the corresponding work contract with the MINEM.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:28.3pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>The UT will remain in force until its purpose has been fulfilled, i.e., once the works involved in the Project have been completed and until the end of the guarantee period, set at 18 months from the provisional reception.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>As a result of the situation of the economic context and the COVID mentioned in Note 1, the UT sent a letter to <I>Integraci&#243;n Energ&#233;tica Argentina S.A</I>. (&#8220;IEASA&#8221;), currently Energ&#237;a Argentina S.A. (&#8220;ENARSA&#8221;), a company currently part of the Ministry of Productive Development, requesting, among other issues, the reestablishment of the economic-financial equation, readjustment of the work schedule, approval of cost redeterminations and price adjustments under the current legal regime.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;margin-right:2.55pt'>On July 9, 2021, the UT and IEASA signed a restart order and a restart certificate for the works related to the Work, through which the work schedule was readjusted and IEASA also assumed the commitment to manage and join efforts to guarantee the cash flow in order to avoid new effects on the economic-financial structure of the contract for the Work, which would give rise to new requests -by the UT- for the recomposition of the economic-financial equation of the contract and the schedule of execution of the Work.</P>

<P align=justify style='font:11pt Arial;margin:0;margin-left:21.3pt'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:11pt Arial;margin-left:-21.3pt'><B>I.</B></KBD><B>Report on the audit of the consolidated financial statements </B>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:11pt Arial;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:11pt Arial;margin:0'><B>Opinion&#160;</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'>We have audited the</FONT><FONT style='font:10pt Arial;color:#FF0000'> </FONT><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'>consolidated financial statements of Transportadora de Gas del Sur S.A. and its subsidiaries (the &quot;Group&quot;)<I>, </I>which comprise the consolidated statement of financial position as of December 31, 2023, and the consolidated statements of comprehensive income, of changes in equity and of cash flows for the year ended on that date, and notes to the consolidated financial statements, which include significant information about accounting policies.&#160;</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'>In our opinion, the accompanying consolidated financial statements present fairly, in all material respects, the Group's consolidated financial position as of December 31, 2023, as well as its financial performance and cash flows for the year ended on that date, in accordance with the Accounting Standards IFRS (International Financial Reporting Standards).</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;color:#000000'><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'><B>Basis of opinion</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;color:#FF0000;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:10pt Arial;color:#000000'>We have conducted our audit in accordance with the International Audit Standards (ISAs) as issued by the International Audit and Assurance Standards Board (IAASB).&#160; Our responsibilities under these standards are described later in the section &quot;</FONT><FONT style=color:#000000> </FONT><FONT style='font:10pt Arial;color:#000000'><I>Auditors&#8217; responsibilities for the audit of the consolidated financial statements &quot; </I>of our report. We consider that the evidence we have obtained provide a sufficient and appropriate basis for our opinion. </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'><B>Independence</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Arial;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style=font-size:10pt>We are independent of the Group in accordance with the &quot;International Code of Ethics for Professional Accountants (including International Standards of Independence)&quot; of the International Ethics Standards Board for Accountants (IESBA Code) together with the ethical requirements that apply to our audit of the consolidated financial statements in Argentina, and we have fulfilled the other ethical responsibilities in accordance with those requirements and the IESBA Code.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><B>Key audit matters</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'>Key audit matters are those matters that, in our professional judgment, were of most significance in our audit of the consolidated financial statements of the current period. These matters were addressed in the context of our audit of the consolidated financial statements as a whole, and in forming our opinion thereon, and </FONT><FONT style='font:10pt Arial;color:#333333;background-color:#FFFFFF'>we do not provide a separate opinion on these matters</FONT><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'>. For the current period matter included below, </FONT><FONT style='font:10pt Arial;color:#333333;background-color:#FFFFFF'>our description of how our audit addressed the matter is provided in that context</FONT><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'>.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;color:#FF0000;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>1</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font-family:Tms Rmn'><IMG src=tgs_145.jpg width=139 height=112 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_146.jpg width=141 height=119 alt=first_page_logo title=first_page_logo></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><TABLE style=border-collapse:collapse;width:460.7pt><TR><TD valign=top bgcolor=#1F497D style=width:219.5pt;padding-top:2.85pt;padding-left:5.4pt;padding-right:5.4pt><P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style=color:#EEECE1>Key audit matters</FONT></P>
</TD><TD valign=top bgcolor=#1F497D style=width:241.2pt;padding-top:2.85pt;padding-left:5.4pt;padding-right:5.4pt><P style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;color:#EEECE1'>Audit response</P>
</TD></TR>
<TR><TD valign=top style='width:0.3pt;padding-top:2.85pt;padding-left:5.4pt;padding-right:5.4pt;border-top:0.5pt dotted #1F497D;border-bottom:0.5pt solid #1F497D'><P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:12pt'><FONT style='border-bottom:1px solid #000000'><B>Recoverability of property, plant and equipment related to the Natural Gas Transportation cash-generating unit</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;color:#000000'>As of December 31, 2023, the Group maintained thousands of pesos 571,160,601 accounted as property, plant and equipment (PPE) related to the Natural Gas Transportation cash-generating unit. As mentioned in Note 5.a) to the accompanying consolidated financial statements, an impairment test of PPE is performed when an existing event or one that will take place in the near future indicates that the recoverable value of PPE amounts may be affected. The recoverable amount of an asset is the highest between fair value less costs to sale of that asset and its value in use. The value in use of the Natural Gas Transportation cash-generating unit is calculated based on discounted future cash flows, considering, among others, significant assumptions related to future tariff adjustments based on negotiations with the regulator, the recognition of cost adjustments, the discount rate, and expected future macroeconomic variables, such as inflation and foreign exchange rates.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin-top:0pt;margin-bottom:12pt'>This matter is key because it involves the application of critical judgment and significant estimates by Management, which are subject to uncertainty and future events. In turn, it led to a high degree of judgment and effort on the part of the auditor in performing procedures to evaluate cash flow projections carried out by<FONT style=color:#202124;background-color:#F8F9FA> Management and test significant assumptions.</FONT></P>
</TD><TD valign=top style='width:0.3pt;padding-top:2.85pt;padding-left:5.4pt;padding-right:5.4pt;border-top:0.5pt dotted #1F497D;border-bottom:0.5pt solid #1F497D'><P style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;color:#000000'>Audit procedures carried out on this key matter included, among others:</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;color:#000000'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:10pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'><I>&#183;</I></KBD>evaluate estimation methodology; &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;color:#000000'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:10pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'><I>&#183;</I></KBD>test significant assumptions such as future tariff adjustments, discount rate, and expected future macroeconomic variables, such as inflation and foreign exchange rates, and test the completeness, accuracy, and relevance of the underlying data used. Significant assumptions were compared with available economic trend data and certain changes were made to regulatory assumptions and other factors to assess how they would affect our conclusions. The historical experience of Management's estimates was also assessed, and sensitivity analyses of significant assumptions were conducted to assess changes in the value in use that would result from changes in assumptions; &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;color:#000000'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:10pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'><I>&#183;</I></KBD>test the arithmetic accuracy of the discounted cash flow model;&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;margin-left:18pt;color:#000000'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:10pt Symbol;margin-left:-18pt'><I>&#183;</I></KBD>and assess the completeness of disclosures in the consolidated financial statements. &nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;margin-left:38.5pt;color:#000000'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt'><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'>Professionals with specialized skills and expertise were used in the evaluation of the methodology and significant assumptions used in future cash flows estimated by Management.</FONT></P>
<P style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Georgia;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
<P style='font:10pt Georgia;margin:0'>&nbsp;</P>
