<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>2</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font-family:Tms Rmn'><IMG src=tgs_145.jpg width=139 height=112 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_146.jpg width=141 height=119 alt=first_page_logo title=first_page_logo></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'><B>Information accompanying the consolidated financial statements (&quot;other information&quot;)</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'>Other information includes the information contained in: (i) the summary of events and (ii) consolidated annual report, presented to comply with the regulations of the National Securities Commission (&#8220;CNV&#8221;). This information is different from the consolidated financial statements and our auditor&#180;s report thereon. Management is responsible for this other information.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'>Our opinion on the consolidated financial statements does not cover the other information mentioned before and we do not express any form of assurance conclusion thereon.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'>In connection with our audit of the consolidated financial statements,<FONT style=color:#FF0000> </FONT>our responsibility is to read the other information and, in doing so, consider whether it is materially inconsistent with the financial statements or our knowledge obtained in the audit, <FONT style=background-color:#FFFFFF>or otherwise appears to be materially misstated</FONT>. If, based on the work we have performed, we conclude, as far as it is within our competence, that there is a material misstatement of this other information, we are required to report that fact. We have nothing to report in this regard. </P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'><B>Responsibilities of Management and the </B><B>Audit Committee</B><B> for the consolidated financial statements</B></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Arial;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style=font-size:10pt>Management is responsible for the preparation and fair presentation of the consolidated financial statements in accordance with Accounting Standards IFRS, and for such internal control as Management determines is necessary to enable the preparation of consolidated financial statements that are free of material misstatements, whether due to fraud or error.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'>In the preparation of the consolidated financial statements<FONT style=color:#4F81BD><I>,</I> </FONT>Management is responsible for assessing the Group's ability to continue as a going concern, disclosing, as appropriate, the matters related to the going concern and using the going concern basis of accounting, unless management either intends to liquidate the Group or to cease operations, or has no realistic alternative but to do so.</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;color:#FF0000;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:10pt Arial;color:#000000'>The Audit Committee is responsible for overseeing the Group's financial reporting process</FONT><FONT style=font-family:Arial;color:#000000><I>.</I></FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><B>Auditors&#8217; responsibilities for the audit of the consolidated financial statements</B></P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'>Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance whether the consolidated financial statements as a whole are free of material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue an auditor&#180;s report that includes our opinion. Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance but is not a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with ISAs will always detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are considered material if, individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisions of users taken on the basis of these consolidated financial statements. </FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'>As part of an audit in accordance with the ISAs, we exercise professional judgment and maintain professional skepticism throughout the audit. We also:</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:10pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the consolidated financial statements, whether due to fraud or error, design and perform audit procedures responsive to those risks and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of not detecting a material misstatement due to fraud is higher than for one resulting from error, as fraud may involve collusion, forgery, intentional omissions, misrepresentations, or the override of internal control.&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=center style='font:9pt Verdana;margin:0'>3</P>
<HR style='page-break-after:always;border:0;height:3pt;background-color:#909090;margin:8pt 0'><P style=line-height:0;margin:0></P>
<P style='font:11pt Arial;margin-top:10pt;margin-bottom:0pt'><FONT style='font-family:Tms Rmn'><IMG src=tgs_145.jpg width=139 height=112 alt='Picture 1' title='Picture 1'></FONT><FONT style='font-family:Times New Roman'><IMG src=tgs_146.jpg width=141 height=119 alt=first_page_logo title=first_page_logo></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<HR style='border:0;height:0;width:0;margin:14pt 0 0 0'><P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:10pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Obtain an understanding of the internal control relevant to the audit in order to design audit procedures that are appropriate in the circumstances, but not for the purpose of expressing an opinion on the effectiveness of the Group's internal control<FONT style=color:#4F81BD><I>.</I></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:10pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Evaluate the appropriateness of accounting policies used and the reasonableness of accounting estimates and related disclosures made by Management.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:10pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Conclude on the appropriateness of the use, by Management, of the going concern basis of accounting and, based on the audit evidence obtained, whether a material uncertainty exists related to events or conditions that may cast significant doubt on the Group's ability to continue as a going concern. If we conclude that there is such material uncertainty, we are required to draw attention in our auditor&#180;s report to the related disclosures in the consolidated financial statements or, if such disclosures are inadequate, to modify our opinion. Our conclusions are based on the audit evidence obtained up to the date of our auditor&#180;s report. However, future events or conditions may cause the Group to<FONT style=color:#4F81BD><I> </I></FONT>cease to continue as a going concern.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:12pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'>Evaluate the overall presentation, structure and content of the consolidated financial statements</FONT><FONT style='font:10pt Arial;color:#4F81BD'><I>,</I></FONT><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'> including the disclosures, and whether the consolidated financial statements</FONT><FONT style='font:10pt Arial;color:#4F81BD'> </FONT><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'>represent the underlying transactions and events in a manner that achieves fair presentation.&#160;</FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:10pt Symbol;margin-left:0pt'>&#183;</KBD><KBD style=margin-left:35.4pt></KBD>Obtain sufficient appropriate audit evidence in relation to financial information from entities or business activities within the Group to express an opinion on the consolidated financial statements. We are responsible for the direction, supervision, and performance of the Group's audit. We remain solely responsible for our audit opinion.&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin-top:5pt;margin-bottom:5pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'>We communicate with the Audit Committee</FONT><FONT style=font-family:Arial;color:#FF0000><I> </I></FONT><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'>related to, among other matters, the planned scope and timing of the audit, and significant audit findings, including any significant internal control deficiencies that we may identify during our audit.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:11pt Times New Roman;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'>We also provide the Audit Committee</FONT><FONT style='font:10pt Arial;color:#FF0000'> </FONT><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'>with a statement that we have complied with relevant ethics requirements related to independence and communicate to them all relationships and other matters that may reasonably be thought to bear on our independence; and, where applicable, communicate all actions taken to eliminate threats or safeguards applied.</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;background-color:#FFFFFF'>Of the matters communicated to the Audit Committee, we determined those matters that were of most significance in the audit of the consolidated financial statements of the current period and are, consequently, the key audit matters. We describe these matters in our auditor&#180;s report unless law or regulation precludes public disclosure about the matter or when, in extremely rare circumstances, we determine that a matter should not be communicated in our report because the adverse consequences of doing so would reasonable be expected to outweigh the public interest benefits of such communication.</P>

<P align=justify style='font:12pt Times New Roman;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:12pt Arial;margin-left:-21.3pt'><B>II.</B></KBD><FONT style='font:10pt Arial'><B>Report on other legal and regulatory requirements</B></FONT>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;color:#000000;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:11pt Arial;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000;background-color:#FFFFFF'><FONT style=font-size:10pt>In compliance with current regulation, we report that:</FONT></P>
<P align=justify style='font:12pt Arial;margin:0;color:#000000;background-color:#FFFFFF'>&nbsp;</P>
<P align=justify style='font:10pt Arial;margin:0;margin-left:14.2pt;color:#000000;background-color:#FFFFFF'><KBD style='position:absolute;font:10pt Arial;margin-left:-21.3pt'>(a)</KBD>The consolidated financial statements mentioned in paragraph 1. are transcribed into the &#8220;Inventory and Balance Sheet&#8221; book of Transportadora de Gas del Sur S.A. and, in our opinion, were prepared in all material respects, in accordance with the provisions of the Commercial Companies Law and pertinent resolutions of the CNV.&nbsp;</P>
